On EchoNet-Dynamic and MSK-Bone, it supports single- and few-shot transfer with accuracy competitive with strong dense-correspondence baselines, while training in minutes on a single consumer GPU. The learned embeddings are interpretable: linear projections reveal structured cohort variation, image-decoder interpolation produces anatomically plausible intermediate frames, and test-time latent inversion reconstructs held-out frames through the atlas. These results suggest that cohort-scale neural atlases offer a practical, interpretable representation for reducing expert annotation burden in ultrasound video analysis.

</details>


### [107] [MMDG-Bench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00891)
*Qianshan Zhan,Qian Wang,Da Li,Xiao-Jun Zeng,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMDG-Bench，一个统一的多模态域泛化基准测试，包含D2M（先域泛化后多模态学习）和M2D（先多模态学习后域泛化）两种框架，在多种任务上验证了结构化组合优于现有方法，并揭示了模态稳定性、骨干网络选择等关键设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态学习（MML）和域泛化（DG）各自领域已有大量进展，但两者的系统集成仍未被充分探索。当前MMDG研究主要局限于动作识别，且缺乏标准化的评估协议，亟需一个统一的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建MMDG-Bench基准，提出D2M（先DG后MML）和M2D（先MML后DG）两种框架；统一多种任务（视频-音频-光流动作识别、RGB-Depth-IR人脸防伪）的实验协议；通过将统一MML配置与五种DG技术配对，实例化十种MMDG基线。

Result: 结构化组合（D2M/M2D框架+DG技术）在多个任务上 consistently 优于现有SOTA方法。三个关键发现：(1) DG技术在不同骨干网络上提供一致的泛化增益，而非DG方法对骨干网络变化高度敏感；(2) D2M在模态关系跨域稳定时表现更优，M2D则对跨域关系变化更鲁棒；(3) 更强的骨干网络在结构化框架中带来更显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: MMDG-Bench为多模态鲁棒性研究提供了原则性基础和可操作的设计指南，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modal Domain Generalization (MMDG) seeks to leverage complementary modalities to enhance model robustness on unseen domains. Despite extensive progress in Multi-modal Learning (MML) and Domain Generalization (DG) as individual fields, their systematic integration remains under-explored. Current MMDG research is largely confined to action recognition and lacks standardized evaluation protocols. To address this, we introduce MMDG-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring two foundational frameworks: DG then MML (D2M) and MML then DG (M2D). We provide unified experimental protocols across diverse tasks, including video-audio-flow action recognition and RGB-Depth-IR face anti-spoofing. By instantiating ten MMDG baselines through pairing a unified MML configuration with five DG techniques under both D2M and M2D orderings, we demonstrate that these structured combinations frequently outperform existing state-of-the-art methods, underscoring the necessity of a unified benchmarking effort. Our analysis yields three key insights: (1) Integrating DG techniques provides consistent generalization gains across various backbones, whereas non-DG methods are highly sensitive to backbone shifts; (2) The optimal framework choice depends on inter-modal stability: D2M excels when modal relations are stable across domains, while M2D is more robust to cross-domain relational variance; (3) Stronger backbones yield amplified performance dividends when integrated into our structured frameworks. MMDG-Bench provides a principled foundation and actionable design guidelines for future research in multi-modal robustness. Code is released at https://github.com/qszhan/MMDG-Bench.

</details>


### [108] [hZACH-ViT: Curved Latent Geometry for Compact Vision Transformers in Low-Data Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00906)
*Athanasios Angelakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出hZACH-ViT系列模型，通过在ZACH-ViT基础上替换为双曲/球面潜空间几何和原型分类器头，在MedMNIST少样本场景下，非欧几何配置相比欧氏基线平均提升+0.021主指标，且低曲率（c=0.1或0.2）在六个数据集上取得最优，表明几何与曲率是依赖数据集的可选超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的紧凑型Vision Transformer变体大多假设欧氏潜空间几何足以组织图像表征，但在医学影像低数据和资源受限场景下，非欧氏几何是否更优尚未被充分探索。本文旨在通过控制变量实验，隔离研究潜空间几何形状对少样本医学影像分类性能的影响。

Method: 基于ZACH-ViT（无位置编码和无类别的紧凑ViT），仅修改最终表征空间和基于原型的分类头，引入庞加莱、克莱因和球面三种非欧几何，在七个MedMNIST数据集上以每类50样本的少样本协议进行770次训练（涵盖七种曲率幅度、三个非欧几何及欧氏基线），进行系统对比。

Result: 在所有七个数据集上，最佳非欧hZACH-ViT配置均优于欧氏ZACH-ViT基线，平均提升+0.021主指标，最大提升在OCTMNIST上达+0.055 MacroF1。低曲率（c=0.1或0.2）在六个数据集上取得最优，且固定低曲率配置在多数数据集上仍保持正向增益。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优流形，几何和曲率应作为依赖数据集（dataset-dependent）的模型选择变量；低曲率配置的稳健性表明即使不进行逐数据集详尽调参，非欧几何仍能带来稳定收益。

Abstract: Compact Vision Transformers are attractive for medical imaging in low-data and resource-constrained settings, but most existing variants assume that Euclidean latent geometry is sufficient for organizing image representations. We introduce hZACH-ViT, a family of curved-geometry extensions of ZACH-ViT, a compact zero-token Vision Transformer that removes positional embeddings and the class token and relies on global average pooling over patch representations. To isolate the role of geometry, we preserve the verified ZACH-ViT backbone and modify only the final representation space and prototype-based classifier head, enabling a controlled comparison between Euclidean, hyperbolic, and spherical latent geometries.
  We evaluate Poincaré, Klein, and spherical hZACH-ViT heads on seven MedMNIST datasets under an identical few-shot protocol with 50 samples per class and five random seeds. The completed benchmark contains 770 training runs spanning seven datasets, three non-Euclidean geometries, seven curvature magnitudes, and a Euclidean baseline. Across all seven datasets, the best non-Euclidean hZACH-ViT configuration improves over Euclidean ZACH-ViT, with an average gain of +0.021 in the dataset-specific primary metric and the largest improvement on OCTMNIST (+0.055 MacroF1). Fixed low-curvature configurations retain positive gains on the majority of datasets, and low curvature values (c = 0.1 or 0.2) account for six of the seven dataset-level winners.
  Rather than identifying a universally optimal manifold, our results establish geometry and curvature as dataset-dependent model-selection variables, with fixed low-curvature analyses confirming that gains persist beyond exhaustive per-dataset tuning.

</details>


### [109] [Reason, Retrieve, Re-rank: A Zero-Shot Reasoning-Aware Framework for Composed Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00910)
*Ali Alavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R3-CoVR是一种完全无需训练的零样本组合视频检索流水线，由冻结的基础模型构建，在CVPR 2026 VidLLMs挑战赛的Reason-Aware CoVR任务上取得了91.9% R@1和98.2% R@10的领先性能，其中约束感知重排序阶段贡献了最大的性能提升（从72.7%提升至91.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组合视频检索方法在处理自由形式文本修改时缺乏对编辑后效果的深层理解（如状态转换、动作阶段、场景、镜头和节奏等），且需要大量训练数据。论文旨在零样本场景下实现Reason-Aware的CoVR，无需任何训练即可准确检索应用文本修改后的目标视频。

Method: 提出R3-CoVR流水线，包含三个阶段：(1) 推理阶段：使用Qwen3-VL-8B多模态大语言模型对参考视频进行推理，生成编辑后果的简洁后编辑描述；(2) 检索阶段：使用SigLIP-2对比性视频-文本编码器对生成描述与候选视频进行嵌入匹配，完成首轮检索；(3) 重排序阶段：使用同一多模态模型作为评估器，基于约束感知对候选结果进行评分并重排序。整个流程无需训练，完全由冻结基础模型构建。

Result: 在挑战赛测试集上，R3-CoVR达到91.9% R@1和98.2% R@10。关键发现：(i) 将描述长度匹配到对比编码器的文本窗口使R@1从67.5提升至72.7；(ii) 约束感知重排序器仅对候选列表进行重排序，使R@1从72.7大幅提升至91.9，为最大单一增益。

Conclusion: R3-CoVR证明纯零样本、无需训练的流水线在组合视频检索任务上可达到极具竞争力的性能。描述长度与编码器窗口的对齐以及约束感知重排序是性能提升的关键因素。论文还分析了重排序器行为、检索/重排序混合策略及候选列表深度，并发布了清晰的3层实现代码。

Abstract: Composed Video Retrieval (CoVR) seeks the target video that results from applying a free-form textual modification to a reference video. We address the \emph{Reason-Aware} CoVR (CoVR-R) challenge at the CVPR~2026 VidLLMs workshop, where retrieval is strictly zero-shot. We present \textbf{R3-CoVR} (\emph{Reason, Retrieve, Re-rank}), a training-free pipeline built entirely from frozen foundation models. A multimodal large language model (Qwen3-VL-8B) reasons about the \emph{after-effects} an edit implies -- state transitions, action phases, scene, camera and tempo -- and verbalises a concise post-edit description; a contrastive video--text encoder (SigLIP-2) embeds this description and the gallery for first-stage retrieval; finally a constraint-aware re-ranking stage uses the same multimodal model as a judge that scores each shortlisted candidate against the intended edited result. On the challenge test set, R3-CoVR attains \textbf{91.9\% R@1} and \textbf{98.2\% R@10}. Two findings drive these results: (i)~matching the description length to the contrastive encoder's text window lifts \Rk{1} from $67.5$ to $72.7$; and (ii)~the constraint-aware re-ranker, which reorders only the shortlist, lifts \Rk{1} from $72.7$ to $91.9$ -- the single largest gain. We analyse the re-ranker's behaviour, the retrieve/re-rank blend, and the shortlist depth, and we release a clean three-layer implementation.

</details>


### [110] [Bridging Topology and Deep Representation Learning: A TDA-ViT Fusion Model for Four-Class Brain Tumor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00927)
*Faisal Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合拓扑数据分析（TDA）特征与预训练Vision Transformer（ViT）表示的脑肿瘤四分类框架，在BRISC2025数据集上达到99.10%的准确率，优于多种现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤MRI分类中，Vision Transformer虽能学习全局上下文表示，但难以捕捉肿瘤区域的固有结构模式和拓扑模式，因此需要引入拓扑特征来提供互补信息。

Method: 提出TDA-ViT融合框架：TDA提取MRI图像的拓扑描述符（几何结构、连通性、形状信息），预训练ViT学习高层语义表示，将两种特征空间融合形成统一判别性表示后进行分类。

Result: 在BRISC2025四分类数据集（胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、垂体瘤、非肿瘤）上，准确率99.10%，精确率99.27%，召回率99.15%，F1分数99.21%，AUC 99.98%，优于ResNet50、ResNet101、EfficientNetB2及单独ViT。

Conclusion: 拓扑特征为深度表示学习提供了有价值的互补信息，TDA与ViT融合可构建鲁棒且高精度的自动化脑肿瘤分类框架。

Abstract: Accurate brain tumor classification from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is a key requirement for early diagnosis and clinical decision-making. Vision Transformers (ViTs) have shown strong performance in medical image analysis by learning global contextual representations, but they often fail to capture intrinsic structural and topological patterns present in tumor regions. To address this limitation, we propose a fusion framework that combines Topological Data Analysis (TDA) features with pretrained Vision Transformer representations for four-class brain tumor classification.
  In the proposed method, TDA is used to extract complementary topological descriptors that capture geometric structure, connectivity, and shape information from MRI images. In parallel, a pretrained ViT model learns high-level semantic representations from the same images. These two feature spaces are then fused to form a unified and more discriminative representation for classification.
  The model is evaluated on the BRISC2025 dataset, which contains four brain tumor classes: glioma, meningioma, pituitary tumor, and non-tumor cases. Experimental results show that combining topological and transformer-based features significantly improves performance compared to using either approach alone. The proposed TDA-ViT fusion model achieves an accuracy of 99.10%, precision of 99.27%, recall of 99.15%, F1-score of 99.21%, and an AUC of 99.98%. It also outperforms several state-of-the-art models, including ResNet50, ResNet101, EfficientNetB2, and standalone Vision Transformers. These results demonstrate that topological features provide valuable complementary information that enhances deep representation learning, leading to a robust and highly accurate framework for automated brain tumor classification.

</details>


### [111] [Single-Channel Tissue Segmentation via Cross-Modal Distillation from Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00928)
*Sakib Mohammad,Jarin Ritu,Md Sakhawat Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种跨模态知识蒸馏框架，将多通道荧光显微镜（DAPI+E-cadherin）的语义知识从冻结的Foundation Model教师（SAM ViT-H/CellSAM）蒸馏到仅使用核通道（DAPI）的轻量级U-Net学生模型中，在TissueNet上实现Dice 78.36，较无蒸馏基线提升13.05点，以23倍参数量缩减恢复教师87.9%性能，且跨数据集泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道荧光显微图像（如DAPI和E-cadherin）虽能提供更丰富的空间上下文信息以改善组织分割，但实际部署时往往仅部分通道可用，限制了多通道模型的直接应用。因此需要一种方法，在推理时仅使用单一核通道，仍能接近多通道模型的性能。

Method: 提出跨模态知识蒸馏框架，教师为冻结的Foundation Model（SAM ViT-H或CellSAM），处理多通道输入；学生为轻量级U-Net（Swin-Tiny/ResNet18/EfficientNet-B0/MobileNetV3），仅使用DAPI核通道。蒸馏目标函数结合：MSE概率匹配损失、边界感知监督和可学习不确定性权重。

Result: 在TissueNet上，SAM蒸馏的Swin-Tiny学生达到Dice 78.36±1.44，较无蒸馏基线（65.31±1.35）提升13.05点，恢复教师oracle性能（89.12±1.21）的87.9%，参数量减少23倍。KD对所有四种学生架构均稳定提升约12个Dice点。SAM ViT-H作为教师优于CellSAM。BBBC038跨数据集评估显示无需教师重训即可获得一致增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的跨模态知识蒸馏框架能有效将多通道先验知识迁移至单通道轻量级模型，实现架构无关的性能提升和良好的跨数据集泛化能力，为实际部署中仅部分通道可用场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multiplexed fluorescence microscopy improves tissue segmentation by providing complementary channels including nuclear (DAPI) and membrane (E-cadherin), that together encode richer spatial context than single-channel imaging alone. However, multiplexed models require all channels at inference, limiting deployment where only a subset is available. This work proposes a cross-modal knowledge distillation framework that transfers semantic information from a frozen foundation model teacher processing multiplexed input to a lightweight student operating on the nuclear channel only. The distillation objective combines MSE-based probability matching, boundary-aware supervision, and learnable uncertainty weighting. SAM ViT-H and CellSAM are evaluated as teachers across four U-Net students: Swin-Tiny (27M), ResNet18 (11M), EfficientNet-B0 (5.3M), and MobileNetV3 (1.5M), on TissueNet and BBBC038. On TissueNet, the SAM-distilled Swin-Tiny student achieves Dice 78.36 (plus or minus 1.44), a 13.05-point improvement over the no-KD baseline (65.31 plus or minus 1.35) and 87.9% recovery of teacher oracle performance (89.12 plus or minus 1.21) at a 23x parameter reduction. KD consistently improves all four students by approximately 12 Dice points, confirming architecture-agnostic distillation. SAM ViT-H outperforms CellSAM as teacher across all settings. Cross-dataset evaluation on BBBC038 shows consistent gains without teacher retraining.

</details>


### [112] [CV-Arena: An Open Benchmark for Instructional Computer Vision Problem Solving with Human-AI Collaborative Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00931)
*Fangzhou Lin,Peiran Li,Lingyu Xu,Wenjing Chen,Qianwen Ge,Shuo Xing,Mingyang Wu,Xiangbo Gao,Siyuan Yang,Kazunori Yamada,Ziming Zhang,Haichong Zhang,Zhen Dong,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CV-Arena，一个包含1.2万对高分辨率真实图像指令的大规模基准，用于评估专业级指令引导图像编辑能力；同时提出了Active Elo评估协议和CV-Agent轻量级代理模型，实验发现现有模型在指令遵循、物理推理、结构控制等方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准过于聚焦于窄范围的表观编辑，无法充分覆盖专业工作流中真实图像任务的多样性，缺乏对指令性计算机视觉问题求解能力的系统性评估。

Method: (1) 构建CV-Arena基准：通过CogRetriever双轨检索-筛选管道，结合目标网络搜索、智能体查询优化、验证和可追溯性，收集1.2万对覆盖16种指令性视觉任务类型的高分辨率真实图像-指令对。(2) 提出Active Elo评估协议：结合CV-Judge（逻辑门控多维VLM评估器）拒绝明显失败案例并解决高置信度比较，将高质量难分案例路由至专家评分员，通过可靠性加权Elo更新聚合人机标注。(3) 开发CV-Agent：集规划、编辑和验证于一体的轻量级代理模型。

Result: 对21个系统（含闭源、开源及代理模型）的综合评估揭示了现有模型在指令遵循、物理推理、结构控制和细粒度细节保留方面存在持续差距；CV-Agent展示了闭环推理在专业级指令遵循视觉编辑中的潜力。

Conclusion: 指令引导的图像编辑需要向更广泛的"指令性计算机视觉问题求解"扩展；CV-Arena可作为专业级评估平台；闭环推理是提升该能力的可行方向。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing is becoming a general interface for visual work, yet existing benchmarks still focus largely on narrow appearance edits and do not fully capture the diversity of real-image tasks in professional workflows. Here, we define instructional computer vision problem solving as a broader formulation of image editing: given a real input image and a natural-language instruction, a system must produce an edited output that realizes the requested transformation while satisfying explicit preservation, geometric, physical, and usability constraints. We introduce CV-Arena, an open benchmark designed to evaluate this capability at professional scales. CV-Arena contains 12K high-resolution real-image instruction pairs spanning 16 instruction-based visual task types, constructed using CogRetriever, a dual-track retrieval-and-curation pipeline that combines targeted web search, agentic query refinement, verification, and traceability. To evaluate models at scale while preserving human fidelity, we propose Active Elo, a human-AI collaborative preference protocol that leverages CV-Judge, a logic-gated, multi-dimensional VLM evaluator, to reject clear failures and resolve high-confidence comparisons; and to route close, high-quality comparisons to expert raters. Mixed human and AI supervision is then aggregated through reliability-weighted Elo updates. Our comprehensive evaluation of 21 systems, including proprietary, open-source, and agentic models, on CV-Arena reveals persistent gaps in instruction adherence, physical reasoning, structural control, and fine-grained detail preservation. We further develop CV-Agent, a lightweight agentic model that combines planning, editing, and verification, and demonstrate that closed-loop reasoning is a promising direction for professional-grade instruction-following visual editing.

</details>


### [113] [One Channel to Rule Them All: Rethinking Input Representation for Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00936)
*Timur Ismagilov,Shakaiba Majeed,Michael Milford,Tan Viet Tuyen Nguyen,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn,Shoaib Ehsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了视觉位置识别（VPR）中RGB彩色输入为必要的假设，通过多种训练范式、模型架构和基准测试发现：灰度图像在全局VPR任务中与RGB性能相当，在严重外观变化下甚至更优，且具有参数效率高、存储带宽需求低等实际优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VPR系统几乎全部依赖RGB输入，隐含假设彩色信息对全局位置识别是必要的。作者质疑这一假设，希望系统性地探究色度信息在真实外观变化条件下的实际贡献。

Method: 在多种训练范式、模型架构和标准基准测试下，系统性对比灰度与RGB输入在VPR任务中的表现，涵盖光照、天气、季节和场景设置等真实外观变化。

Result: 灰度训练的MixVPR模型平均Recall@1为82.4%，超过RGB版本的81.2%；轻量级灰度变体（参数减少60%）在某些情况下可超越更重的RGB模型；仅在具有持久且可区分的色度线索时，彩色才带来有意义增益。

Conclusion: 对于全局VPR，在光照、天气、季节和场景变化下，彩色贡献极小，灰度图像足以完成可靠的位置识别任务。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is fundamental to long-term robot localization and SLAM, yet current systems overwhelmingly rely on RGB input, implicitly assuming color is necessary for global place recognition. We challenge this assumption, investigating the role of chromatic information across training regimes, model architectures and standard benchmarks under real-world appearance variation. We find that grayscale matches RGB performance generally and outperforms it under severe appearance shifts where color invariance is insufficiently learned, while color provides meaningful gains only where persistent and discriminative chromatic cues are present. Across selected benchmarks, a fully gray-trained MixVPR model achieves an average 82.4% Recall@1 compared to 81.2% for its RGB counterpart. In some cases, lightweight grayscale variants with 60% fewer parameters can outperform heavier RGB models. Grayscale further offers practical advantages in storage, bandwidth and alignment with resource-constrained systems. We conclude that for global VPR where scenes vary across illumination, weather, season and setting, color contributes minimally, and grayscale alone is sufficient for reliable place recognition.

</details>


### [114] [COLLAR: Cascaded Object-Level Latent Refinement for High-Fidelity Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00954)
*Xinlong Zhang,Jia Wei,Xiaoyu Zhang,Teng Zhou,Chengyu Lin,Yongchuan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COLLAR是一种无需训练的框架，通过级联对象级潜在精炼和视野扩展，在Diffusion Transformers中实现高保真对象级控制，在COCO-MIG和COCO-POS基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Diffusion Transformers在对象级条件生成中面临视觉伪影和局部区域控制不精确的问题，现有方法难以在保持图像质量的同时实现精细的对象级控制。

Method: 提出COLLAR框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 跨尺度语义对齐(CSSA)模块，通过注意力机制将对象级特征注入扩展视野分支；2) 循环特征注入(CFI)模块，采用基于频率的自适应策略和背景反馈机制选择性更新全局主干网络。

Result: 在COCO-MIG和COCO-POS基准上，COLLAR在语义对齐、图像质量和空间保真度方面均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过扩展视野分支作为特征优化枢纽，无需额外训练即可将对象级特征有效整合到全局生成过程中，同时保持最终图像质量。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity object-level control in Diffusion Transformers remains a significant challenge despite the introduction of structural priors like depth and Canny maps. Current object-level conditional generation methods frequently suffer from visual artifacts and struggle to maintain precise control over objects within small localized regions. To address these limitations, we propose Cascaded Object-Level Latent Refinement (COLLAR), a training-free framework that progressively optimizes object-level features via the Field-of-View (FoV) expansion. First, we propose the Cross-Scale Semantic Alignment (CSSA) module to address spatial-semantic gaps by injecting object-level features into extended-FoV branches via attention mechanisms. To further optimize these features, the Cyclic Feature Injection (CFI) module introduces a reciprocal background feedback mechanism. It leverages a frequency-based adaptive strategy to selectively update the global backbone with context-aligned local information. Finally, the extended-FoV branch serves as a hub for feature optimization, ensuring that object-level features are integrated into the global generation process without compromising final image quality. Extensive experiments on the COCO-MIG and COCO-POS benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across semantic alignment, image quality, and spatial fidelity.

</details>


### [115] [Boundary-Protection W8A8 HiFloat8 Quantization for Large-Scale Text-to-Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00957)
*Yiming Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对14B参数量的文生视频扩散变压器Wan2.1-T2V-14B，提出了一种面向Ascend 910B NPU上W8A8 HiFloat8 (HiF8)格式的后训练量化方法。通过分析40个注意力块中边界块与中间块的激活分布差异，采用边界保护策略（保留前2后3块为BF16，其余35块量化为W8A8 HiF8），在5个VBench维度上匹配甚至略超BF16基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer模型在量化时面临激活分布异构的挑战：边界块与中间块的统计特性存在根本差异，导致均匀量化策略效果不佳。

Method: 首先对所有40个注意力块进行逐块激活分析，基于分析结果设计边界保护策略：保留前2个和后3个块为BF16精度，将中间35个块量化为W8A8 HiF8格式。此外还探索了量化感知训练（QAT）作为补充微调阶段。

Result: 在5个VBench维度上，提出的PTQ方法匹配或略超BF16基线性能；消融实验表明完整的边界保护配置可获得最高平均VBench得分；此外分析了QAT在单卡硬件上未能超越纯PTQ的条件。

Conclusion: 基于数据驱动的边界保护策略实现了大规模视频扩散模型的无精度损失量化，为低比特推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present a post-training quantization (PTQ) approach for Wan2.1-T2V-14B, a 14-billion-parameter text-to-video diffusion transformer, targeting the W8A8 HiFloat8 (HiF8) format on Ascend 910B NPUs. A central challenge in quantizing video DiT models is the heterogeneous activation distribution across transformer blocks: boundary blocks (the first and last few blocks) exhibit fundamentally different statistical properties from middle blocks, making uniform quantization ineffective. We conduct a systematic per-block activation analysis across all 40 WanAttentionBlocks and use the findings to motivate a boundary-protection strategy that retains the first two and last three blocks in BF16 while quantizing the remaining 35 blocks with W8A8 HiF8. The proposed PTQ method matches or marginally exceeds the BF16 baseline on all five VBench dimensions evaluated, indicating no measurable accuracy loss within the 5-prompt evaluation set. An ablation study over four protection configurations confirms that full boundary protection yields the highest average VBench score, validating the data-driven block selection. We additionally investigate quantization-aware training (QAT) as a complementary fine-tuning stage and analyze the conditions under which it fails to outperform plain PTQ on single-card hardware.

</details>


### [116] [Reasmory: 3D Reconstruction as Explicit Memory for VLMs Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00963)
*Jixuan He,Xueting Li,Chieh Hubert Lin,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Reasmory框架，通过结构化程序执行+显式3D空间记忆（点云+语义实例）+领域特定语言（DSL）约束VLMs的查询与变换操作，在空间推理任务上比GPT-5-mini和Gemini-3-flash提升6-18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在需要精确空间理解的任务（视点推理、方向比较、距离估计）上不可靠；多视图图像和单目视频中的空间线索稀疏且分散；直接让VLMs调用重建模型作为自由形式工具易出错（调用不当、跳过变换、误用中间结果）。

Method: 1) 构建显式3D记忆（点云）；2) 用语义化的3D目标实例增强记忆；3) 设计轻量级领域特定语言（DSL），约束VLMs如何查询目标与相机、变换视点、渲染观测；4) 生成的程序经解析和验证后执行，实现对空间记忆的更可靠交互。

Result: 在多视图图像和视频空间推理基准上，相比GPT-5-mini和Gemini-3-flash等强基线，一致提升6-18%。

Conclusion: 显式3D记忆在通过受约束、经过验证的操作访问时最有价值，而非自由形式的工具调用。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit emerging spatial reasoning capabilities, yet they remain unreliable on tasks requiring precise spatial understanding, such as viewpoint reasoning, directional comparison, and distance estimation. In multi-view images and monocular videos, relevant spatial cues are often sparse and distributed across redundant observations, making them difficult to organize and exploit. Reconstruction-based Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) offer a natural way to aggregate such observations into explicit spatial memory, such as point clouds. However, simply exposing reconstruction models as free-form tools is brittle, VLMs may invoke tools incorrectly, skip required spatial transformations, or misuse intermediate results. We propose \textbf{Reasmory}, a framework that formulates spatial reasoning as structured program execution over reconstructed spatial memory. Reasmory constructs explicit 3D memory, augments it with semantically grounded 3D object instances, and introduces a lightweight Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that constrains how VLMs query objects and cameras, transform viewpoints, and render observations during reasoning. Generated programs are parsed and validated before execution, enabling more reliable interaction with spatial memory than unconstrained tool use. Experiments on multi-view image and video spatial reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains of 6--18\% over strong baselines, including GPT-5-mini and Gemini-3-flash, indicating that explicit 3D memory is most useful when accessed through constrained, validated operations rather than free-form tool calls.

</details>


### [117] [Flexible Control of 3D CT Generation via Text and Semantically-Defined Segmentation Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00967)
*Weicheng Dai,Chenyu Wang,Andy Li,Shantanu Ghosh,Kayhan Batmanghelich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种灵活的多模态框架，支持通过放射学报告和/或部分分割掩膜（无需完整器官标注）控制三维医学图像生成，采用改进的扩散Transformer架构联合处理图像与分割token，并通过门控注意力机制有效处理长文本报告，在CT体积生成上实现了SOTA性能（mFID相对提升24%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维医学图像生成方法中，文本提示缺乏空间控制能力，而分割方法虽空间精度高但需完整器官标注，灵活性差。亟需一种既能提供精细空间控制又无需完整标注的灵活可控生成框架。

Method: 提出多模态可控生成框架，支持放射学报告和可选的局部分割掩膜作为条件输入；分割掩膜的语义通过伴随文本描述指定；采用基于改进扩散Transformer（DiT）的显存高效架构，联合处理图像和分割token；引入门控注意力机制有效编码长放射学报告。

Result: 在感知和语义指标上达到SOTA（mFID相对提升24%），生成高分辨率解剖一致CT体积；数据增强任务中提升数据效率；放射科医师评估确认生成图像与真实图像高度一致。

Conclusion: 所提方法在灵活性与空间可控性之间取得了更好平衡，无需完整器官标注即可实现精细的局部结构控制，为医学图像生成提供了更实用的条件控制方案。

Abstract: Generative models for volumetric medical images have found many applications in medical imaging, ranging from data augmentation to serving as priors for inverse problems. For these applications, generating high-resolution 3D images with strong controllability is essential but remains highly challenging. Existing approaches typically control generation either through radiology reports used as text prompts or through full image segmentation. While text-based prompting is flexible, it provides limited spatial control over the location, shape, and boundary of abnormalities. In contrast, segmentation-based methods receive precise spatial guidance but are restrictive in requiring full-organ annotations. In this work, we propose a flexible multimodal framework for controllable volumetric image generation that supports input from radiology reports and segmentation prompts (both optional). Our approach allows users to provide segmentation of a specific anatomy or abnormality without requiring full-organ annotations. The semantic meaning of the segmentation mask is specified through an accompanying text description, resulting in a highly flexible and scalable conditioning mechanism. We develop a memory-efficient architecture based on a modified diffusion transformer that jointly processes image and segmentation tokens. The model further incorporates gated attention to effectively attend to long radiology reports. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art perceptual and semantic scores (e.g., 24% relative improvement in mean FID), generates high-resolution anatomically consistent CT volumes, and improves data efficiency when used for data augmentation. Radiologists' evaluation further confirms strong alignment between generated and real medical images.

</details>


### [118] [An Open-Source Benchmark and Baseline for Multi-temporal Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00987)
*Bingyu Li,Da Zhang,Tao Huo,Zhiyuan Zhao,Junyu Gao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多时序指代分割（MTRS）新任务，构建了自动化数据管道CRAFT-Agent和首个基准MTRefSeg-21K（21K个高质量多时序图像-文本-掩码三元组），并提出了变化感知LVLM框架MTRefSeg-R1，采用两阶段训练策略，在MTRS任务上取得了优于现有LVLM基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在视觉理解和语言引导定位方面表现出色，但其多时序视觉推理能力尚未被充分探索。为填补这一空白，作者提出了多时序指代分割（MTRS）任务。

Method: 1）提出CRAFT-Agent自动化数据构建管道（含人工审核），构建MTRefSeg-21K基准；2）提出MTRefSeg-R1框架，采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段从20K纯视觉双时序样本学习通用时序变化感知，第二阶段在MTRefSeg-21K上进行细粒度语言引导的时序定位微调；3）显式建模跨时序视觉差异，对齐语言指令与时序变化，预测所指变化掩码。

Result: 在MTRefSeg-21K基准上对多种VLM和LVLM模型进行评测，发现直接推理效果差，任务特定微调仍有局限。MTRefSeg-R1在实验中取得了强且常优于现有LVLM基线的性能。

Conclusion: MTRS是一个具有挑战性的新任务，结合了时序对应推理、语言定位和像素级掩码预测。MTRefSeg-R1展示了该任务的潜力，为未来多时序视觉推理研究提供了基准和方法基础。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown strong visual understanding and language-guided grounding abilities, yet their capacity for multi-temporal visual reasoning remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{Multi-temporal Referring Segmentation (MTRS)}, a new task that aims to segment language-described temporal changes from multi-temporal images. MTRS extends conventional referring segmentation and change detection by jointly requiring temporal correspondence reasoning, language grounding, and pixel-level mask prediction. We propose \textbf{CRAFT-Agent}, an automated data construction pipeline with human auditing, and build \textbf{MTRefSeg-21K}, the first MTRS benchmark, containing 21K high-quality multi-temporal image-text-mask triplets across diverse scenes, viewpoints, and domains. Benchmarking a broad set of VLM- and LVLM-based models reveals that direct inference performs poorly, while task-specific fine-tuning remains limited. To address this, we propose \textbf{MTRefSeg-R1}, a change-aware LVLM framework trained with a two-stage strategy. It first learns general temporal-change perception from 20K vision-only bi-temporal samples, and is then fine-tuned on MTRefSeg-21K for fine-grained language-guided temporal localization. MTRefSeg-R1 explicitly models cross-temporal visual differences, aligns language instructions with temporal variations, and predicts referred change masks. Extensive experiments show that MTRefSeg-R1 achieves strong and often superior performance compared with existing LVLM baselines, demonstrating the challenge and potential of MTRS.

</details>


### [119] [SWARD: Stochastic Window-Attention-Based Relational Distillation for Cross-Architectural Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00999)
*Aditya Makineni,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SWARD知识蒸馏框架，通过多尺度窗口注意力蒸馏（MWAD）和原型判别正则化（PDR）两种机制，弥合基于Transformer的大规模视觉基础模型与轻量卷积学生网络之间的表征鸿沟，在语义分割任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer视觉基础模型在密集预测任务上表现出色但部署困难，而知识蒸馏面临师生架构异构（Transformer vs CNN）问题，现有蒸馏方法假设架构同质且依赖直接特征模仿，未能弥合表征差距，也忽略了语义分割所需的结构化空间依赖和判别性组织。

Method: 提出SWARD框架，包含两个组件：1）MWAD模块，在随机偏移的窗口分区中对齐师生注意力关系，消除窗口边界偏差，多尺度设计捕获长短程空间依赖；2）PDR损失，通过强制类间分离和类内紧凑来塑造学生特征分布，增强判别结构。

Result: 在城市场景解析和医学图像分割等多个视觉应用上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SWARD通过注意力关系蒸馏和判别性正则化有效解决了异构师生架构间的知识迁移问题，证明了在资源受限场景下部署轻量高精度分割模型的可行性。

Abstract: Large-scale vision foundation models have driven substantial gains on dense prediction tasks such as semantic segmentation, but their size makes deployment impractical in resource-constrained settings, motivating knowledge distillation as a means of transferring their capabilities to lightweight student networks. However, modern foundation teachers are predominantly transformer-based that encode global context, whereas efficient students are typically convolutional networks with locally biased receptive fields. Existing distillation methods largely assume architectural homogeneity and rely on direct feature mimicry, which fails to bridge this representational gap and neglects the structured spatial dependencies and discriminative organization required for accurate semantic segmentation. In this paper, we propose SWARD, a knowledge distillation framework that addresses this gap through two complementary mechanisms. First, we introduce a Multi-Scale Windowed Attention Distillation (MWAD) module that aligns teacher-student attention-based relations within stochastically shifted window partitions whose offsets are randomly resampled at every training iteration. This removes window boundary bias, and, combined with the multi-scale design, captures both short- and long-range spatial dependencies. Second, we introduce Prototype Discriminative Regularization (PDR), a loss that helps shape the student's feature distribution by enforcing inter-class separation and intra-class compactness, further sharpening the discriminative structure beyond what feature mimicry alone can produce under the student's reduced capacity. Experiments across different vision applications (i.e., urban scene parsing and medical image segmentation) show that SWARD achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [120] [Automated Erythrocyte Detection and Tracking for Retinal Blood Flow Quantification in Erythrocyte-Mediated Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01006)
*Chiao-Yi Wang,Havish S Gadde,Yi-Ting Shen,Saige M. Oechsli,Osamah Saeedi,Yang Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EMTrack框架，通过流上下文模块和拓扑感知跟踪策略实现视网膜毛细血管级红细胞自动检测与跟踪，并在新建立的RBF-EMA数据集上验证了其在血流量化中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毛细血管级视网膜血流量(RBF)是多种眼病的潜在生物标志物，但现有测量手段有限；红细胞介导血管造影(EMA)虽能可视化单个红细胞，但缺乏自动化的红细胞检测与跟踪方法，这阻碍了血流的量化分析。

Method: 提出EMTrack框架，包含：(1)流上下文模块(flow-context module)，用于区分运动细胞与静止细胞的检测；(2)拓扑感知跟踪策略(topology-aware tracking strategy)，应对大帧间位移和运动变化下的跟踪挑战。同时构建了RBF-EMA数据集，提供全面的红细胞检测与跟踪标注。

Result: 在RBF-EMA数据集上，EMTrack在检测和跟踪任务中均优于基线方法（定量和定性评估），且血流量化结果展示了该方法在自动视网膜血流测量中的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: EMTrack框架有效填补了EMA成像中自动化红细胞跟踪的空白，为实现视网膜毛细血管血流的自动化、精准量化提供了可行方案，具有重要的临床应用前景。

Abstract: Capillary-level retinal blood flow (RBF) has strong potential as a biomarker for various ocular diseases. However, modalities for measuring capillary-level RBF remain limited. Erythrocyte-mediated angiography (EMA), an emerging imaging technique, enables capillary-level RBF measurement by visualizing individual erythrocytes, yet automated erythrocyte detection and tracking, which are essential for quantifying blood flow, remain largely unexplored. To address this gap, we propose EMTrack, a novel framework featuring a flow-context module for erythrocyte detection that distinguishes moving from paused cells and a topology-aware tracking strategy that enables tracking under large inter-frame displacements and substantial motion variations. In addition, we establish RBF-EMA, a new EMA dataset with comprehensive erythrocyte detection and tracking annotations. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms baseline methods both quantitatively and qualitatively on detection and tracking tasks in the RBF-EMA dataset. Moreover, RBF quantification results highlight the strong potential of our framework for automated retinal blood flow measurement.

</details>


### [121] [Cross-Axis Feature Fusion with Joint-Wise Motion Difference Prediction for Text-Based 3D Human Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01014)
*Gyojin Han,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于文本的三维人体运动编辑方法，通过双轴锚定Transformer架构和辅助任务，不仅学习编辑发生的时间位置，还能识别哪些关节需要修改，在MotionFix数据集上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的人体运动编辑方法主要集中在学习编辑发生的时间维度，但忽略了哪些关节需要被修改这一关键信息。作者希望创建一种模型，既能理解编辑的时间方面，也能理解负责变化的具体关节。

Method: 提出双轴锚定Transformer架构：沿着关节维度和时间维度分别提取不同特征，并通过跨轴融合模块整合表征；引入辅助任务，训练关节锚定Transformer回归源运动与目标运动关节旋转之间的Soft-DTW距离，使其学会理解哪些关节需要修改、哪些需要保留。

Result: 在MotionFix数据集上的全面实验表明，该方法在文本指令和源运动的语义对齐以及生成运动的整体保真度方面均有显著提升，达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模关节层面的编辑信息，并利用辅助任务进行训练，可以显著提升基于文本的运动编辑模型对语义指令的理解能力和运动生成质量。

Abstract: We address text-based 3D human motion editing, where the goal is to preserve the style and structure of a source motion while applying edits described in natural language. The release of the MotionFix dataset has spurred active research into training-based diffusion models that directly generate an edited motion from a source motion and a text instruction. While previous works have focused primarily on learning when an edit should occur temporally, our goal is to create a model that understands not only this temporal aspect but also which specific joints are responsible for the change. Targeting this, we propose a novel architecture and a complementary auxiliary task to aid its training. Our architecture consists of two axis-anchored transformers, which extract distinct features along the joint and time dimensions respectively, and a cross-axis fusion block that integrates these representations. We further introduce an auxiliary task that trains the joint-anchored transformer to regress the Soft-DTW distance between source and target joint rotations. This objective teaches the module to understand which joints to modify and which to preserve. Through comprehensive experiments on the MotionFix dataset, we demonstrate that our method significantly improves semantic alignment with both the text instruction and the source motion, as well as the overall fidelity of the generated motion, achieving state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Neural Deformation Representation for 4D Dynamic Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01021)
*Gyojin Han,Jiwan Hur,Jaehyun Choi,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的4D动态形状生成方法，通过解耦运动隐空间与形状隐空间的神经变形表示，结合条件神经符号距离场，在生成质量和效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D生成工作（如HyperDiffusion）因运动表示与形状表示未分离，导致时间一致性差、渲染速度慢；且4D动态形状生成的研究相对匮乏。

Method: 提出一种新的神经变形表示（通过预测蒙皮权重和多个部件的刚性变换），并将其与条件神经符号距离场结合，构建形状与运动隐空间解耦的4D表示架构；在此基础上设计扩散模型的训练过程，利用提取的形状和运动特征作为数据点。

Result: 在无条件生成、条件生成和运动重定向实验中，该方法在4D动态形状生成性能上优于先前工作，并展示了多种潜在应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦式神经变形表示能够更好地理解形状结构，提升4D动态形状的生成质量和效率，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Recent developments in 3D shape representation opened new possibilities for generating detailed 3D shapes. Despite these advances, there are few studies dealing with the generation of 4D dynamic shapes that have the form of 3D objects deforming over time. To bridge this gap, we focus on generating 4D dynamic shapes with an emphasis on both generation quality and efficiency in this paper. HyperDiffusion, a previous work on 4D generation, proposed a method of directly generating the weight parameters of 4D occupancy fields but suffered from low temporal consistency and slow rendering speed due to motion representation that is not separated from the shape representation of 4D occupancy fields. Therefore, we propose a new neural deformation representation and combine it with conditional neural signed distance fields to design a 4D representation architecture in which the motion latent space is disentangled from the shape latent space. The proposed deformation representation, which works by predicting skinning weights and rigid transformations for multiple parts, also has advantages over the deformation modules of existing 4D representations in understanding the structure of shapes. In addition, we design a training process of a diffusion model that utilizes the shape and motion features that are extracted by our 4D representation as data points. The results of unconditional generation, conditional generation, and motion retargeting experiments demonstrate that our method not only shows better performance than previous works in 4D dynamic shape generation but also has various potential applications.

</details>


### [123] [ProductWebGen: Benchmarking Multimodal Product Webpage Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01022)
*Zhihong Liu,Siqi Kou,Zheng Li,Ye Ma,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Kai Yu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProductWebGen基准，系统评估多模态生成模型在根据源产品图像和指令生成产品展示网页方面的能力，包含500个测试样本和两类工作流（基于编辑和基于统一模型），实验表明编辑方法在网页指令跟随和内容吸引力上领先，统一模型在视觉内容指令执行上更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产品展示网页的自动生成在营销、广告和电子商务等领域具有重要实用价值，但现有研究缺乏对该任务的系统性基准测试。该任务要求严格的视觉一致性和高保真指令跟随，这与先进多模态生成模型（如图像编辑模型和统一模型）的核心能力高度契合，因此需要构建基准来评估这些模型的表现。

Method: 构建ProductWebGen基准，包含500个测试样本（13个产品类别），每个样本由源图像、视觉内容指令和网页指令组成。设计两种评估工作流：(1) 基于编辑的工作流：使用LLM生成HTML代码，图像编辑模型生成图片；(2) 基于统一模型的工作流：单一模型同时生成HTML和图像。此外构建了ProductWebGen-1k微调数据集（1000组真实产品图像+LLM生成的HTML代码），并在开源模型BAGEL上进行验证。

Result: 基于编辑的方法在网页指令跟随和内容吸引力方面取得领先结果；基于统一模型的方法在视觉内容指令执行上表现更优。在开源模型BAGEL上的微调实验验证了ProductWebGen-1k数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: ProductWebGen为产品网页生成任务提供了系统化的评估基准，揭示了编辑方法和统一模型方法各自的优势领域，为未来研究指明了方向。数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Crafting a product display webpage from a source product image, along with layout and visual content instructions, holds significant practical value for domains such as marketing, advertising, and E-commerce. Intuitively, this task demands strict visual consistency across product displays and high-fidelity instruction following to jointly generate renderable HTML code. These requirements on controllability and instruction-following are closely aligned with the core features of advanced multimodal generative models, such as image editing models and unified models. To this end, this paper introduces ProductWebGen to systematically benchmark the product webpage generation capacities of these models. We organize ProductWebGen with 500 test samples covering 13 product categories; each sample consists of a source image, a visual content instruction, and a webpage instruction. The task is to generate a product showcase webpage including multiple consistent images in accordance with the source image and instructions. Given the mixed-modality input-output nature of the task, we design and systematically compare two workflows for evaluation -- one uses large language models and image editing models to separately generate HTML code and images (editing-based), while the other relies on a single UM to generate both, with image generation conditioned on the preceding multimodal context (UM-based). Empirical results show that editing-based approaches achieve leading results in webpage instruction following and content appeal, while UM-based ones may display more advantages in fulfilling visual content instructions. We also construct a supervised fine-tuning dataset, ProductWebGen-1k, with 1,000 groups of real product images and LLM-generated HTML code. We verify its effectiveness on the open-source UM BAGEL. The data and code are available at https://github.com/SJTU-DENG-Lab/ProductWebGen.

</details>


### [124] [Data Collection for Training Quality-Control AI in Carpet Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01023)
*Akbar Erkinov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于地毯生产的在线机器视觉检测系统设计方案，集成了线扫相机成像、缺陷分类体系、从无监督到监督的分阶段建模策略，并将数据收集作为首要工程目标，通过六西格玛DMAIC框架量化质量改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地毯生产中的目视检测速度慢、主观性强且不一致，难以满足现代织机的高速宽幅生产需求；同时，新增织机将造成生产瓶颈，基线缺陷率高企且质量失败带来显著财务风险，亟需自动化在线检测方案。

Method: 基于六西格玛DMAIC项目背景，设计成像子系统（同步线扫相机+明场/掠射组合照明），推导多米幅宽下的分辨率与吞吐量需求，定义地毯专用缺陷分类体系；提出分阶段建模策略：先以无监督异常检测（如MVTec AD范式）在无缺陷材料上训练，再通过人在回路标注飞轮演进至有监督检测与分割模型。

Result: 给出了端到端可部署的蓝图，将检测性能与DMAIC目标关联，展示了逃逸缺陷减少如何转化为过程质量和过程西格玛水平的提升。

Conclusion: 本研究的主要贡献在于提供了一个将数据收集视为一级工程目标的完整可部署方案，而非事后补救；该方案能够随着安装生命周期不断训练更强大的质量控制模型。

Abstract: Visual inspection remains the dominant quality-control practice in woven and tufted carpet production, yet it is slow, subjective, and inconsistent at the line speeds and widths of modern looms. We present a design proposal for an in-line machine-vision system whose primary purpose is twofold: to inspect the carpet web in real time and, equally importantly, to systematically collect and label images of defect patterns so that increasingly capable quality-control models can be trained over the life of the installation.The proposal is grounded in a concrete industrial setting: a Six Sigma (DMAIC) project at a woven-carpet production facility that anticipated a production bottleneck following the installation of additional weaving machines, with a substantial baseline defect rate and significant financial exposure associated with quality failures. We describe an imaging subsystem based on synchronized line-scan cameras with combined bright-field and grazing illumination, derive the resolution and throughput requirements needed to resolve fine structural defects across a multi-metre web, and define a carpet-specific defect taxonomy.We then lay out a staged modelling strategy that begins with unsupervised anomaly detection trained on defect-free material, following the paradigm exemplified by the carpet category of the MVTec Anomaly Detection benchmark, and matures through a human-in-the-loop annotation flywheel into supervised detection and segmentation models. Finally, we connect detection performance to the DMAIC objectives, showing how reductions in escaped defects translate into improved process quality and process sigma levels. The contribution is an end-to-end, deployable blueprint that treats data collection as a first-class engineering objective rather than an afterthought.

</details>


### [125] [Ask4VG: Risk-Aware Question Selection for Reducing Prior-Driven Answers in Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01044)
*Xiaorong Zhu,Qiang Li,Zibo Xu,Weijie Wang,Weizhi Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ask4VG框架，通过反事实视觉探针估计问题引发的幻觉风险，并对候选问题重写进行重排序，选择对视觉证据更敏感的问题，从而减少医学VQA中的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VQA中许多问题是模板化或高度相似的，这促使模型学习问答捷径而非基于图像的推理，增加了幻觉风险。现有方法多在答案层面缓解幻觉，但忽略了问题本身可能引发幻觉。

Method: Ask4VG框架包含两个阶段：(1) 反事实视觉探针——将同一问题在原始图像、扰动图像、空白图像和错配图像下提问，利用答案关系作为弱监督训练反事实风险估计器；(2) 风险感知重排序——用训练好的估计器对候选问题重写进行重排序，优先选择对缺失或错配视觉证据更敏感（即风险更低）的问题。

Result: 在VQA-RAD上，基于Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct，仅提示词重写反而增加风险，而预测风险重排序将保留风险从0.658降至0.623，精确准确率从0.337提升至0.356。在PMC-VQA的300样本外检验中也观察到风险降低和准确率小幅提升。

Conclusion: 问题选择是医学VQA中答案级幻觉缓解的有前景的补充方法，通过选择对视觉证据依赖更强的问题来降低幻觉风险。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering requires models to ground their responses in image evidence, because visually unsupported answers can mislead downstream interpretation. However, many medical VQA questions are generic, template-like, or highly similar in form, which can encourage models to learn question-answer shortcuts instead of image-dependent reasoning and thereby increase the risk of hallucinated responses. We propose Ask4VG, a label-free pilot framework for risk-aware question selection. Ask4VG estimates question-induced hallucination risk through counterfactual visual probing: the same question is asked under the original image, a perturbed image, a blank image, and a mismatched image, and the resulting answer relations are converted into weak supervision for a counterfactual risk estimator. The learned estimator then reranks candidate question rewrites to favor intent-preserving questions that are less invariant to missing or mismatched visual evidence before final answer generation. On VQA-RAD with Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct, prompt-only rewriting increases counterfactual risk, whereas predicted-risk reranking reduces held-out risk from 0.658 to 0.623 and improves exact accuracy from 0.337 to 0.356. A 300-sample PMC-VQA external check shows the same direction of risk reduction with a small accuracy gain. These results suggest that question selection is a promising complement to response-level hallucination mitigation for reliable medical VQA.

</details>


### [126] [Decoupled Residual Denoising Diffusion Models for Unified and Data Efficient Image-to-Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01048)
*Ziyue Lin,Jiahe Hou,Hongyu Xia,Xinrui Xie,Feifei Wang,Yuyin Zhou,Wei Wang,Jiawei Liu,Liangqiong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦残差去噪扩散模型（DRDD），将扩散过程解耦为随机噪声扩散（域协调与流形提升）和确定性残差扩散（核心语义映射），实现统一且数据高效的图像到图像翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在统一I2I翻译中，注入高斯噪声除了流形提升外还具有域协调作用（隐式对齐跨域特征分布），但耦合的扩散过程过早地削弱了这种协调效应，因为噪声和残差被同时移除。

Method: 将扩散过程解耦为两个顺序且独立的阶段：(1) 随机噪声扩散——仅使用未配对目标域图像训练，实现域协调和流形提升；(2) 确定性残差扩散——在固定噪声域内学习核心语义映射。

Result: DRDD与主流扩散模型广泛兼容，在有限配对数据下也能实现鲁棒、统一的I2I翻译，显著提升数据效率，理论分析与实验均验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦噪声扩散和残差扩散，保留域协调效应，简化了跨任务和跨域的统一映射学习，为数据高效的统一I2I翻译提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose Decoupled Residual Denoising Diffusion models (DRDD) for unified and data-efficient image-to-image (I2I) translation. While diffusion models have advanced I2I translation in terms of quality and diversity, we uncover a previously under-explored property in diffusion models. Crucially, beyond its conventional role of manifold lifting (i.e., moving data off low-dimensional manifolds), injecting Gaussian noise facilitates domain harmonization by implicitly aligning feature distributions across domains, a property particularly advantageous for unified I2I translation. However, existing diffusion models prematurely erode this harmonization effect, as noise and residuals are simultaneously removed in a single coupled diffusion process. To address this, DRDD decouples the diffusion process into two sequential and independent diffusion stages: (1) a stochastic noise diffusion for domain harmonization and manifold lifting, and (2) a deterministic residual diffusion that learns the core semantic mapping entirely within the fixed-noise domain. This decoupling preserves harmonization and manifold lifting effects throughout the transformation, substantially simplifying the learning of unified mappings across diverse tasks and domains. Notably, the noise diffusion stage is trained exclusively on abundant, unpaired target-domain images, greatly improving data efficiency. Comprehensive theoretical and empirical analysis demonstrates that DRDD is broadly compatible with mainstream diffusion models and consistently delivers robust, unified I2I translation, even under limited paired data. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKU-HealthAI/DRDD.

</details>


### [127] [TextFake: Benchmarking AI-Generated Image Detection on Text-Rich Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01050)
*Yuning Zhang,Changtao Miao,Mingyu Liao,Tingyu Liu,Xinghao Wang,Tao Gong,Qi Chu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextFake基准（2万张图像，28种语言），系统评估了现有AIGI检测器在含文本伪造图像上的表现，发现所有方法准确率均低于80%，并归纳出三种主要失败模式：文本密度诅咒、渲染保真度伪装和阈值坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGI检测器在自然图像基准上表现良好，但在含大量文本的伪造内容（如伪造截图、文档、新闻页面）中的行为尚未被测试，而这些正是虚假信息传播中常见的媒介。

Method: 构建了涵盖28种语言、4个主题类别、2种场景模态的2万张图像基准TextFake；通过四阶段pipeline合成伪造图像：在真实图像上沿三个受控维度进行标注，并通过分布对齐的结构化提示生成对应伪造图像，排除协变量捷径；对14种专用检测器和3种前沿VLM API进行零样本评估。

Result: 所有方法在TextFake上的准确率均未超过80%，部分方法相比自然图像基准下降超过60%；诊断评估识别出三种失败模式：文本密度诅咒（密集字形淹没低级检测器）、渲染保真度伪装（更强的文本渲染抑制生成伪影）、阈值坍塌（常规扰动使检测器降至随机水平）。

Conclusion: 现有AIGI检测器在文本丰富的伪造图像上存在系统性缺陷，亟需开发针对此类内容的新型检测方法。

Abstract: Recent AI-generated image (AIGI) detectors perform well on natural-image benchmarks, but their behavior on text-rich forgeries, such as fabricated screenshots, documents, and news pages prevalent in misinformation, remains untested. We introduce TextFake, a 20,000-image benchmark for text-rich AIGI detection spanning 28 languages, 4 topic categories, and 2 scene modalities. Fake images are synthesized via a four-stage pipeline that annotates real images along three controlled dimensions and generates counterparts through distribution-aligned structured prompting, ruling out covariate shortcuts. Zero-shot evaluation of 14 specialized detectors and 3 frontier VLM APIs reveals a large systematic gap: no method exceeds 80% accuracy, with some dropping over 60% from natural-image benchmarks. Diagnostic evaluations identify three failure modes: the Text Density Curse, where dense glyphs overwhelm low-level detectors; Cloaking via Rendering Fidelity, where stronger text rendering suppresses enerative artifacts; and Threshold Collapse, where routine perturbations drive detectors toward chance-level performance.

</details>


### [128] [3DCodeBench: Benchmarking Agentic Procedural 3D Modeling Via Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01057)
*Yipeng Gao,Lei Shu,Genzhi Ye,Xi Xiong,Ameesh Makadia,Meiqi Guo,Laurent Itti,Jindong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3DCodeBench基准和3DCodeArena排名平台，用于系统评估视觉语言模型在程序化3D建模中的表现。研究发现主要失败源于API不匹配和几何组件分离/漂浮问题，测试时扩展（如更高思考预算和多轮精炼）可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化3D建模具有确定性、可直接用于引擎、可精确编辑等优势，但传统方法需要深厚的3D软件API、参数化设计和代码级几何推理专业知识。现有神经3D生成器缺乏这些特性，而VLM在执行此类任务时缺乏系统评估基准。

Method: 构建包含多模态（文本/图像）提示、程序化代码、3D对象三元组的3DCodeBench数据集；设计基于成对人类偏好的3DCodeArena排名平台；对12种先进VLM进行系统评估。

Result: (1) 失败主要源于API不匹配，成功渲染的案例仍存在几何组件分离或漂浮问题；(2) 测试时扩展（更高思考预算、多轮精炼）可提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 需要高质量的程序化编码数据来推动商业VLM发展；有效的程序化3D建模需要提供高保真反馈的鲁棒执行环境以支持迭代精炼。

Abstract: Procedural 3D modeling through code is emerging as a versatile paradigm, offering deterministic, engine-ready, and precisely editable assets that neural 3D generators inherently lack. Authoring such procedural content, however, demands deep expertise in 3D software APIs, parametric design, and code-level geometric reasoning. In this paper, we propose 3DCodeBench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating vision-language model (VLM) agents for procedural 3D generation in 3D modeling software. Specifically, 3DCodeBench evaluates how effectively 12 advanced VLMs can serve as procedural 3D modelers by translating text and image references into procedural code for 3D modeling software. Recognizing that automated metrics may not fully capture the perceptual quality of 3D shapes, we build 3DCodeArena, a ranking platform based on pairwise human preferences over generated 3D outputs. From extensive evaluations and results, we observe that: (1) Failures mostly arise from API mismatches, while successful renders still suffer from disconnected or floating 3D geometric components. (2) Test-time scaling, such as higher thinking budgets and multi-turn refinement, improves performance overall. Our findings highlight a critical need for high-quality procedural coding data to advance commercial VLMs. Furthermore, effective procedural 3D modeling requires a robust execution environment that provides high-fidelity feedback for iterative refinement. We release 3DCodeBench, including the curated large-scale dataset of multimodal (text/image) prompts, procedural code, 3D object triplets, evaluation protocol, and the public 3DCodeArena platform as a foundational toolkit for exploring VLM-based procedural 3D modelers.

</details>


### [129] [A Multiscale Network with Supervised Contrastive Learning for Real-Time Facial Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01069)
*Rejoy Chakraborty,Archisman Adhikary,Chayan Halder,Payel Rakshit,Sanchita Ghosh,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的实时面部表情情绪变化检测系统，在标准数据集上取得了满意的效果，可用于心理咨询等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频中面部表情的情绪识别具有挑战性，因为情绪状态随时间连续变化，且不同个体的表情表达差异显著，现有计算方法难以有效表示这种连续性变化。开发能检测面部表情变化的系统对心理学咨询等领域具有重要意义。

Method: 采用深度学习系统，通过对人脸面部表情变化进行建模，实现对实时视频中个体情绪变化的检测。

Result: 在标准数据集上训练并取得了非常满意的结果。

Conclusion: 提出的深度学习系统能够有效检测实时视频中的情绪变化，有望为心理咨询师提供关于被试情绪状态的额外洞察。

Abstract: Real-time emotion recognition from facial expressions is a challenging task, particularly in video-based scenarios where multiple emotional states may occur over time. The difficulty increases further due to the fact that each emotional state is associated with facial expressions that vary significantly across individuals. The change of facial expressions portraying emotional state is not discrete, but rather continuous, which is very challenging to represent through computational aids. A system with the ability to detect variations in facial expressions can have a significant impact on determining the emotional state of an individual. Such a system can be very beneficial for psychologists during counseling by providing additional insights into the emotional state of a subject. In this paper, a deep learning-based system is presented to detect emotional changes in real-time video of a person by modeling the change in facial expressions. The current study is conducted on a standard dataset for training of the deep learning system and the system has provided very satisfactory outcomes in this respect.

</details>


### [130] [Chameleon: Style-Content Disentangled Framework for Cross-Domain Object Compositing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01079)
*Sukhun Ko,Soo Ye Kim,Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对跨域图像合成中前景与背景域不匹配的问题，构建了首个大规模训练数据集ChameleonDataset，并提出两阶段训练框架Chameleon，通过联合硬对比学习（JHCL）解耦风格与内容表示，以及时空注意力门控（STAG）自适应调节风格注入，在保持前景物体身份的同时实现风格匹配，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域图像合成方法缺乏大规模配对训练数据，只能依赖无训练的混合与精炼策略，导致仅能进行色调级对齐，结果往往风格不一致或过度风格化。为解决这一局限，需要构建大规模配对数据集并开发基于训练的有效方法。

Method: 首先通过可扩展的数据构建流程构建大规模跨域合成数据集ChameleonDataset；然后提出两阶段框架Chameleon：第一阶段使用联合硬对比学习（JHCL）训练ChameleonEncoder以解耦风格和内容表征；第二阶段在扩散Transformer中引入时空注意力门控（STAG），自适应调节第一阶段编码器的风格令牌在空间和时间维度上的注入方式。

Result: 该方法在合成合理性和风格保真度上均优于最先进的域内合成、跨域合成模型、流水线模型以及商业模型。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模数据集和两阶段训练框架，有效解决了跨域图像合成中前景身份保持与风格匹配的平衡问题，为跨域合成领域提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Image compositing aims to seamlessly insert a foreground object into a background image, and recent advances in diffusion models have significantly enhanced the quality, especially when the foreground and background images come from the same domain (e.g., natural images). However, cross-domain compositing, where the foreground and background come from different domains, is relatively underexplored and remains challenging because the model must preserve the foreground object's identity while stylizing it to match the background domain. Existing cross-domain compositing approaches largely rely on training-free blending and refinement strategies. This is partly due to the lack of large-scale paired datasets for cross-domain compositing, limiting the development of training-based solutions. As a result, they are limited to tone-level alignment and often produce style-inconsistent or overstylized results. To overcome such limitations, we construct ChameleonDataset, the first large-scale training dataset for cross-domain compositing, with a comprehensive evaluation benchmark, built through a scalable data construction pipeline. Building on this, we propose Chameleon, a novel two-stage training-based cross-domain compositing framework. In the first stage, we propose Joint Hard Contrastive Learning (JHCL) to train ChameleonEncoder, which effectively disentangles style and content representations. In the second stage, we introduce Spatio-Temporal Attention Gating (STAG) into a diffusion transformer for effective stylization, adaptively regulating how style tokens from the first-stage encoder are injected across spatial and temporal dimensions. Our method outperforms state-of-the-art in-domain and cross-domain compositing models, sequential pipelines and commercial models, achieving improvements in both compositional plausibility and stylistic fidelity.

</details>


### [131] [Dual-Route Top-K Retrieval with 1v1 VLM Reranking for the CoVR-R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01097)
*Yuyang Sun,Yongliang Wu,Xingyu Zhu,Yuxia Chen,Zhenxiang Jiang,Yangguang Ji,Wenbo Zhu,Yanxi Shi,Jay Wu,Shuo Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双路Top-K检索与1v1 VLM重排序方法，通过召回-选择解耦策略在CoVR-R任务上取得95.28 R@1等领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将组合视频检索解耦为两个耦合子问题：获取足够完整的Top-K候选集，以及安全判断候选是否应替换当前最优结果，以提升检索精度。

Method: 1) 改进推理/文本路由：使用VLM槽位选择器筛选候选，不引入DFN视觉检索；2) 增加视觉路由：基于contact-sheet嵌入使用DFN-H/DFN-L；3) 合并两路得到Top-10候选集；4) VLM最终重排序器对当前Top-1与每个挑战者进行保守的1v1比较。

Result: 隐藏测试集上R@1=95.28, R@5=97.47, R@10=98.48, R@50=99.66。

Conclusion: CoVR-R任务中，召回-选择解耦策略优于宽泛的文本重排序或直接多候选VLM分类。

Abstract: We describe \emph{Dual-Route Top-K Retrieval with 1v1 VLM Reranking} for the CoVR-R challenge. The method treats composed video retrieval as two coupled problems: finding a sufficiently complete top-k candidate set, and then safely deciding whether any candidate should replace a strong current top-1. We first improve the reasoning/text seed with a VLM slot selector over existing candidates, without introducing DFN visual retrieval. We then add a visual route from contact-sheet embeddings using DFN-H/DFN-L. The routes are merged into a top-10 candidate set, after which a VLM final reranker performs conservative 1v1 comparisons between the current top-1 and each challenger. On the hidden test split, the final system reaches 95.28 R@1, 97.47 R@5, 98.48 R@10, and 99.66 R@50. The main lesson is that CoVR-R benefits more from recall-selection decoupling than from broad text reranking or direct multi-candidate VLM classification.

</details>


### [132] [Adaptive Dense Evidence Refinement for Video Relational Reasoning for VRR-QA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01104)
*Yuyang Sun,Yongliang Wu,Xingyu Zhu,Yuxia Chen,Zhenxiang Jiang,Yangguang Ji,Wenbo Zhu,Yanxi Shi,Jay Wu,Shuo Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个自适应测试时计算的VQA系统，通过轻量级视图识别不稳定问题，仅对困难问题路由到高预算密集证据模块，在VRR-QA上达到90.07%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言系统难以推断单帧无法解决的时空、视角、深度和可见性关系，现有VQA方法常混淆"寻找替代答案"与"决定何时应改变当前答案"这两个问题。

Method: 两阶段自适应推理：1) 直接视频语言模型推理得到初步答案；2) 通过多个轻量级视图识别不稳定问题；3) 仅对困难问题激活高预算密集证据模块，该模块包含时间戳帧观察、关系特定探测、候选验证与保守时间聚合。

Result: 在测试集上平均准确率90.07%，宏平均准确率87.81%。

Conclusion: 将"寻找替代答案"与"决定何时改变答案"解耦是有效的，自适应测试时计算能显著提升复杂视频关系推理的准确性。

Abstract: VRR-QA evaluates whether video-language systems can infer spatial, temporal, viewpoint, depth, and visibility relations that are not always resolved by a single frame. We present an inference-only system built around adaptive test-time computation. The system first answers each question with a direct video-language model pass, then uses multiple lightweight views to find unstable questions. Only these difficult questions are routed to a high-budget dense evidence module that constructs timestamped frame observations, relation-specific probes, candidate verification, and conservative temporal aggregation. This design separates two problems that are often confused in video question answering: finding plausible alternative answers and deciding when a current answer should actually be changed. On the test split, the final system obtains 90.07 average accuracy and 87.81 macro average accuracy. The report focuses on the final test system and the implementation settings required to reproduce the adaptive dense verifier.

</details>


### [133] [Temporal Evidence Routing with Structured Visual Evidence for TimeLogicQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01106)
*Yuyang Sun,Yongliang Wu,Xingyu Zhu,Yuxia Chen,Zhenxiang Jiang,Yangguang Ji,Wenbo Zhu,Yanxi Shi,Jay Wu,Shuo Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeLogicQA是一个评估视频问答系统时间推理能力的基准，本文提出了一种视觉证据路由管道，将感知与符号时间推理分离，通过多模态大模型生成结构化视觉证据、程序化验证器恢复密集动作区间、确定性缩减器应用时间规则，最终在官方测试集上达到81.8的AvgAcc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答系统缺乏对时间关系（如事件存在性、顺序、持久性、边界条件和重叠）的有效推理能力，亟需一个专门的评估基准和相应的解决方案。

Method: 提出视觉证据路由管道，包含四个步骤：1) 将问题解析为事件目标、答案模式、候选选项和时间算子；2) 根据视频时长和算子难度路由视频，短片段使用有序全帧证据，长视频使用事件聚焦候选窗口；3) 多模态大语言模型生成结构化视觉证据，程序化验证器恢复密集动作区间；4) 确定性缩减器应用算子特定的时间规则产生最终答案，保守融合机制仅在视觉证据、时间程序和置信度检查一致时接受答案。

Result: 在官方测试集上，最终系统达到81.8的AvgAcc。

Conclusion: 将感知与符号时间推理分离，结合程序化验证和保守融合策略，能有效提升视频问答系统的时间推理能力。

Abstract: TimeLogicQA evaluates whether video question answering systems can reason over temporal relations such as event existence, ordering, persistence, boundary conditions, and overlap. We address this task with a visual evidence routing pipeline that separates perception from symbolic temporal reasoning. The system first parses each question into event targets, answer mode, candidate options, and temporal operators. It then routes videos according to duration and operator difficulty, using ordered full-frame evidence for short clips and event-focused candidate windows for long videos. A multimodal large language model produces structured visual evidence for the relevant events, while programmatic verifiers recover dense action intervals and a deterministic reducer applies operator-specific temporal rules to produce the final answer. Conservative fusion accepts an answer only when the visual evidence, temporal program, and confidence checks agree, reducing noisy answer flips. On the official test evaluation, our final system achieves an AvgAcc of 81.8.

</details>


### [134] [R^3: Composed Video Retrieval via Reasoning-Guided Recalling and Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01113)
*Zixu Li,Yupeng Hu,Zhiheng Fu,Zhiwei Chen,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R³，一种零样本组合视频检索框架，通过推理引导的召回与重排序，将源视频和文本编辑指令转化为推理增强查询，显著提升了组合视频检索的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合视频检索（CoVR）中，查询由源视频和文本编辑指令共同定义，标准视频-文本检索方法难以准确表达目标视频中的状态变化、动作替换、物体保留或时间一致性等细节，而穷举重排序计算成本过高。

Method: R³框架包含三个阶段：(1) 生成推理轨迹，描述应用编辑后预期的目标视频；(2) 将推理轨迹与源视频编码为推理增强查询，通过一致性门控残差机制与基础组合查询融合进行召回；(3) 通过源-候选视频直接比较的重排序器验证召回结果。

Result: 实验证明了该方法在组合视频检索挑战中的有效性。

Conclusion: 将源-编辑查询转化为推理引导的检索程序，而非将编辑文本视为简短描述，能够更准确地表达目标视频特征，提升检索性能。

Abstract: The CoVR-R challenge evaluates composed video retrieval, where a system must retrieve a target video from a large gallery given a reference video and a textual edit instruction. This setting is not a standard video-text retrieval problem: the query is defined by both the visual evidence in the source video and the transformation implied by the edit. A strong embedding model can provide scalable candidate recall, but it may under-express target-side consequences such as state changes, action replacement, object preservation, or temporal consistency. A pairwise multimodal reranker can verify such details more directly, but exhaustive reranking over the full gallery is computationally infeasible. We present $\mathbb{R}^3$, a zero-shot composed video retrieval pipeline built around Reasoning-guided Recalling and Reranking. The core idea is to turn the source-edit query into a reasoning-grounded retrieval program rather than treating the edit text as a short caption. First, the model generates a reasoning trace that describes the expected target video after applying the edit. Then the trace is encoded together with the source video as a reasoning-augmented query, and its retrieval score is fused with the base composed query through an agreement-gated residual rule. At last, a re-ranker verifies the recalled candidates with direct source-candidate comparison. Experiments have demonstrated the effectiveness of our method in addressing this challenge. Codes are available on https://github.com/Lee-zixu/R-3.

</details>


### [135] [Rank-Aware Quantile Activation for Motion-Robust Crop Segmentation in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01118)
*Abinav Kiran,Sravan Danda,Aditya Challa,Sougata Sen,Daya Sagar B S*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双分位数激活（QAct），用实例级秩归一化替代幅度门控，在高速无人机运动模糊场景下提升语义分割性能，尤其在稀有纹理依赖类上效果显著；结合模糊域蒸馏可取得最佳鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速无人机采集导致的运动模糊破坏了标准CNN依赖的高频幅度特征，使得稀有纹理依赖类（具有高农艺价值）的语义分割性能严重下降，传统ReLU激活无法应对这种统计性信号擦除问题。

Method: 提出Dual Quantile Activation (QAct)，一种秩感知模块，用实例级秩归一化替代传统的幅度门控机制，在特征层面进行分位数级别的激活调整。

Result: 在Agriculture-Vision 2021数据集上，QAct在零样本和模糊监督两种设置下、多种模糊程度上均持续优于ReLU，获得mIoU增益；零样本QAct在中等模糊下超越蒸馏训练的ReLU；Distill-QAct在所有模糊程度上取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 秩感知激活（QAct）与模糊域训练（蒸馏）是互补的鲁棒性来源，两者结合可在运动模糊场景下取得最优分割性能。

Abstract: Motion blur from high-speed UAV acquisition de-grades semantic segmentation on rare texture-dependent classes with high agronomic value. Standard CNNs rely on high-frequency magnitude features that blur destroys, causing statistical erasure of minority signals. We propose Dual Quantile Activation (QAct), a rank-aware block replacing magnitude gating with instance-level rank normalization. Evaluated onAgriculture-Vision 2021 across zero-shot and blur-supervised regimes at multiple severities, QAct is the dominant architectural factor: it delivers consistent mIoU gains over ReLU across both regimes and all severities, with strongest gains on rare structural and texture-dependent classes. Some dominant classes (water,planter skip) show mixed per-class performance under distillation. At moderate blur, zero-shot QAct outperforms distillation-trained ReLU; across all severities, Distill-QAct achieves best performance, confirming rank aware activation and blur-domain training are complementary robustness sources.

</details>


### [136] [HakushoBench: A Japanese Chart and Table VQA Benchmark from Governmental White Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01132)
*Issa Sugiura,Shuhei Kurita,Yusuke Oda,Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HakushoBench，一个基于日本政府白皮书构建的日文图表VQA基准，包含2,053张图像和10种以上类型，评估发现开源模型最佳准确率仅58.6%，与闭源模型差距达34.9个百分点，揭示了非英语图表理解领域的巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表理解基准主要针对英语，非英语基准稀缺，难以评估视觉语言模型在多语言场景下的泛化能力，且大规模收集真实多样的非英语图表图像存在困难。

Method: 利用各国政府白皮书作为可扩展的非英语基准构建来源，从中提取自然出现的图表和表格；以日本为例，从33份政府白皮书中构建了含2,053张图像、覆盖10种以上图像类型的HakushoBench基准，并人工标注了问答对。

Result: 开源模型中最佳准确率为58.6%，开源模型与闭源模型（如GPT-4V等）之间准确率差距达34.9个百分点，表明HakushoBench对开源模型仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 非英语图表理解任务仍存在显著进步空间，政府白皮书可作为多语言图表基准构建的有效可扩展来源。

Abstract: Understanding chart and table images is essential for applying vision-language models (VLMs) to real-world document understanding. While English benchmarks have advanced rapidly, non-English counterparts remain scarce, leaving it unclear whether this progress generalizes across languages. A key obstacle is the difficulty of collecting realistic and diverse non-English chart and table images at scale. To address this, we leverage governmental white papers as a scalable source for benchmark construction beyond English, as they contain naturally occurring charts and tables across diverse formats and domains and are freely accessible in many countries. As a first instantiation, we introduce HakushoBench, a challenging Japanese chart and table VQA benchmark built from 33 governmental white papers. HakushoBench contains 2,053 images spanning over 10 image types, with manually annotated QA pairs, designed to assess deep and holistic understanding of charts and tables, rather than local visual cues alone. Experiments across a broad range of VLMs demonstrate that HakushoBench remains challenging for open-weight models: the best open-weight model achieves only 58.6% accuracy, and a 34.9-point gap between open-weight and proprietary models highlights substantial room for improvement in complex chart and table understanding. We release our dataset and code.

</details>


### [137] [CoSTL: Comprehensive Spatial-Temporal Representation Learning for Moment Retrieval and Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01149)
*Xin Dong,Wenjia Geng,Wenfeng Deng,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoSTL框架，通过文本驱动的渐进式细粒度图像编码器和多尺度时序感知模块，同时捕获细粒度空间信息和时序动态，在视频时刻检索和高亮检测任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要使用时序建模处理帧级特征，忽略了单帧内与文本查询相关的丰富视觉信息，导致定位结果不准确。

Method: 提出CoSTL框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 文本驱动的渐进式细粒度图像编码器，通过两步文本驱动知识提取过程学习细粒度空间表示；(2) 多尺度时序感知模块，捕获全面的时空表示以增强时序动态处理能力。

Result: 在QVHighlights、Charades-STA、TACoS和TVSum四个公开基准上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 联合建模细粒度图像级信息和时序动态对视频时刻检索和高亮检测任务至关重要，CoSTL通过文本驱动的空间编码与多尺度时序感知相结合，有效解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Video Moment Retrieval (MR) and Highlight Detection (HD) are crucial tasks in video analysis that aim to localize specific moments and estimate clip-wise relevance based on a given text query. Recent approaches treat them as similar video grounding tasks and use the same architecture to solve them. These tasks require both fine-grained comprehension at the image level and high-level temporal understanding across the entire video. Existing approaches have primarily focused on temporal modeling using frame-level features, often neglecting the rich visual information related to the text query within individual frames. This oversight leads to inaccurate grounding results. To address this limitation, we propose a Comprehensive Spatial-Temporal Representation Learning Framework (CoSTL), which captures both fine-grained image-level information and temporal dynamics. Specifically, CoSTL incorporates a text-driven progressive fine-grained image encoder, performing a two-step text-driven knowledge extraction process to learn fine-grained spatial representations. Furthermore, a multi-scale temporal perception module captures comprehensive spatial-temporal representations, enhancing the model's ability to process temporal dynamics. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on four public benchmarks: QVHighlights, Charades-STA, TACoS, and TVSum.

</details>


### [138] [HiTokSR: A Coarse-to-Fine Tokenizer with Hierarchical Codebooks for High-Fidelity Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01157)
*Mingxi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiTokSR提出了一种分层 token 预测框架，通过将潜在空间沿通道维度分解为频率感知组并用独立子码本量化，解耦全局结构与细节纹理，结合视觉基础模型先验和索引级扰动策略，在真实图像超分辨率中实现了最优的感知质量和重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQ生成模型在Real-ISR中依赖单一潜在空间，纠缠了低频结构与高频纹理，导致单个码本需捕获组合上复杂的结构-纹理配对，限制了表示能力和码本利用率。

Method: 1) 将潜在空间沿通道维度划分为频率感知组，每组用独立子码本量化，实现粗到细的解耦；2) 生成器集成视觉基础模型先验（自适应特征调制、多尺度类别token、表示对齐损失）；3) 解码器微调时引入索引级扰动策略，弥合离散token预测中的训练-测试差异。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上，HiTokSR在感知质量和重建保真度方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过分层解耦与先验融合，HiTokSR有效提升了码本利用率和组合表达能力，为Real-ISR提供了一种新的高效框架。

Abstract: Vector-quantized (VQ) generative models have shown promising results in real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR). However, existing methods typically rely on a monolithic latent space that entangles low-frequency structures with high-frequency textures. This entanglement forces a single codebook to capture a combinatorially complex set of structure-texture pairings, which constrains representational capacity and limits codebook utilization. To address this issue, we present HiTokSR, a hierarchical token prediction framework. Instead of using a single codebook, HiTokSR partitions the latent space along the channel dimension into frequency-aware groups, quantizing each with an independent sub-codebook. This coarse-to-fine design disentangles global structures from fine details, enhancing combinatorial expressiveness while circumventing the optimization instability of high-dimensional nearest-neighbor lookups. To further improve semantic consistency, our generator integrates priors from a vision foundation model via adaptive feature modulation, multi-scale class tokens, and a representation alignment loss. Additionally, we introduce an index-level perturbation strategy during decoder fine-tuning to bridge the train-test discrepancy in discrete token prediction. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that HiTokSR achieves state-of-the-art performance in both perceptual quality and reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [139] [Towards Interactive Video World Modeling: Frontiers, Challenges, Benchmarks, and Future Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01164)
*Jiuming Liu,Chaojun Ni,Mengmeng Liu,Chensheng Peng,Fangjinhua Wang,Sitian Shen,Marc Pollefeys,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Ayush Tewari,Per Ola Kristensson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了交互式世界建模领域的最新研究趋势、技术进展、评估基准及未来方向，涵盖动作可控性、长程交互记忆、实时响应等关键技术挑战，以及开放世界探索、游戏引擎、自动驾驶和机器人四个应用领域的基准对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和扩散模型的发展，世界建模受到越来越多关注，但现有综述缺乏对交互式世界建模的系统性梳理，本文旨在填补这一空白，推动该领域的进一步发展。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，首先从应用场景、世界状态演化和场景模态三个维度总结现有工作与趋势；然后深入分析三个关键技术挑战（动作条件可控性、长程交互与记忆、动作跟随实时响应）；进而对比四个应用领域的现有基准与评估指标；最后展望未来方向。

Result: 总结了交互式世界建模在动作条件视频/3D生成范式下的最新进展，识别出三个核心技术挑战，并系统对比了开放世界探索、游戏引擎、自动驾驶和机器人四个领域的基准和指标。

Conclusion: 交互式世界建模正处于快速发展阶段，未来需在实时交互性、长程一致性、跨模态泛化等方面进一步突破，以实现下一代交互式世界模型。

Abstract: With rapid development of large language models and diffusion-based content generation, world modeling has attracted increasing research attention, benefiting various downstream domains such as game engines, embodied AI, autonomous driving, etc. Through explicitly incorporating user actions into world state transition, recent literature empowers world modeling with interactivity in an action-conditioned video or 3D generation paradigm, further enhancing controllability over world evolutions and facilitating users to freely traverse, manipulate, navigate, and personalize the state evolution. In this paper, we aim to systematically review recent research trends, technical developments, evaluation benchmarks, and also propose future potential directions in interactive world modeling. Specifically, we first summarize recent efforts and trends in terms of application scenarios, world state evolution, and scene modality. Afterwards, we delve into three crucial technical challenges, including action-conditioned controllability, long-horizon interactions and memory, and action-following responsiveness for real-time interactivity. Furthermore, we also thoroughly compare existing benchmarks and metrics in four specific application fields: open-world exploration, game engine, autonomous driving, and robotics. Finally, we discuss several promising future directions in achieving next-generation interactive world modeling. The corresponding repository is publicly available at: https://github.com/liujiuming123/Awesome-Interactive-World-Model.

</details>


### [140] [Reusing Fusion-Time Spectral Reliability for Adaptive Fusion and Expert Routing in RGB-Infrared Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01173)
*Yefeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数无关的7维光谱可靠性描述子，用于表征RGB-红外跨模态融合的可靠性，并通过两项设计（SRF和RCER）将该描述子复用于融合和后融合阶段，显著提升了多模态目标检测在合成退化与自然昼夜场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-红外检测器在跨模态融合过程中产生的统计信息通常被丢弃，导致下游模块无法判断当前交互是否可靠，缺乏对融合质量的显式反馈机制。

Method: 提出参数无关的7维光谱可靠性描述子，包含波段能量、幅度比、相位一致性和跨模态相关性；基于该描述子设计两个模块：SRF（利用描述子门控光谱残差与保守空间基底的融合）和RCER（结合描述子与池化内容引导稀疏后融合专家路由）。

Result: 在消融实验中，描述子感知的门控优于纯内容自适应门控mAP50；2×2析因分析显示描述子条件路由比专家架构本身带来更大边际收益。在DroneVehicle的六种合成退化下，平均保持率95.0%（对比内容MoE 92.0%、拼接87.9%），在自然昼夜划分下mAP50提升+5.2/+5.3。

Conclusion: 保留跨模态融合时的可靠性作为显式信号，有利于自适应融合和后融合条件计算，验证了描述子作为中间表征的有效性。

Abstract: RGB-infrared detectors typically discard the statistics generated during cross-modal fusion, leaving downstream modules unaware of whether the current interaction is reliable. We propose to extract a parameter-free, 7-dimensional spectral reliability descriptor -- summarizing band energy, amplitude ratio, phase consistency, and cross-modal correlation -- and to reuse it beyond the fusion stage. The descriptor drives both Spectral Reliability Fusion (SRF), which gates a spectral residual against a conservative spatial base, and Reliability-Conditioned Expert Routing (RCER), which combines the descriptor with pooled content to steer sparse post-fusion experts. Under matched ablations, descriptor-aware gating improves mAP50 over content-only adaptive gating; a $2{\times}2$ factorial analysis further shows that descriptor-conditioned routing provides the larger marginal gain over expert architecture alone at near-equal parameter count. Under six synthetic degradations on DroneVehicle, average retention rises to 95.0%, versus 92.0% for content-only MoE and 87.9% for concatenation, with the largest gain under modality drop; the same model also improves mAP50 by +5.2/+5.3 on the natural day/night split. These results suggest that preserving fusion-time reliability as an explicit signal benefits both adaptive fusion and post-fusion conditional computation.

</details>


### [141] [PairedGTA: Generating Driving Datasets for Controlled Photometric Shift Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01192)
*Andrea Chianese,Giulio Rossolini,Alessandro Biondi,Marco Cococcioni,Giorgio Buttazzo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于GTA游戏引擎的框架，生成完美配对的驾驶场景图像，支持在不同光照和天气条件下独立评估环境变化对视觉感知系统的影响，从而更准确分析语义分割模型的性能退化原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界数据集难以获取同一场景在不同环境条件下的完美配对图像（因相机位姿、交通流、动态物体位置等随时间变化），导致难以独立评估光照和天气变化（光度偏移）对感知系统的影响，无法区分性能退化是由环境变化引起还是由场景几何/语义变化引起。

Method: 基于高保真GTA游戏引擎的数据生成框架。通过软件API与游戏引擎通信，在保持场景几何、相机位姿、动态物体身份和位置不变的前提下，程序化修改光照和天气条件，渲染像素级对齐的图像。针对每个采样位置，程序化实例化动态实体并生成不同恶劣条件下的对齐图像。

Result: 在驾驶场景中系统分析了语义分割模型，证明模型输出的退化可更直接地归因于光度偏移，而非不受控的语义或几何因素。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了在受控条件下评估自动驾驶视觉感知系统的有效手段，能够隔离环境变化的影响，为模型在恶劣条件下的鲁棒性分析提供更公平、更精确的基准。

Abstract: Evaluating the performance of visual perception systems for autonomous driving is essential to ensure reliable operation across diverse environmental scenarios. Ideally, a balanced and fair analysis across different adverse conditions would require perfectly paired images of the same scene under different weather or illumination changes. This would allow evaluating the effect of photometric shifts independently of geometry and semantic changes.
