A fine-tuned multimodal large language model then acts as a virtual director, mapping natural language descriptions and coarse human motion to sparse DSL keyframes that are deterministically interpolated into continuous camera trajectories, which are then provided as input to video generators. We train and evaluate Auteur on a new dataset of 34K aligned text, human motion, and DSL-annotated camera trajectories drawn from procedural synthesis and real-world movie footage from the CondensedMovies dataset. Auteur enables cinematographic framing of human-centered scenes, a capability largely absent in prior generative models. To assess this behavior, we propose new framing-focused metrics, and our experiments show that Auteur consistently outperforms existing methods

</details>


### [214] [The Image Reconstruction Game: Drawing Common Ground Through Iterative Multimodal Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01901)
*Sherzod Hakimov,Mattia D'Agostini,Ivan Samodelkin,David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像重建游戏这一全自动基准，通过多轮视觉-语言模型交互使累积共同理解直接可视化为渲染图像。实验发现描述者是重建质量的主导因素，而生成器决定迭代优化是否有益。数学和几何图像最具挑战性。描述者的token预算强烈影响收敛效果。人类验证显示最优自动评判与人类偏好的一致性仅为轻微至一般水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以直接观察和量化视觉-语言模型在多轮交互中建立的共同理解（common ground）。本文旨在构建一个可自动执行、结果直接可视化的基准，通过图像重建游戏来评估和剖析模型间多轮交互的沟通效率。

Method: 设计图像重建游戏：描述者（Describer）模型向生成者（Generator）模型发出多轮修正指令，生成者据此逐步优化图像。交叉组合两种描述者模型和两种生成者模型，在七个图像类别上进行基准测试。分析描述者token预算、修正词汇类别、迭代轮次等变量对重建质量的影响。通过人类评估验证自动评判指标的可靠性。

Result: 描述者是重建质量的主导因素，生成者决定迭代优化是否有益；数学和几何图像最难；短token预算产生更稀疏的初次渲染但有更多改进空间，长预算绝对质量更高但改进余地小；强描述者使用更丰富的修正词汇（空间、数值、结构），弱描述者集中于表面属性且倾向于早年停止；最佳自动评判与人类偏好仅达轻微至一般一致性。

Conclusion: 图像重建游戏可作为研究多模态模型间共同理解的有效基准。描述者能力比生成者能力更关键。自动评分需经过人类重新校准才能可靠使用，提示未来工作应关注与人类偏好对齐的评估指标设计。

Abstract: We introduce the Image Reconstruction Game, a fully automated benchmark in which a vision-language model issues corrective instructions to an image generator across multiple turns, making accumulated common ground directly observable as a rendered image. Benchmarking two Describer models crossed with two Generator models across seven image categories, we find that the describer is the dominant factor in reconstruction quality, while the generator determines whether iterative refinement helps or hurts. Mathematical and geometric images pose the greatest challenge. The describer's token budget strongly affects convergence: shorter budgets yield sparser first renderings with more room for visible improvement, while longer budgets raise absolute quality but leave less to fix. Stronger describers use a richer correction vocabulary spanning spatial, numeric, and structural categories, while weaker describers concentrate on surface properties and tend to stop after a few turns. Human validation shows that the best automated judge reaches only slight-to-fair agreement with human preferences, and automated scores require human recalibration to be used reliably.

</details>


### [215] [Residual Decoder Adapter: ID-Preserving Tokenizer Adaption for Autoregressive Text Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01911)
*Dongxing Mao,Jinpeng Wang,Jiahao Tang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Lijuan Wang,Min Li,Jingru Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出残差解码器适配器（RDA），在不重新训练视觉分词器和自回归模型的前提下，通过引入配对码本和平行分支学习像素空间残差，显著提升视觉自回归模型的文本渲染能力，OCR准确率从24.52%提升至58.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自回归模型在整体图像生成上表现良好，但在文本渲染方面存在模糊笔画和破坏字母形状的问题，根本原因在于视觉分词器难以重建细粒度细节。重新训练分词器虽可直接改进但成本高昂，因此需要一种无需重新训练现有模型即可提升文本渲染性能的方法。

Method: 提出残差解码器适配器（RDA），以事后（post-hoc）方式升级现有分词器而不改变其token空间。包含两个核心组件：(i) 配对码本（paired codebook），与原始码本共享token分布；(ii) 并行分支（parallel branch），在像素空间学习重建图像与真实图像之间的微小差异（残差）。这种残差设计使得分词器的增强是非侵入式的，保持与先前AR模型的兼容性。

Result: 在TextAtlas基准测试上，微调后的Janus-Pro在TextVisionBlend子集上OCR准确率从24.52%提升至58.26%，在StyledTextSynth子集上从12.75%提升至36.81%。

Conclusion: RDA能够在不重新训练视觉分词器和AR模型的前提下，大幅提升文本渲染性能，验证了残差式后处理增强的有效性。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (AR) models generate images by predicting discrete tokens that are decoded by a visual tokenizer. Despite demonstrating strong overall image generation ability, they still underperform on text rendering with blur strokes and disrupt letter shapes. In this work, we trace this limitation to the visual tokenizer, which struggles to reconstruct fine-grained detail. Improving the tokenizer is straightforward but expensive, as it necessitates retraining both the tokenizer and the AR model. Can we improve text rendering performance of AR models without retraining the existing tokenizer and AR model? To achieve this, we propose the Residual Decoder Adapter(RDA) that upgrades an existing tokenizer post-hoc without changing its token space. Specifically, it refines the decoder output of the visual tokenizer by introducing two novel components: (i) a paired codebook that shares the token distribution with the original one; (ii) a parallel branch to learn the tiny differences (residual) between the reconstructed image and the ground-truth images in the pixel space. This residual design allows us to enhance the tokenizer non-invasively while preserving compatibility with prior AR models. RDA substantially improves text rendering significantly by a large margin. For instance, we boost finetuned Janus-Pro OCR accuracy rises from 24.52% to 58.26% (TextVisionBlend), from 12.75% to 36.81% (StyledTextSynth) on competitive TextAtlas benchmark. The code is available at https://github.com/CSU-JPG/RDA

</details>


### [216] [Pool-Select-Refine: Allocation-Aware Generative Dataset Distillation with Soft-Label-Guided Latent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01920)
*Wenmin Li,Shunsuke Sakai,Zhongkai Zhao,Tatsuhito Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Pool-Select-Refine"两阶段框架，解决扩散模型数据集蒸馏中"生成即使用"策略导致的预算浪费问题。通过构建过完备候选池、选择性子集采样和潜在空间精炼，将生成、选择与精炼过程解耦，在图像分类基准上取得一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式数据集蒸馏方法采用僵化的"生成即使用"策略，生成的样本直接作为最终蒸馏集，导致候选生成与预算分配紧密耦合，造成有限预算的冗余浪费或生成样本信息量不足。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 构建过完备候选池，在目标预算下选择紧凑子集；2) 在潜在空间中使用教师模型的软标签监督对选定样本进行精炼，提升语义对齐同时保留生成先验。将生成、选择与精炼过程显式解耦。

Result: 在大规模和细粒度图像分类基准上，所提框架相较于扩散式基线方法取得一致性性能提升。

Conclusion: 在精炼之前引入策展阶段是提升扩散式数据集蒸馏性能的简单有效方式。

Abstract: Diffusion-based dataset distillation has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for condensing large-scale datasets into compact synthetic sets. By leveraging pretrained generative priors, these methods can produce realistic class-conditional samples more efficiently than traditional matching-based approaches. However, most existing diffusion-based methods still adopt a rigid ``Generate-and-Use'' strategy, where the generated samples are directly treated as the final distilled set under a fixed images-per-class budget. Such a design tightly couples candidate generation with final budget allocation, which may result in redundant waste of the limited budget or insufficiently informative samples. In this paper, we propose ``Pool-Select-Refine'', a two-stage framework for allocation-aware generative dataset distillation. First, instead of directly using a fixed number of generated samples, we construct an over-complete candidate pool and select a compact subset under the target budget. Second, we refine the selected samples in latent space using soft-label supervision derived from the teacher model, improving semantic alignment while preserving the generative prior. This design explicitly decouples generation, selection, and refinement, enabling more effective use of the distillation budget. Experiments on large-scale and fine-grained image classification benchmarks show that the proposed framework delivers consistent gains over diffusion-based baselines. The results suggest that introducing a curation stage before refinement is a simple yet effective way to improve diffusion-based dataset distillation.

</details>


### [217] [3rd Place at CVPR 2026 CASTLE Challenge: Agentic Multi-View Long-Context Video Understanding via Hierarchical Knowledge Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01933)
*Raghad Albusayes,Munirah Alyahya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的自适应代理框架，通过视频知识图谱和分层检索机制，在CASTLE 2026挑战赛（CVPR 2026 EgoVis Workshop）中取得全球第三名，实现了对600+小时多视角视频流的高效零样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模多模态视频流中高度复杂的视觉、时空和语言问题（如视觉计数、动作定位、多视角追踪和说话者时序推理），应对超长上下文和极端规模带来的挑战。

Method: 提出训练无关的代理框架，包含两大核心组件：(i) 视频知识图谱——映射静态/动态实体、时序关系及交叉事件，支持多跳关系推理；(ii) 自适应代理工作流——通过分层检索与索引解析复杂查询。

Result: 在长上下文多视角视频流上实现了高准确率的零样本推理，在CASTLE 2026挑战赛中获得全球第三名。

Conclusion: 无需训练的代理框架结合知识图谱与分层检索，能够有效应对大规模多视角长视频理解任务，为未来类似挑战提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents our winning methodology for the CASTLE 2026 Challenge at the CVPR 2026 EgoVis Workshop, where our team secured third place globally. The challenge tasks participants with answering highly complex visual, spatiotemporal, and verbal questions, including visual counting, action localization, multi-view tracking and speaker temporal reasoning, within massive, multimodal video streams. The underlying dataset consists of over 600 hours synchronized footage captured by 15 ego and exo camera sources. To tackle the extreme scale and long-context demands of this environment, we introduce a training-free agentic framework optimized for long-form video understanding. Our framework introduces two core architectural components: i) a Video Knowledge Graph that maps static and dynamic entities, their temporal relationships, and intersecting events to enable multi-hop relational reasoning, and ii) an adaptive agentic workflow that resolves complex queries through a hierarchical retrieval and indexing. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework achieves high zero-shot reasoning accuracy on long-context multi-view streams. Our code will be released at https://github.com/RaghadKhaled/CASTLE-Challenge-Framework.

</details>


### [218] [Unified Driving Tokens: Representation- and Geometry-Guided Discrete Tokenizer for Driving World Models and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01935)
*Ziyang Yao,Zeyu Zhu,YunCheng Jiang,Zibin Guo,Huijing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合监督的离散视觉分词器，通过特征解码对齐DINO空间、RGB重建保持外观、相邻帧深度与相对位姿注入几何信息，并采用多码本量化稳定训练。在NAVSIM上，该分词器在重建保真度、表示一致性、规划性能和生成质量上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散视觉分词器多继承自图像生成领域，主要优化像素重建，导致易于生成的特征与驾驶决策所需的解码特征之间存在鸿沟，缺乏对驾驶任务有用的几何和表示引导信息。

Method: 1) 表示引导：通过特征解码将离散瓶颈与冻结的DINO特征空间对齐；2) 外观保持：使用感知损失和对抗损失的RGB重建；3) 几何增强：加入相邻帧深度和相对位姿监督；4) 多码本量化稳定联合训练目标。

Result: 在NAVSIM上：重建保真度和表示一致性提升；固定解码器下规划性能具有竞争力；同等设置下生成质量更优。

Conclusion: 联合监督的离散分词器能够弥合生成与驾驶决策之间的鸿沟，为基于token的世界建模和规划提供更有效的紧凑表示。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens should provide a compact representation for both token-based world modeling and planning in autonomous driving. However, most tokenizers are inherited from image generation and are optimized mainly for pixel reconstruction, which may leave a gap between what is easy to generate and what is useful to decode for driving decisions. We present a representation-guided and geometry-enhanced tokenizer that learns discrete tokens under joint supervision. The tokenizer aligns its discrete bottleneck with a frozen DINO feature space through feature decoding, while preserving appearance via RGB reconstruction with perceptual and adversarial losses. To inject geometric state-related cues, we add adjacent-frame depth and relative-pose supervision during training and stabilize joint objectives with multi-codebook quantization. We evaluate the same learned tokens with a lightweight planning readout and a GPT-style next-token world model. Experiments on NAVSIM show improved reconstruction fidelity and representation consistency, competitive planning performance under a fixed decoder, and better generative quality under matched settings.

</details>


### [219] [SAVMap: Structure-Aided Visual Mapping of Large-Scale 2.5D Manhattan Wireframes from Panoramic Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01939)
*Howard Huang,Bharath Surianarayanan,Keifer Lee,Chenyu Wang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAVMap提出了一种仅使用全景视频相机作为输入，生成仓库货架和灯光结构语义线框地图的方法。通过从全景视频中提取货架和天花板视图，利用语义分割网络提取结构特征点，并结合曼哈顿网格等几何约束的结构运动恢复算法，从一小时全景视频中为46排货架（每排跨度55m×7m）的5000多个货架元素生成了线框地图，累计平均绝对误差为4.8cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境的精确3D表示对于机器人定位和数字孪生生成等任务至关重要，而现有方法在仓库这类结构化环境中存在效率或精度不足的问题。

Method: 使用全景视频相机作为唯一传感器；从全景视频中提取货架和天花板方向的校正图像序列；利用语义分割网络前端从每张图像中提取稀疏语义结构特征点（如货架角点、灯中心点）；跨序列跟踪这些特征点；结合曼哈顿网格等真实世界几何关系，使用受约束的结构运动恢复算法生成3D线框地图。

Result: 在包含46排货架（每排55m×7m）的仓库中完成验证；从一小时全景视频中为超过5000个货架元素生成线框地图；累计平均绝对误差为4.8cm。

Conclusion: SAVMap能够仅凭全景视频相机实现大规模仓库环境的高精度语义线框地图生成，具有良好的可扩展性和准确性，适用于机器人定位和数字孪生等应用。

Abstract: Precise 3D representations of industrial environments enable tasks such as robot localization and digital twin generation. We propose SAVMap, a method for generating a semantic wireframe map of warehouse shelf and light structures using only a panoramic video camera as the sensor input. Sequences of rectified images with shelf and ceiling-facing views are extracted from a panoramic video captured along the warehouse aisles. Using a semantic segmentation network front end, a set of sparse, semantic structure feature points (e.g., corners of shelf structures, centers of lights) are extracted from each image and tracked across the sequences. By accounting for real-world geometric relationships among the points such as Manhattan grids, a constrained structure-from-motion algorithm yields the 3D points that form a wireframe map. We demonstrate the scalability and accuracy of our proposal in a warehouse with 46 shelving rows, each with faces spanning 55\,m by 7\,m. From an hour of panoramic video content, we create wireframe maps for over 5000 shelf elements across the rows, achieving an aggregate mean absolute error of 4.8\,cm with respect to ground-truth.

</details>


### [220] [SCAPO: Self-Supervised Category-Level Articulated Pose Estimation from a Single 3D Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01940)
*Can Zhang,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCAPO是一个自监督框架，能从单张RGB-D观测中估计物体的规范几何、刚性部件分割、关节枢轴/轴和关节状态，无需真实标签或类别特定模型，通过SE(3)-等变自编码器、关节感知混合蒙皮模块和循环重建与跨空间对齐实现，在合成和真实数据集上优于所有自监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次3D观测下的类别级物体关节估计方法依赖密集监督、多帧输入或CAD模板，难以解耦几何与关节参数或恢复显式关节参数，亟需一种无需标注的自监督方法。

Method: 1) 使用SE(3)-等变向量神经元自编码器消除全局姿态，将不同实例对齐到共享规范空间；2) 在对齐形状上设计关节感知混合蒙皮模块建模部件运动；3) 通过观测形状与规范形状之间的循环重建以及可学习规范模板的跨空间对齐来学习表征，该模板解耦共享类别几何与实例特定残差形状。

Result: 在合成和真实铰接物体数据集上，SCAPO能够恢复一致的部件结构和准确的关节参数，且优于所有自监督基线方法。

Conclusion: SCAPO通过自监督方式有效解耦了几何与关节结构，实现了无需标注的单帧3D物体关节估计，为铰接物体理解提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing methods for category-level object articulation from a single 3D observation often rely on dense supervision, multi-frame inputs, or CAD templates, and still struggle to disentangle geometry from articulation or to recover explicit joint parameters. We propose SCAPO, a self-supervised framework that estimates canonical geometry, rigid part segmentation, and joint pivots, axes, and articulation states from a single RGB-D observation without ground-truth labels or category-specific models. Our SCAPO first uses an SE(3)-equivariant vector-neuron autoencoder to factor out global pose and align diverse instances into a shared canonical space. On this aligned shape, a joint-aware blend-skinning module is then designed to model part motion. We learn this representation through cycle reconstruction between observed and canonical shapes and cross-space alignment with a learnable canonical template that decouples shared category geometry from instance-specific residual shape. Experiments on synthetic and real articulated-object datasets show that our SCAPO recovers consistent part structure and accurate articulation parameters and outperforms all self-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [221] [Beyond Low-Rank: Low-Rank Sparse Prompting via Spiking Neural Network and Prompt Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01945)
*Yumiao Zhao,Bo Jiang,Beibei Wang,Xixi Wan,Xiao Wang,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoRSP是一种受大脑启发的新型视觉提示方法，利用脉冲神经元的稀疏放电机制生成低秩稀疏视觉提示，在多个视觉主干和基准上以更少可调参数实现了与现有方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉提示方法使用密集像素级提示，存在冗余扰动、泛化能力有限和能效低的问题。

Method: 通过低秩分解构建提示因子，输入脉冲神经网络进行整合-放电过程，生成实例自适应的稀疏视觉提示。

Result: 在五个异质视觉主干和多个基准上，以更少可调参数实现了与现有VP方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: LoRSP的稀疏低秩提示设计实现了更紧凑、鲁棒的跨任务自适应，验证了大脑启发式脉冲学习在视觉提示中的有效性。

Abstract: Visual Prompting (VP) has emerged as an efficient paradigm for adapting large-scale pre-trained vision models to downstream tasks by incorporating learnable prompts at the input level. However, existing VP methods typically employ dense pixel-level prompts, which often suffer from redundant perturbations, limited generalization and energy inefficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose to integrate brain-inspired spiking learning into visual prompt learning tasks. As we know that spiking neuron can perform inexpensive information processing by transmitting the input data into discrete spike trains and return sparse outputs. Inspired by this, we propose \textbf{Lo}w-\textbf{R}ank visual \textbf{S}pike \textbf{P}rompting (LoRSP), a novel framework that learns dynamic low-rank sparse visual prompts naturally via a Spiking neuron learning mechanism. The core idea of LoRSP is to exploit the brain-inspired sparse firing mechanism of spiking neurons to generate pixel-level sparse prompt for each instance. To be specific, we first construct a series of prompt factors via low-rank factorization to capture distinct prompt subspaces. These prompt factors are then fed into an SNN architecture, which performs the integrate-and-fire process to emit spikes. As a result, our LoRSP generates a \emph{sparse} visual prompt while maintaining the low-rank constraint. This design enables instance-specific selective prompting, leading to more compact and robust adaptation across diverse downstream tasks. Extensive experiments on five heterogeneous vision backbones and multiple benchmarks demonstrate that LoRSP achieves competitive performance while requiring fewer tunable parameters compared to existing VP methods.

</details>


### [222] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Pretrained Models for Instance Segmentation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01947)
*Nermeen Abou Baker,David Rohrschneider,Uwe Handmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地探索了参数高效微调方法（适配器和LoRA）在基于Transformer的实例分割模型中的应用，通过仅微调1-6%的参数即可达到与全量微调（40-55%参数）相竞争的性能，并发现每Transformer块使用2-3个适配器以及将LoRA应用于可变形注意力机制是最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型预训练模型在众多任务上取得了最优结果，但参数量的激增带来了训练效率问题，而参数高效微调方法在Transformer-based实例分割领域的研究尚属空白。

Method: 在两种Transformer模型上，针对四个基准数据集，研究了顺序排列的适配器模块和首次应用于可变形注意力的LoRA方法，通过仅微调1-6%的模型参数来评估PEFT方法的有效性。

Result: 仅微调1-6%参数即可达到竞争性能（传统方法需40-55%）；每Transformer块使用2-3个适配器可实现最佳效率与性能平衡；LoRA应用于可变形注意力展现出强参数效率，某些情况下甚至超越适配器配置；PEFT效果因数据集复杂度和模型架构而异。

Conclusion: PEFT方法在实例分割任务中具有实现可扩展、可定制且计算高效的迁移学习的潜力，且效果高度依赖于上下文特定的调优策略。

Abstract: Research and applications in artificial intelligence have recently shifted with the rise of large pretrained models, which deliver state-of-the-art results across numerous tasks. However, the substantial increase in parameters introduces a need for parameter-efficient training strategies. Despite significant advancements, limited research has explored parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods in the context of transformer-based models for instance segmentation. Addressing this gap, this study investigates the effectiveness of PEFT methods, specifically adapters and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), applied to two models across four benchmark datasets. Integrating sequentially arranged adapter modules and applying LoRA to deformable attention--explored here for the first time--achieves competitive performance while fine-tuning only about 1-6% of model parameters, a marked improvement over the 40-55% required in traditional fine-tuning. Key findings indicate that using 2-3 adapters per transformer block offers an optimal balance of performance and efficiency. Furthermore, LoRA, exhibits strong parameter efficiency when applied to deformable attention, and in certain cases surpasses adapter configurations. These results show that the impact of PEFT techniques varies based on dataset complexity and model architecture, underscoring the importance of context-specific tuning. Overall, this work demonstrates the potential of PEFT to enable scalable, customizable, and computationally efficient transfer learning for instance segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [223] [Contrastive Augmented Transformer with Domain-specific Enhancement for Robust Multi-scenario Metal Surface Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01962)
*Yiyao Liua,Wenxiao He,Liyuan Ren,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对比增强Transformer（CAT）框架用于金属表面缺陷检测，通过分层Swin Transformer骨干、重设计特征金字塔网络、域特定液滴增强算法和难负样本挖掘对比损失，在KolektorSDD2数据集上达到像素级AUROC 99.54%，并在三个未见数据集上展现优异泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属表面缺陷检测面临标注数据有限、微小多尺度缺陷难以识别、跨场景泛化能力差三大挑战，现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 采用分层Swin Transformer作为骨干网络；2) 重新设计特征金字塔网络，融合低层纹理与高层语义；3) 提出域特定液滴增强算法提升噪声鲁棒性；4) 在对比损失中引入难负样本挖掘策略增强判别能力。

Result: 在KolektorSDD2数据集上达到像素级AUROC 99.54%，超越现有方法；在KSDD1、MTD瓷砖缺陷、MSDD轨道表面缺陷三个未见数据集上表现优异。

Conclusion: CAT框架在金属表面缺陷检测任务上实现了最先进的性能，并展现了强大的泛化能力和鲁棒性，具有广泛的工业部署潜力。

Abstract: Metal surface defect detection is critical for maintaining product quality in industrial manufacturing. However, it faces significant challenges, including limited annotated data, difficulty in identifying subtle multi-scale defects, and poor generalization across diverse scenarios. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel Contrastive Augmented Transformer (CAT) framework for robust defect detection. CAT employs a hierarchical Swin Transformer backbone and redesigns the feature pyramid network to effectively fuse low-level textures with high-level semantics, enabling precise modeling of subtle and multi-scale defect patterns. To enhance robustness under real-world noise conditions, we propose a domain-specific droplet augmentation algorithm. Furthermore, we incorporate a hard negative mining strategy into the contrastive loss to strengthen the model's discrimination ability in ambiguous defect regions. Experimental results on the KolektorSDD2 dataset demonstrate that CAT achieves a pixel-level AUROC of 99.54%, outperforming existing methods. In addition, CAT exhibits superior generalization and robustness on three unseen datasets, including KSDD1, MTD for tile defects, and MSDD for rail surface defects, demonstrating its potential for wide-scale industrial deployment.

</details>


### [224] [Generalization Limits in Vehicle Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01981)
*Anis Yassine Ben Mabrouk,Antoine Tadros,Rafael Grompone von Gioi,Gabriele Facciolo,Axel Davy,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了车辆重识别数据集存在训练集与测试集包含高度相似车辆（相同品牌、型号、颜色）的问题，导致现有方法通过记忆训练数据获得高测试分数但泛化能力差。为此提出新的评估方法以衡量对未见车辆类型的泛化能力，并基于视角进行拆分评估，发现大多数最先进方法在未见车辆类型上表现不佳，且其视角鲁棒性和细节关注仅限于训练中见过的车辆类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆重识别数据集存在训练集和测试集中包含视觉差异极小的车辆（相同品牌、型号、颜色），导致模型通过记忆训练数据即可在测试集上取得高分，但泛化到其他数据集时性能严重下降。因此需要提出更有效的评估方法来衡量模型对未见车辆类型的泛化能力。

Method: 1. 提出一种新的评估方法，专注于衡量模型对未见车辆类型的泛化能力；2. 引入基于视角的拆分评估，区分视角鲁棒性与同视角重识别对性能的影响，以更细致地分析模型表现。

Result: 大多数最先进方法在未见车辆类型上表现不佳；模型对视角变化的鲁棒性和对细节的关注仅限于训练中见过的车辆类型，无法泛化到未见类型。

Conclusion: 现有方法在车辆重识别任务中过度依赖训练数据中的特定车辆类型记忆，缺乏对未见类型的泛化能力；所提出的评估方法能够更有效地区分模型的真实泛化能力，并为未来研究提供了新的评估基准。

Abstract: Vehicle re-identification focuses on retrieving images of the same vehicle from a gallery given a query image. Upon closer inspection of commonly used datasets, we observe that vehicles with few visual differences-e.g., the same make, model, and color-appear in both the training and test sets. As a result, methods that effectively memorize the training data tend to perform well on these test sets but struggle to generalize to other datasets. In this paper, we address this issue by proposing a novel evaluation approach that more effectively measures generalization capability to unseen vehicle types. To further study generalization performance, we also propose splitting the evaluation based on view, allowing us to differentiate the effect of viewpoint robustness from that of same-view re-identification. Our findings reveal that most state-of-the-art methods struggle with unseen vehicle types, and that their robustness to viewpoint changes and attention to detail are limited to vehicle types seen during training.

</details>


### [225] [MT-EditFlow: Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Image Editing with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01985)
*Jiahui Huang,Yasi Zhang,Tianyu Chen,Shu Wang,Jianwen Xie,Oscar Leong,Mingyuan Zhou,Nanzhu Wang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-EditFlow，一种基于流匹配的强化学习框架，通过多轮视角和多奖励公式优化序列图像编辑的奖励信号，有效解决了多轮编辑中的错误传播和暴露偏差问题，在多个基础模型上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑模型主要针对单轮编辑训练，在多轮迭代编辑场景中会出现"全有或全无"的失败（单次失败导致整个序列失效）以及暴露偏差导致的错误累积问题，亟需一种能够优化序列编辑奖励信号的方法。

Method: MT-EditFlow采用流匹配强化学习框架，集成多轮视角与多奖励公式，统一适用于GRPO和基于NFT的强化学习方法。通过优化回合级聚合策略、VLM推理模式（权衡奖励偏差与方差）以及优势融合层级（防止奖励黑客），并提出将聚合优势广播到整个编辑轨迹以弥合局部规划与全局任务成功之间的差距。

Result: 在多个基础模型上显著提升多轮编辑性能，其中FLUX.1-Kontext-dev在第三轮总体性能提升6.85分，超越Qwen-Image-Edit等最先进开源模型，同时保持高边际成功率并降低暴露偏差。

Conclusion: MT-EditFlow通过优化序列级奖励信号，有效解决了多轮图像编辑中的错误传播问题，为人机协作视觉内容创作提供了更可靠的基础。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in instruction-based image editing have captured significant attention, as models are now capable of handling real-world editing demands with the practicality required by everyday users. However, editing models trained primarily for single-turn edits often break down in multi-turn editing--the natural interactive setting where a user iteratively refines an image based on the model's own previous outputs. This failure stems from the all-or-nothing requirement, where a single failed turn compromises the entire sequence, and error propagation, where exposure bias leads to compounding editing errors. To address these challenges, we introduce MT-EditFlow, a flow-matching reinforcement learning framework designed to optimize reward signals for sequential image editing. MT-EditFlow integrates a multi-turn perspective with a multi-reward formulation to provide a unified structure applicable to both GRPO and NFT-based reinforcement learning methods. We systematically analyze and optimize the reward signal by investigating effective scoring strategies for turn-level aggregation, VLM reasoning modes to trade off reward bias and variance, and advantage fusion levels to prevent reward hacking. Our findings reveal that broadcasting the aggregated advantage across the entire editing trajectory effectively bridges the gap between local planning and global multi-turn task success. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MT-EditFlow significantly improves performance across diverse base models. Notably, it boosts FLUX.1-Kontext-dev by 6.85 points in turn-3 overall performance, surpassing state-of-the-art open-source models such as Qwen-Image-Edit. By maintaining high marginal success rates and reducing exposure bias, MT-EditFlow provides a foundation for more reliable and natural human-AI collaboration in visual content creation.

</details>


### [226] [A Structured Benchmark for Text-Guided Anomaly Detection: When Language Stops Conditioning the Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01992)
*Stefano Samele,Eugenio Lomurno,Teodora Jovanovic,Sanjay Shivakumar Manohar,Alberto Crivellaro,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出文本引导异常检测（TGAD）基准，通过渐进增加语言功能角色的三个场景（提示灵敏度、部件级约束、实际工业装配面板），系统评估多模态异常检测模型。结果表明，当前模型仅在表面上受文本条件影响：移除关键名词时生成模型性能骤降（97.4→82.6），部件级指令无法约束决策（90.3→66.3），在真实场景中图像级辨别力甚至低于随机水平（31.5）。标准基准夸大了文本引导能力，此类协议是工业部署中可靠语言控制的前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测的多模态模型虽声称支持文本引导的零样本和少样本检测，但其评估协议沿用单模态基准（保持文本条件恒定），无法测量语言是否真正影响决策。报告的性能提升可能源于强大的预训练视觉特征而非文本引导，缺乏可靠的评估方法。

Method: 构建TGAD基准，逐步增加语言功能角色：场景一为MVTec AD上的受控提示灵敏度测试；场景二为部件标注版MVTec AD，要求模型仅评估指定部件；场景三为新型Assembled Panel Dataset (APD)，模拟需要同时具备缺陷类型和部件位置知识的真实工业环境。评估三类代表性模型：生成式大视觉语言模型、免训练判别模型和嵌入自适应判别模型。

Result: 所有三类模型中，文本接口对决策的影响仅停留在表面：1）生成模型在移除物体名词后I-AUROC从97.4降至82.6；2）部件级指令下，允许部件外缺陷被当作正常时AUROC从90.3降至66.3；3）APD上图像级判别性能崩溃，低于MVTec水平（71.2, 50.5, 31.5），其中一种情况低于随机水平。

Conclusion: 标准基准高估了当前多模态异常检测系统的文本引导能力，这些系统无法通过语言进行可靠控制。所提出的渐进式评估协议是验证模型是否真正利用文本条件、能否满足工业部署要求的必要前提。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection has historically been a unimodal task. Recent multimodal vision-language models have produced systems that admit textual input alongside the image and are presented as enabling text-guided zero- and few-shot inspection. Yet these methods are evaluated with protocols inherited from unimodal benchmarks that hold the textual condition constant and therefore cannot measure whether language conditions the decision; whether reported gains reflect text guidance or strong pretrained visual features remains open. We introduce Text-Guided Anomaly Detection (TGAD), a structured benchmark that progressively increases the functional role of language across three scenarios: a controlled prompt-sensitivity setting on MVTec AD; a component-tagged extension of MVTec AD that requires the model to restrict its assessment to an instructed part; and the new Assembled Panel Dataset (APD), a realistic industrial setting that requires both defect-type and component-location knowledge. We evaluate one representative model per paradigm: generative large vision-language, training-free discriminative, and embedding-adaptive discriminative. In all three, the textual interface conditions the decision only superficially: prompt content is absorbed unless the object noun is removed (the generative model's I-AUROC drops from 97.4 to 82.6); component-level instructions do not constrain the decision once defects outside the instructed part are admitted as normal (from 90.3 to 66.3); and when both combine on APD, image-level discrimination collapses below the MVTec level, in one case below chance (71.2, 50.5, 31.5). These results suggest that standard benchmarks overstate the text-guided capabilities of current multimodal anomaly detection systems, and that a protocol of this kind is a prerequisite for models that can be reliably controlled through language for industrial deployment.

</details>


### [227] [Towards 3D-Aware Video Diffusion Models: Render-Free Human Motion Control with Mesh Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02000)
*Jingyun Liang,Min Wei,Shikai Li,Yizeng Han,Hangjie Yuan,Lei Sun,Weihua Chen,Fan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D人体网格令牌化的无渲染框架，将压缩后的3D人体网格令牌与视频令牌在DiT架构中联合处理，使视频扩散模型能直接感知3D几何结构、相机视角和场景上下文，在人体运动控制任务上取得优于传统2D引导方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视频扩散模型是否真正理解底层3D结构，而非仅复现合理的2D投影。现有方法依赖渲染的2D运动引导视频，存在视角依赖伪影和轨迹-姿态不匹配等问题。

Method: 提出无渲染框架，将3D人体网格压缩为离散令牌，与视频令牌在DiT（Diffusion Transformer）架构中进行联合建模，使模型在视频生成过程中同时推理外观、3D结构和相机视角。

Result: 在人体运动控制基准上取得强性能，减少了由视角依赖的2D引导和轨迹-姿态不匹配导致的伪影。

Conclusion: 配备网格令牌化的视频扩散模型能够更好地捕捉复杂3D人体结构及其与环境交互的信息，验证了模型具备3D结构感知能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown remarkable success in video generation. However, whether such models are truly aware of the 3D structure underlying visual observations, rather than simply reproducing plausible 2D projections, remains an open question. In this work, we investigate this question through human motion control, a task that requires precise modelling of 3D human geometry, motion, camera viewpoint, and scene context. Unlike prior methods that rely on rendered 2D motion guidance videos, we propose a render-free framework that conditions video generation directly on compressed 3D human mesh tokens. This representation preserves full 3D geometric information while enabling a unified token-based generation pipeline that processes video tokens jointly with motion tokens in a DiT-based architecture. This design requires the model to reason jointly about appearance, 3D structure, and camera viewpoint during video generation. Experimental results demonstrate strong performance on human motion control benchmarks, while reducing artifacts induced by view-dependent 2D guidance and trajectory-pose mismatches during editing. These findings suggest that video diffusion models, when equipped with mesh tokenization, can better capture complex 3D human structures and their interactions with the surrounding environment.

</details>


### [228] [Distortion-Aware Fusion of Statistical and Vision-Language Features for Blind Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02002)
*Bishr Omer Abdelrahman Adam,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种失真感知融合框架，通过乘法门控机制动态整合NSS特征（138维）与两种VLM嵌入（SigLIP和CLIP-H），无需微调VLM主干，在三个标准BIQA基准上取得优异表现，SROCC分别达0.9142（KonIQ-10k）、0.9715（KADID-10k）和0.8527（LIVE Challenge）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典NSS描述符与现代VLM嵌入从根本不同的角度处理BIQA问题，但两者结合是否能产生互补效益以及如何根据输入图像为其贡献加权尚未被探索。

Method: 提出失真感知融合框架，集成138维NSS描述符与SigLIP和CLIP-H两种VLM嵌入，通过乘法门控机制学习基于输入内容的流权重。采用混合损失（MSE、PLCC和成对排序目标），无需端到端微调VLM主干。

Result: KonIQ-10k: SROCC=0.9142, PLCC=0.9279；KADID-10k: SROCC=0.9715, PLCC=0.9733（超越最新SOTA）；LIVE Challenge: SROCC=0.8527, PLCC=0.8802。门控权重与独立消融实验测量的每失真NSS贡献正相关（rho=0.33）。

Conclusion: NSS特征在噪声和色偏等直接影响像素统计的失真上贡献最大，在色彩饱和度变化等感知失真上贡献最小。模型自动发现与手动分析一致的失真-流亲和模式，验证了门控机制的有效性。

Abstract: Blind image quality assessment (BIQA) aims to predict perceived image quality without access to a reference image. Classical natural scene statistics (NSS) descriptors and modern vision-language model (VLM) embeddings address this problem from fundamentally different perspectives, yet whether combining them yields complementary benefits and how to weight their contributions per input image remains unexplored. We propose a distortion-aware fusion framework that integrates a 138-dimensional NSS descriptor with two complementary VLM embeddings, SigLIP and CLIP-H, through a multiplicative gating mechanism that learns per-input stream weights conditioned on image content. Unlike static concatenation fusion, the proposed gating network suppresses or amplifies each stream's contribution based on the input, producing weights that correlate positively (Spearman rank correlation rho=0.33) with the per-distortion NSS contribution measured by independent ablation on KADID-10k. The framework requires no end-to-end fine-tuning of the VLM backbones and is trained with a hybrid loss combining mean squared error, Pearson linear correlation, and pairwise ranking objectives. We evaluate on three standard benchmarks: KonIQ-10k (SROCC=0.9142, PLCC=0.9279), KADID-10k (SROCC=0.9715, PLCC=0.9733, surpassing recent state-of-the-art methods), and LIVE Challenge in-the-Wild (SROCC=0.8527, PLCC=0.8802 with cross-dataset pretraining and fine-tuning). A per-distortion analysis on KADID-10k reveals that NSS features contribute most on noise and color-shift distortions where pixel statistics are directly affected, and least on perceptual distortions such as color saturation changes. The learned gate values validate these findings, confirming that the model autonomously discovers distortion-stream affinity patterns consistent with the manual per-distortion study.

</details>


### [229] [PerBite: A Curated Diagnostic Workflow for Bite-Aware Food Volume Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02021)
*Ahmad AlMughrabi,Farid Al-Areqi,David Fernández Gómez,Umair Haroon,Marc Bolaños,Ricardo Marques,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前后餐食状态配对的3D食物重建与体积估算方法，在CVPR 2026 MetaFood挑战中排名第一，平均Chamfer距离8.31，状态级体积MAPE为33.87%，消耗体积MAPE为53.74%，并强调了各环节（重建、尺度、网格清理、体积集成、物理消耗一致性）应独立评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉上合理的食物网格是否可以被信任用于估算实际消耗的食物体积，即验证3D食物重建在膳食评估中的可靠性。

Method: 采用配对的前后餐食状态，流程包括：SAM 3分割食物与餐盘区域；Hunyuan3D/SAM 3D生成无量纲食物网格；以餐盘直径提供公制尺度；在Blender中去除餐盘几何；对剩余网格进行补洞、水密化处理并积分估算体积。MoGe-2仅作为辅助线索用于初始餐盘直径估计。

Result: 在挑战中排名第一，34个网格平均Chamfer距离8.31（刚性ICP无尺度校正）；17个前后配对状态级体积MAPE为33.87%，零单调性违规；消耗体积MAPE为53.74%。

Conclusion: 表面重建、公制尺度、网格清理、水密体积积分及物理消耗一致性应分别独立评估，以提升膳食评估的可信度。

Abstract: Can a visually plausible food mesh be trusted to estimate the volume of consumed food? \method investigates this question using selected paired before- and after-consumption states from the MetaFood CVPR 2026 Continuous 3D Reconstruction While Eating Challenge. The submitted workflow follows a curated reconstruction protocol: SAM~3 segments the food and plate regions; Hunyuan3D/SAM~3D generates a dimensionless food mesh; the plate diameter provides the metric scale; the plate geometry is removed in Blender; and the remaining mesh is hole-filled, made watertight, and integrated to estimate volume. MoGe-2 is used only as an auxiliary cue for initial dish-diameter estimation when direct plate measurement is uncertain; it is not the primary scale source for the reported challenge result. \method ranks first, with an average Chamfer distance of 8.31 across 34 meshes using rigid ICP without scale correction. On 17 before- and after-pairs, it achieves 33.87\% state-level volume MAPE and zero monotonicity violations, while consumed-volume MAPE remains 53.74\%. The results show that surface reconstruction, metric scale, controlled mesh cleanup, watertight volume integration, and physical depletion consistency should be evaluated separately for dietary assessment. Source code and evaluation scripts will be available at \href{https://github.com/GCVCG/PerBite-CVPR-MetaFood-2026}{github.com/GCVCG/PerBite-CVPR-MetaFood-2026}.

</details>


### [230] [Ranking vs. Assignment: The Metric Mismatch in Multi-View Object Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02022)
*Matvei Shelukhan,Timur Mamedov,Aleksandr Chukhrov,Karina Kvanchiani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多视角目标关联任务中常用的排序评估指标（AP、FPR-95）与真实一对一匹配目标之间存在根本性不匹配，理论上证明这些指标在分配正确时仍可能不完美，而基于Sinkhorn的归一化可使其完美；反之，最优排序仍可能导致错误分配。实验表明优化后处理参数可显著提升AP/FPR-95，但对分配级指标（ACC、IPAA）无帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作依赖AP和FPR-95等成对排序指标评估多视角目标关联模型，但这些指标与任务本质的一对一匹配目标存在根本性不匹配，需要理论分析和实证验证这种不匹配。

Method: 理论分析证明AP/FPR-95与分配目标的不匹配性；提出基于Sinkhorn的归一化作为受控后处理压力测试；通过优化少量后处理参数观察AP/FPR-95提升而分配级指标不变。

Result: AP和FPR-95即使分配正确也可能不完美；Sinkhorn归一化可使其完美；最优成对排序仍可能导致错误分配；优化后处理参数显著提升AP/FPR-95但对ACC和IPAA无改善。

Conclusion: 当前广泛使用的排序指标与多视角目标关联的分配目标不匹配，仅依赖这些指标可能导致模型评估偏差，应同时关注分配级指标。

Abstract: Multi-view object association is an important computer vision problem that underlies many multi-camera perception tasks. While this task is naturally formulated as a constrained one-to-one matching problem, recent works heavily rely on pairwise ranking metrics like AP and FPR-95 for model evaluation. We highlight a fundamental mismatch between these metrics and the actual assignment objective. Theoretically, we show that AP and FPR-95 can be imperfect even when the assignment is already correct, and that Sinkhorn-based normalization can make them perfect. Conversely, optimal pairwise ranking can still lead to incorrect assignments. We validate this mismatch in practice by using our Sinkhorn-based normalization as a controlled post-processing stress test. We show that optimizing just a few post-processing parameters significantly boosts AP and FPR-95 without corresponding improvements in assignment-level metrics such as ACC and IPAA.

</details>


### [231] [Normality-Preserving Continual Industrial Anomaly Detection via Orthogonal LoRA Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02042)
*Weibai Fang,Haijun Che,Feiyang Ren,Qiancheng Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于历史冻结正交LoRA库（HF-OLB）和层次化新颖性自适应库增长（HNABG）的持续工业异常检测框架，通过在正交补空间中约束新任务残差来保护历史正常性先验，在MVTec和VisA数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续扩散方法依赖重放或约束优化来保留旧知识，但缺乏显式机制来隔离和保护类别特定的正常性先验；标准LoRA既不能冻结历史正常性子空间，也无法防止新适配器干扰旧适配器。

Method: 提出HF-OLB模块，冻结预训练U-Net骨干和已学LoRA库，将新任务残差约束到历史LoRA子空间的正交补空间中；HNABG模块按层分配残差容量，仅在残差正常性新颖性超出已有库表达能力时才扩展库。

Result: 在VisA 2x6设定下，图像级和像素级A-AUROC分别达83.6/91.8，FM为3.8/3.9；像素级A-AUROC比SOTA提升3.2点，像素级FM降低1.3。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效在长周期持续类别序列中保护历史正常性先验，且优于现有持续异常检测方法。

Abstract: Continual industrial anomaly detection with diffusion models suffers from historical normality prior drift and catastrophic forgetting. Existing continual diffusion methods preserve previous knowledge through replay or constrained optimization, but they lack an explicit mechanism for isolating and protecting category-specific normality priors during sequential adaptation. Although low-rank adaptation provides modular residual updates, standard LoRA neither freezes historical normality subspaces nor prevents new adapters from interfering with previous ones. To address this issue, we propose a normality-preserving continual anomaly detection framework based on two modules: History Frozen Orthogonal LoRA Bank (HF-OLB) and Hierarchical Novelty Adaptive Bank Growth module (HNABG). HF-OLB freezes both the pre-trained U-Net backbone and the learned LoRA banks, and constrains new task-specific normality residuals to the orthogonal complement of historical LoRA subspaces. HNABG further allocates layer-dependent residual capacity and expands the bank only when the residual normality novelty exceeds the expressive capacity of existing banks. Extensive experiments on MVTec and VisA demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. On the challenging VisA 2x6 setting, our method achieves 83.6/91.8 image and pixel level A-AUROC with 3.8/3.9 FM, improving pixel level A-AUROC over the state of the art by 3.2 points while reducing pixel level FM by 1.3. These results show that our method effectively preserves historical normality priors in long horizon continual category sequences.

</details>


### [232] [Attention mechanisms and transfer learning for robust peach leaf damage classification under domain shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02045)
*Adrián Cánovas-Rodriguez,Miguel A. González-Illán,Maria Fernanda García-Cruz,Pedro Nortes Tortosa,José Salvador Rubio-Asensio,Miguel A. Zamora Izquierdo,Juan Antonio Martínez Navarro,Antonio F. Skarmeta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度学习的桃树叶部损伤分类方法，创建了包含1,366张图像、6个类别的基准数据集。EfficientNet系列表现优异，其中EfficientNetB5结合CBAM注意力模块达到最高准确率93.3%。通过迁移学习在本地数据集（180张图像、4个类别）上进一步验证，EfficientNetB3+CBAM获得93%的宏F1分数，证明了模型在不同田间条件下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧了桃园非生物胁迫和生物胁迫（病虫害），这些胁迫常产生视觉上相似的叶片症状，人工诊断困难且难以跨田地泛化，因此需要具备强泛化能力的自动化模型进行作物损伤评估。

Method: 1) 创建了包含1,366张桃叶图像、6个损伤类别的基准数据集；2) 评估多种深度学习架构（EfficientNetB0/B3/B5、DenseNet121、InceptionV3等）；3) 集成CBAM注意力模块进行性能增强；4) 收集180张图像、4个类别的本地数据集，采用三种微调策略进行迁移学习以解决域偏移问题。

Result: EfficientNetB0准确率92.9%，EfficientNetB3为91.5%，DenseNet121为92.6%，CBAM增强的EfficientNetB5达到最佳93.3%准确率。迁移学习后，EfficientNetB3+CBAM在本地数据集上获得93%的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的模型对少数类别更具鲁棒性，且在不同田间条件下具有更好的泛化能力，证明了深度学习和迁移学习在桃树叶片损伤自动检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence provides a practical framework for crop damage assessment from imagery data, supporting early decision-making in agricultural management. In peach orchards, climate change increases abiotic stress and biotic pressures, including pests and diseases, which often produce visually similar foliar symptoms. This overlap makes manual diagnosis difficult, especially across multiple fields with varying environmental conditions, highlighting the need for automated models with strong generalization ability.
  We propose an image-based classification approach for peach leaf damage detection. A benchmark dataset was created through manual annotation of publicly available images, consisting of 1,366 peach leaves across six damage categories. Several deep learning architectures were evaluated. EfficientNet models achieved the best results, with EfficientNetB0 reaching 92.9 percent accuracy, EfficientNetB3 achieving 91.5 percent, and EfficientNetB5 showing the strongest performance on minority classes. DenseNet121 reached 92.6 percent accuracy. The integration of the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) improved performance in several backbones, particularly EfficientNetB5 and InceptionV3, while showing limited or negative impact in others. The CBAM-enhanced EfficientNetB5 achieved the best overall accuracy of 93.3 percent.
  To evaluate robustness under realistic conditions, a local dataset of 180 images across four classes was collected, and transfer learning strategies were applied to address domain shift. Three fine-tuning strategies were tested. EfficientNetB3 combined with CBAM achieved the best performance in the local domain, reaching a 93 percent macro F1-score after transfer. Overall, attention-based models showed improved robustness for minority classes and better generalization across different field conditions.

</details>


### [233] [TIDES: Time-Derivative Event Simulation via Deformable Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02058)
*Christopher Thirgood,Dipon Kumar Ghosh,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIDES是一种基于动态高斯溅射的连续时间事件模拟器，通过显式3D场景表示和逐像素强度动力学建模，避免了传统方法中的时间戳批处理问题，并模拟了有限传感器带宽的伪影，在事件流保真度和下游任务迁移效果上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机模拟器大多从帧序列推断事件时间戳，导致多个阈值跨越共享离散时间点（时间戳批处理问题），在快速运动和遮挡场景下尤为严重，且缺乏真实传感器带宽模型。

Method: 基于动态高斯溅射构建连续时间事件模拟器，从显式3D场景表示中直接推导逐像素强度动态，实现精确的阈值跨越预测（单渲染步内可多次跨越）；利用场景模型检测遮挡区域，引导自适应时间步进；引入瓦片级仲裁器模型有限传感器带宽，模拟吞吐量、抖动和事件丢失。

Result: 在RGB-事件配对基准上，TIDES的事件流保真度达到最优；模拟的事件在真实下游任务中的迁移效果优于对比方法。

Conclusion: TIDES通过连续时间建模和显式3D场景表示有效解决了时间戳批处理问题，结合传感器带宽建模提升了事件模拟的真实性和下游任务适用性。

Abstract: Event cameras emit asynchronous events in response to environmental appearance changes. The scarcity of real-world event datasets makes simulation essential. However, most simulators infer event timestamps from frame sequences, forcing many threshold crossings to share a small set of discrete times; a failure mode we term timestamp batching that worsens under fast motion and occlusion.
  We present TIDES, a continuous-time event simulator built on dynamic Gaussian splatting. Because TIDES operates on an explicit 3D scene representation with learnt geometry and motion, it can derive per-pixel intensity dynamics directly from the scene, rather than by differencing rendered frames. This enables accurate threshold-crossing prediction, including multiple crossings per rendering step, without temporal upsampling or frame interpolation. The same 3D scene model reveals where objects partially occlude one another; TIDES uses this to guide adaptive time stepping, concentrating computation only in regions where occlusion dynamics make simple models of brightness change unreliable.
  Finally, we model finite sensor bandwidth using a tile-level arbiter whose throughput, jitter, and event drops reproduce realistic sensor artifacts. Across paired RGB-event benchmarks, TIDES attains state-of-the-art event-stream fidelity. We also show that events simulated by TIDES transfer more effectively to real downstream tasks than competitors'.

</details>


### [234] [Fast and Lightweight Novel View Synthesis with Differentiable Multiplane Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02068)
*Kaidi Zhang,Guanxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多平面图像(MPI)的高效新视角合成方法，利用视觉基础模型预测点图进行几何初始化，并通过一步扩散解决稀疏初始化中的空洞和伪影问题，在保持合成质量的同时，相比3DGS方法速度提升30.7%，模型大小仅为其14.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法(NeRF/3DGS)在渲染速度、模型大小、优化时间和稀疏视图条件下表现不佳，且前馈重建方法产生数百万高斯体不适合移动端部署。

Method: 基于MPI表示，利用视觉基础模型预测点图进行几何初始化，通过可微优化更新MPI，并引入一步扩散同时参与可微优化和渲染后处理以修复空洞和伪影。

Result: 相比基于3DGS的代表方法，速度提升30.7%，模型大小仅为其14.8%，在前视图场景下合成质量具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 重新审视的MPI表示结合现代视觉基础模型和扩散技术，能够在保持高效渲染的同时显著降低模型大小和优化时间，适用于移动端部署。

Abstract: Recently, novel view synthesis has witnessed remarkable progress, with mainstream methods such as Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) delivering impressive results. However, these approaches often struggle to balance rendering speed and model size, and their optimization-based training can be highly time-consuming. Furthermore, they typically rely on dense observations, often failing to produce satisfactory results under sparse-view conditions. Although feed-forward reconstruction significantly reduces the optimization time of 3DGS, its pixel-aligned formulation generates millions of Gaussians from a single image, severely limiting its practical deployment on mobile devices. To address these limitations, we revisit the Multiplane Image(MPI) representation, which represents scenes using a compact set of planar layers for efficient novel view synthesis. Leveraging recent advances in visual foundation models, we utilize predicted point maps for reliable geometric initialization, followed by differentiable optimization. To address the issues of holes and artifacts in sparsely initialized MPI, we introduce one-step diffusion, which participates in both the differentiable optimization of MPI and the postprocessing of rendering results. Compared with a representative GS-based method, our approach is 30.7% faster and uses only 14.8% of its model size, while achieving competitive synthesis quality on front-view scenarios

</details>


### [235] [FACT: A Simple and Efficient Framework for Active Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02079)
*Wenshuai Xu,You Song,Yuzhuo Cui,Minjie Ren,Qingjie Liu,Zhenghui Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FACT框架，一种面向主动微调场景的三阶段层次化微调方法，通过系统探索微调方法而非仅关注数据选择，在多个数据集和模型上取得显著性能提升，尤其在低采样率下ViT模型性能提升超20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动微调研究过度聚焦于数据选择（主动方面），而统一使用全参数微调进行模型适配，这不可避免地因分布偏移扭曲预训练特征，尤其在模型规模相对于微调数据量较大时，过拟合风险加剧。本文旨在系统性地探索主动学习中的微调方法，填补这一关键空白。

Method: 提出FACT框架，一种三阶段层次化微调框架，兼具高效性和简洁性。实验涵盖三类数据集（经典、长尾、细粒度）、三种预训练架构（ConvNeXt、ViT、ViL），并系统研究了冻结特征增强（FroFA）策略。

Result: 在低采样率下，ViT模型在CIFAR10、CIFAR100和ImageNet-1k基准上实现超过20%的性能提升；整体展现了强泛化性和鲁棒性，建立了新的最先进性能标准，同时保持参数效率。

Conclusion: 该系统性方法在参数效率方面建立了新的最先进性能，在标注数据稀缺时尤为有效，证明了探索主动学习中微调方法的重要性。

Abstract: The main goal of active finetuning is to improve a pretrained model's performance on a specific task or domain by finetuning it with carefully selected informative or challenging data. Previous research has predominantly focused on the active aspect (i.e., data selection) while uniformly employing full finetuning for model adaptation, which inevitably distorts pretrained features due to distribution shift. This issue becomes particularly pronounced when the model size is large relative to the finetuning data quantity, leading to heightened overfitting risks. To address this critical gap, we formally outline the FiAF task that emphasizes systematic exploration of finetuning methodologies in active learning. We propose FACT, a three-phase hierarchical finetuning framework featuring both efficiency and simplicity, specifically designed for active finetuning scenarios. Our comprehensive experiments span: (1) Three major dataset categories encompassing classic (CIFAR10, CIFAR100, ImageNet-1k), imbalanced (CIFAR10-LT, CIFAR100-LT), and fine-grained (StanfordCars, FGVCAircraft) image classification datasets, each evaluated under 3-5 distinct sampling ratios; (2) Diverse pretrained architectures including Convolutional Neural Network (ConvNeXt), Vision Transformer (ViT), and Vision LSTM (ViL) networks; (3) A systematic investigation of frozen feature augmentation (FroFA) strategies. (4) A comprehensive and rigorous analysis of efficiency and generalizability. The results demonstrate significant improvements with strong generalization and robustness. Notably, under low sampling ratios, our framework achieves remarkable performance gains of over 20% on the ViT model for CIFAR10, CIFAR100, and ImageNet-1k benchmarks. This systematic approach establishes new state-of-the-art performance while maintaining parameter efficiency, proving particularly effective when labeled data is scarce.

</details>


### [236] [FocusDiT: Masking Queries in Diffusion Transformers for Fine-grained Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02090)
*Xueji Fang,Liyuan Ma,Jianhao Zeng,Jinjin Cao,Mingyuan Zhou,Guo-Jun Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusDiT通过对关键查询token进行掩码处理，使其仅通过FFN层检索视觉语义知识，从而增强扩散Transformer在细粒度图像生成中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）中FFN层充当解码视觉内容的键值词典，但并非所有查询token对生成质量贡献相同。作者发现，聚焦于对应复杂细节的关键查询token并鼓励模型改进这些token，对于细粒度视觉生成至关重要。

Method: 提出FocusDiT，设计一种掩码方案（Masking scheme），将关键查询token单独送入FFN层，被掩码的查询从FFN词汇表中检索视觉token以解码其视觉细节，而其他token则正常经过注意力和FFN层。

Result: 在文本到图像生成任务上的大量实验验证了token掩码策略在提升生成性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FocusDiT通过选择性掩码机制，让更重要的查询token专注于从FFN中检索视觉语义信息，有效提升了细粒度视觉生成的质量。

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) has been widely adopted in the generative diffusion field, advancing the denoising of query tokens through attention and Feed-Forward (\text{FFN}) layers. FFN actually acts as the key-value vocabulary for decoding visual contents where the value embeds the visual semantical knowledge. We present that focusing on critical query tokens corresponding to more complex details and encouraging the model to improve these tokens is essential for fine-grained visual generation. To this end, we propose FocusDiT, which applies a Masking scheme to focus on critical query tokens that are exclusively fed into FFN. The masked queries can retrieve visual tokens from the FFN vocabularies, and use them to decode their visual details. Extensive text-to-image experiments validate the effectiveness of token masking in enhancing generative performance.

</details>


### [237] [WebSpline: Structure-Informed Splines for Real-Time 3D Gaussians from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02096)
*Jongmin Park,Jeonghwan Yun,Minh-Quan Viet Bui,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WebSpline提出了一种基于结构信息样条（SIS）表示的动态3D高斯框架，利用结构代理图（SPG）初始化并通过两阶段优化，从单目视频中实现结构连贯且高保真的动态场景重建，渲染速度比第二名方法WorldTree快10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在有限的视图线索下难以平衡全局结构连贯性和局部细节，导致单目视频动态场景重建质量不佳。

Method: 提出WebSpline框架，核心为结构信息样条（SIS）表示，利用可学习的三次埃尔米特样条建模每个动态高斯轨迹，并通过结构代理图（SPG）组织运动结构。两阶段优化：第一阶段从2D点轨迹初始化SPG，使用时序刚性正则化增强结构连贯性；第二阶段从SPG初始化SIS，在空间和结构邻域约束下优化。推理时仅需评估学习到的SIS即可获得高斯运动。

Result: 在iPhone和NVIDIA两个挑战性单目动态场景基准上，WebSpline达到最先进的渲染质量，同时在iPhone数据集上渲染速度比第二名方法WorldTree快10倍以上。

Conclusion: WebSpline通过引入结构信息样条和结构代理图，有效解决了单目视频动态场景重建中结构连贯性与细节保真度的平衡问题，同时实现了快速渲染。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular videos remains highly challenging, as existing methods often struggle to balance global structural coherence and local fine-grained details under limited multi-view cues. To address this challenge, we propose WebSpline, a novel dynamic 3D Gaussian framework that enables structurally coherent and high-fidelity reconstruction from monocular videos with fast rendering. The core of WebSpline is the Structure-Informed Spline (SIS) representation, which models each dynamic Gaussian trajectory using a learnable cubic Hermite spline whose motion is structurally organized with an auxiliary Structural Proxy Graph (SPG). The proposed framework is optimized in two stages: (i) in the first stage, the SPG is initialized from 2D point tracks and refined with temporal rigidity regularization to establish structural coherence for moving objects across the sequence; and (ii) in the second stage, the SIS representation is initialized from the refined SPG and optimized under both spatial and structural neighborhood constraints. At inference, Gaussian motion is obtained solely by evaluating the learned SIS, enabling fast rendering. Extensive experiments on the challenging monocular dynamic scene benchmarks, iPhone and NVIDIA, demonstrate that our WebSpline achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while rendering over 10 times faster than WorldTree, the second-best method on the iPhone dataset.

</details>


### [238] [Multimodal Action Diffusion for Robust End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02105)
*Jorge Daniel Rodríguez-Vidal,Diego Porres,Gabriel Villalonga Pineda,Antonio M. López Peña*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ADT（Action Diffusion Transformer），一种基于扩散Transformer的无锚点端到端驾驶模型，直接预测多模态驾驶动作（油门、转向、刹车），通过Nearest Neighbour Matching选出最优动作。在Bench2Drive benchmark上以10倍更低延迟超越SOTA，证明了动作多模态性对驾驶性能、表征质量和训练稳定性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶系统大多预测中间轨迹路点，依赖带GPS的手工控制器。直接控制信号预测（端到端输出油门、转向、刹车）研究不足，且动作多模态性的作用未被充分理解。作者认为超越确定性单动作输出不仅是建模选择，更是提升驾驶性能、表征质量和训练稳定性的关键。

Method: 提出ADT（Action Diffusion Transformer），一种无锚点的扩散Transformer模型，使用MSE损失训练，天然建模合理驾驶动作的多模态分布。推理时生成K个动作候选，通过Nearest Neighbour Matching (NNM) 选出最合适的动作。

Result: 在闭环Bench2Drive benchmark上超越之前SOTA，同时实现10倍更低的延迟。动作多模态性在学习表征和行为一致性方面带来可衡量的收益，这是确定性架构无法复现的。

Conclusion: 表达性的多模态动作建模对于鲁棒的端到端驾驶既是实际高效的，也是概念上必不可少的。

Abstract: End-to-End Autonomous Driving (E2E-AD) systems have largely converged on predicting intermediate trajectory waypoints, delegating final control to hand-crafted controllers with GPS access. Direct control-signal prediction (outputting throttle, steer and brake in an end-to-end fashion) remains underexplored, and critically, the role of action multimodality in such systems is not well understood. We argue that moving beyond deterministic, single-action outputs is not merely a modelling choice, but a key driver of driving performance, representational quality, and training stability. To validate this, we introduce the Action Diffusion Transformer (ADT), an anchor-free diffusion transformer trained with a MSE objective that natively models the multimodal distribution of plausible driving actions. Rather than committing to a single deterministic command, ADT generates K action candidates and selects the most suitable one at inference via Nearest Neighbour Matching (NNM). Beyond strong benchmark numbers, we show that action multimodality yields measurable benefits in learned representations and behavioral consistency, effects that deterministic architectures cannot replicate. ADT surpasses previous state-of-the-art on the challenging closed-loop Bench2Drive benchmark while achieving ten times lower latency, demonstrating that expressive, multimodal action modelling is both practically efficient and conceptually essential for robust end-to-end driving.

</details>


### [239] [Jailbreaking Multimodal Large Language Models using Multi-Clip Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02111)
*Choongwon Kang,Seungjong Sun,Hyunmin Jun,Jang Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MCV SafetyBench数据集（2920个视频），研究多模态大模型在视频输入下的安全对齐漏洞。实验表明：视频攻击成功率随片段数量增加而提升；视频模态比图像模态更脆弱，动态视频比静态视频更脆弱，上下文多样性越高越脆弱。并基于图像模态的相对鲁棒性提出防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱研究已证实多模态大模型的安全对齐可通过视觉输入绕过，但视频输入的哪些属性会诱发这种脆弱性尚不明确。本文旨在系统性地探究视频输入的多样性（多片段、动态性、上下文多样性）如何影响模型的安全脆弱性。

Method: 构建MCV SafetyBench数据集（2920个视频），每个视频由多个展示与有害查询相关多样化上下文的短视频片段组成。在8个代表性视频多模态大模型上进行实验，通过控制视频片段数量、动态/静态属性、上下文多样性等变量，系统评估攻击成功率的变化规律。

Result: 攻击成功率随视频片段数量增加而持续提升；视频模态比图像模态更脆弱；动态视频比静态视频更脆弱；视频上下文越多样越脆弱。

Conclusion: 揭示了视频输入中片段数量、动态性和上下文多样性是导致多模态大模型安全脆弱性的关键因素，并利用图像模态相对更鲁棒的特性提出可行的防御策略。

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced to process video inputs, concerns have emerged about their potential for malicious misuse. Prior jailbreak studies have shown that safety alignment in MLLMs can be bypassed through visual inputs, yet it remains unclear which properties of video inputs induce this vulnerability. To address this gap, we introduce Multi-Clip Video (MCV) SafetyBench, a dataset of 2,920 videos designed to evaluate how the diversity of video inputs affects the vulnerability of MLLMs. Each video consists of multiple short clips depicting diverse contexts related to a harmful query. Experiments on eight representative video MLLMs show that attack success consistently increases with the number of clips. Our results further indicate that the video modality is (1) more vulnerable than the image modality, (2) more vulnerable to dynamic videos than to static videos, and (3) more vulnerable when videos contain more diverse contexts. Building on these findings, we propose a defense strategy that leverages the relative robustness of the image modality.

</details>


### [240] [Understanding-Enhanced Model Collaboration for Long-Tailed Egocentric Mistake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02120)
*Boyu Han,Qianqian Xu,Shilong Bao,Zhiyong Yang,Ruochen Cui,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合小模型（高效粗粒度视频理解）和大模型（精确细粒度动作推理）的UE-MCM方法，用于从第一人称视频中检测用户操作错误，并通过协作门控和互补损失函数处理长尾分布问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时兼顾效率与准确性，尤其是难以检测那些局部正确但与整体工作流不一致的细微、罕见或模棱两可的操作错误。

Method: UE-MCM包含双分支结构：小模型分支（基于CLIP4CLIP+扩散对比重建）处理粗粒度视频+细粒度片段，检测局部正确但整体不一致的错误；大模型分支（Qwen3-VL Embedding）聚焦细粒度动作本身的执行错误。通过轻量级协作门控自适应融合两分支预测，并采用重加权交叉熵、AUC导向学习和标签感知调整等互补损失函数优化分类器。

Result: 所提系统在平衡速度与准确性的同时，能够有效检测第一人称教学视频中的细微、罕见和模棱两可的操作错误。

Conclusion: 通过粗粒度与细粒度理解的协同以及针对长尾分布的优化，UE-MCM为第一人称视频中的操作错误检测提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this report, we address the problem of determining whether a user performs an action incorrectly from egocentric video data. To this end, we propose an Understanding-Enhanced Model Collaboration Method (UE-MCM) that combines efficient coarse-grained video understanding with accurate fine-grained action reasoning. Specifically, UE-MCM contains a small model branch and a large model branch. The large model branch focuses on whether the fine-grained action itself is executed incorrectly, while the small model branch jointly takes the coarse-grained video and fine-grained segment as input to identify actions that may be locally correct but inconsistent with the overall workflow. The small model branch is built on a CLIP4CLIP video encoder initialized from a CLIP model enhanced by Diffusion Contrastive Reconstruction, and the large model branch uses the Qwen3-VL Embedding model to extract high-capacity representations from fine-grained action segments. The small-branch prediction and the large-branch prediction are then adaptively fused by a lightweight collaboration gate. To handle the long-tailed distribution of mistake instances, we optimize the classifiers with complementary objectives, including reweighted cross-entropy, AUC-oriented learning, and label-aware adjustment. The resulting system balances speed and accuracy, making it effective for detecting subtle, rare, and ambiguous mistakes in egocentric instructional videos.

</details>


### [241] [Equilibrated Diffusion: Frequency-aware Textual Embedding for Equilibrated Image Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02129)
*Liyuan Ma,Xueji Fang,Guo-Jun Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Equilibrated Diffusion，一种基于频率解耦的概念定制方法，通过分离低频（主体内容）和高频（风格）成分，分别优化嵌入，并引入掩码引导扩散和残差参考注意力，实现主体保真度与文本一致性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像定制方法将多概念属性打包到统一隐嵌入中，导致风格、背景等无关干扰难以消除，影响定制效果和文本-视觉匹配一致性。

Method: 1) 在频率空间分解概念，低频表示主体、高频表示风格，分别优化各频率嵌入；2) 融合多频率嵌入保留空间定制能力；3) 掩码引导扩散限制无关背景变化；4) 残差参考注意力(RRA)插入空间注意力以保持主体结构和身份一致性。

Result: 在主体保真度和文本对齐方面超过主流基线方法。

Conclusion: 频率驱动的解耦策略能有效分离主体与风格，实现更平衡的图像定制，验证了方法优越性。

Abstract: Image customization learns target subjects from reference concept images and generates conditioned images per text prompts, mainly modifying styles or backgrounds. Prevailing methods adopt fine-tuning to pack diverse concept attributes into a unified latent embedding, yet entangled attributes hinder elimination of irrelevant disturbances from style and background. To address this issue, we propose Equilibrated Diffusion, a frequency-driven approach that disentangles tangled concept features for balanced customization and consistent text-visual matching. Unlike conventional methods learning full concepts with shared embeddings and unified tuning, our work utilizes the inherent link between image frequency components and semantics: low frequencies represent subject content and high frequencies correspond to styles. We decompose concepts in frequency space and optimize each embedding independently. This separate optimization enables the denoiser to capture style detached from subject identity and generalize better to unseen stylistic prompts. Merging multi-frequency embeddings preserves the model's original spatial customization ability. We further deploy mask-guided diffusion to restrict irrelevant background changes and boost text alignment. Residual Reference Attention (RRA) is inserted into spatial attention to retain subject structure and identity consistency. Experiments prove Equilibrated Diffusion exceeds mainstream baselines on subject fidelity and text adherence, verifying our method's superiority.

</details>


### [242] [Ultra Diffusion Poser: Diffusion-Based Human Motion Tracking From Sparse Inertial Sensors and Ranging-Based Between-Sensor Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02153)
*Dominik Hollidt,Tommaso Bendinelli,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ultra Diffusion Poser模型，通过显式建模UWB测距的几何约束（空间布局模块+扩散引导）来提升稀疏惯性姿态估计精度，关节位置误差较先前工作降低22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏惯性姿态估计方法仅将UWB距离作为额外输入特征，忽略了其对传感器位置的物理几何约束，导致估计精度受限。

Method: 1) Spatial Layout Module：从UWB测量值解析重建3D传感器位置；2) 将传感器位置与IMU信号、UWB距离共同作为扩散模型的条件信号；3) UWB-Diffusion Guidance：在扩散采样过程中引导预测姿态与实测距离对齐。

Result: 关节位置误差较先前最优方法降低22%，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 显式建模UWB几何约束（解析重建传感器位置+扩散距离引导）能有效提升稀疏惯性姿态估计的精度。

Abstract: Methods using inertial measurement units (IMUs) provide a wearable alternative to camera-based motion capture. To mitigate drift from inertial signals, recent sparse inertial pose estimators integrate inter-sensor distances measured by ultra-wideband (UWB) ranging. So far, UWB distances have only been used as an additional input feature, ignoring the physical constraints they impose on sensor positions. However, these distances can also be used to reconstruct the underlying 3D sensor layout, which in turn provides more informative input for pose reconstruction. We propose Ultra Diffusion Poser, a diffusion model that explicitly models these geometric constraints. It includes a Spatial Layout Module that analytically reconstructs the 3D sensor positions from UWB measurements. These sensor positions are used alongside IMU signals and UWB distances as a conditioning signal during diffusion. Still, network predictions can violate inter-sensor distance measurements. To address this, we introduce UWB-Diffusion Guidance, which encourages alignment between predicted poses and measured distances during diffusion sampling. Together, these contributions enable our model to achieve state-of-the-art performance, reducing joint position error by up to 22% over prior work.

</details>


### [243] [InfoMerge: Information-aware Token Compression for Efficient Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02161)
*Xinxin Liu,Shiwei Gan,Xiao Liu,Yafeng Yin,Lei Xie,Sanglu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfoMerge是一种无需训练的视觉令牌压缩方法，通过段级二阶时序冗余估计（Temporal Fingerprint Difference）和内容感知预算分配（CABA），在减少85%视觉令牌的同时保留98.8%原始性能，实现4.24倍预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型中视觉令牌过多导致计算开销大，而现有的无训练压缩方法依赖局部相邻帧相似性估计时序冗余、按片段长度分配令牌预算，对帧级噪声敏感且无法捕捉真实视频中非均匀的信息分布。

Method: 提出InfoMerge，包含两个核心组件：1) Temporal Fingerprint Difference：基于段级二阶时序冗余估计策略，建模相同空间位置令牌在各片段内的时序相似性结构；2) Content-Aware Budget Allocation (CABA)：根据片段独特性和基于谱熵的表征丰富度动态分配段级令牌预算。

Result: 在多个基准和骨干网络上取得优异的效率-准确率权衡，在高压缩率下优势更明显。在LLaVA-OneVision-7B上，减少85%视觉令牌后保留98.8%原始平均性能，预填充阶段实现4.24倍加速。

Conclusion: InfoMerge通过更鲁棒的冗余估计和内容感知的预算分配，在有限令牌预算下更有效地利用令牌资源，同时保持强性能，优于现有依赖局部相似性和均匀分配的压缩方法。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) achieve strong performance in video understanding, but their excessive visual tokens bring substantial computational overhead. Existing training-free compression methods improve inference efficiency by reducing visual tokens, yet they often rely on local adjacent-frame similarity for temporal redundancy estimation or allocate token budgets mainly according to segment length. Such designs are sensitive to frame-level noise and fail to capture the non-uniform information distribution of real-world videos. To address these challenges, we propose InfoMerge, a training-free visual token compression method that improves token utilization through robust redundancy estimation and content-aware budget allocation. Specifically, we propose the Temporal Fingerprint Difference: a segment-level second-order temporal redundancy estimation strategy, which models the temporal similarity structure of tokens at the same spatial positions within each segment. We further introduce Content-Aware Budget Allocation (CABA), which dynamically allocates segment-level token budgets based on segment uniqueness and spectral-entropy-based representational richness. By reducing repeated preservation of redundant static regions and allocating more tokens to informative segments, InfoMerge makes better use of the limited token budget while maintaining strong performance. Extensive experiments show that InfoMerge achieves strong efficiency--accuracy trade-offs across multiple benchmarks and backbones, with more pronounced advantages under aggressive compression. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, InfoMerge retains 98.8\% of the original average performance while reducing 85\% of visual tokens and achieving a 4.24-fold speedup in the prefill stage.

</details>


### [244] [Multimodal Approaches for Visually-Rich Document Type Classification: A Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02162)
*Catyana Heyne,Jürgen Frikel,Filippo Riccio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对文档类型分类任务中的多模态建模策略进行了结构化分析，在统一实验框架下对比了四种代表性模型（LayoutLMv3、Donut、Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct、Qwen3-32B）在RVL-CDIP基准上的表现，发现专用多模态Transformer优于LLM方法，图像信息贡献最大，OCR文本提供辅助支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档类型分类方法依赖多模态信息（文本、视觉、布局），但模型架构异构、评估设置不统一，导致系统比较困难，难以评估领域进展。本文旨在通过统一框架下的结构化分析和受控实验来弥补这一不足。

Method: 在统一实验框架下，选取四种代表性模型（LayoutLMv3、Donut、Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct、Qwen3-32B），涵盖OCR依赖型与OCR无关型方法，在RVL-CDIP基准上进行受控对比实验，系统分析文本、图像和布局信息对文档类型分类的贡献。

Result: 专用多模态Transformer在视觉丰富和布局密集型文档上优于基于LLM的方法；图像信息对可靠分类贡献最大，OCR文本提供有用但次要的支持。

Conclusion: 多模态处理对于具有明显布局结构的文档仍然至关重要；本文为比较多模态架构提供了系统基础，并为选择有效的特征组合和模型设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Document type classification in visually rich documents remains challenging, as relevant information is distributed across textual, visual, and layout modalities. To capture this complexity, current approaches rely on diverse multimodal modeling strategies, resulting in heterogeneous architectures that complicate systematic comparison. This variability is also reflected in existing comparative studies, which often rely on heterogeneous evaluation setups, further complicating systematic comparison and making it difficult to assess progress. To address these limitations, this work provides a structured analysis of multimodal design strategies across transformer- and LLM-based architectures, combined with a controlled empirical comparison within a unified experimental framework. Specifically, four representative models (LayoutLMv3, Donut, Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct, and Qwen3-32B) are evaluated on the RVL-CDIP benchmark to systematically analyze the contributions of text, image, and layout information for document type classification, with a particular focus on contrasting OCR-dependent and OCR-free approaches. The results show that specialized multimodal Transformers outperform LLM-based approaches on visually rich and layout-intensive documents. Image information contributes most strongly to reliable classification, while OCR-derived text provides useful but secondary support. These findings highlight that multimodal processing remains essential for documents with pronounced layout structure. Overall, the study provides a systematic basis for comparing multimodal architectures and offers practical guidance for selecting effective feature combinations and model designs for document type classification.

</details>


### [245] [Disentanglement-Based Equivariant Learning for Compositional VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02168)
*Zhou Du,Zhaoquan Yuan,Xiao Wu,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于解耦的等变学习框架（DEAL），通过因果启发的干预实现视觉和文本概念的解耦，并利用等变约束增强模型的组合推理能力。在CLEVR-CoGenT和GQA-SGL基准上，该方法在视觉和语言泛化设置下均优于现有最先进方法。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合VQA方法忽视底层概念的解耦，难以有效捕捉组合变化机制；且最先进技术依赖额外线索进行训练，在真实VQA场景中不可行。

Method: 提出DEAL框架：1）采用因果启发的干预在重新编码框架中解耦视觉和文本输入中的概念；2）基于等变性原则，对推理输入进行组合变换并对输出施加等变约束，增强组合推理能力；3）仅依赖真实答案进行训练，无需额外线索。

Result: 在CLEVR-CoGenT和GQA-SGL基准上，DEAL在视觉和语言泛化设置下均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的DEAL框架有效解决了组合VQA中的概念解耦和组合推理问题，且无需额外监督线索，在多个基准上取得了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Compositional visual question answering (VQA) represents a challenging yet fundamental task that requires models to comprehend novel combinations of previously learned concepts. The current methods often overlook the disentanglement of underlying concepts and are restricted in terms of their ability to effectively capture the compositional variation mechanism. Moreover, the state-of-the-art techniques depend on additional clues for training, which is not feasible in real-world VQA scenarios. To address these issues, in this paper, we introduce a novel Disentanglement-based EquivAriant Learning (DEAL) framework for compositional VQA, which is guided exclusively by ground-truth answers. In DEAL, we employ causality-inspired interventions to disentangle concepts derived from visual and textual inputs within a re-encoding framework. Based on the principle of equivariance, we subsequently perform a compositional transformation on the inference input and impose the equivariant constraint on the output to augment the compositional reasoning capacity of the model. Comprehensive experiments conducted on the benchmark CLEVR-CoGenT and GQA-SGL datasets validate the superiority of our proposed DEAL approach over the existing state-of-the-art methods for compositional VQA tasks in both visual and linguistic generalization settings.

</details>


### [246] [InsightVQA: High-Dimensional Emotion-Cognitive Visual Question Answering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02171)
*Shiyu Wang,Ziyu Liu,Chaoyi Yu,Yujie Yin,Zhongqian Mao,Jing Chen,Jiaqi Song,Yunshi Lan,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsightVQA是一个层次化视觉问答数据集，包含72.5万QA对，从35.1万张图像中筛选出13.8万张高质量图像，覆盖感知、基础理解和认知推理三个层次，用于情感理解和认知推理评估。同时提出InsightNet作为情感调优基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦于情感识别，缺乏对情感产生原因的深层理解和响应导向分析的支持，因此需要构建支持层次化情感理解与认知推理的数据集。

Method: 从6个公开来源收集35.1万张图像，经过多阶段筛选得到13.8万张高置信度图像；在三个层次上标注：感知QA（情感和效价识别）、基础理解QA（通过约束引导生成提取视觉触发因素）、认知QA（响应意图预测和序列洞察推理）；构建含72.5万QA对的InsightVQA和含3万样本的细粒度评估基准InsightVQA-Bench，并提出InsightNet作为基线模型。

Result: InsightVQA对多模态大语言模型在基础情感理解和推理方面构成了显著挑战。

Conclusion: InsightVQA有效填补了情感理解基准在层次化推理和基础理解方面的空白，InsightNet作为基线为后续研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Visual emotion understanding requires models not only to recognize emotional states, but also to why they arise and perform higher-level cognitive reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on emotion recognition, offering limited support for grounded understanding and response-oriented analysis. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{InsightVQA}, a large-scale dataset for hierarchical visual question answering on emotion understanding and cognitive reasoning. Building from 351K images collected from six public sources, we apply a rigorous multi-stage filtering pipeline to curate 138K high-confidence images. Each image is annotated at three hierarchical levels: perception QA for emotion and valence recognition, grounded understanding QA constructed from visual trigger extraction through constraint-guided generation, and cognition QA centered on response intent prediction and sequential insight reasoning. In total, InsightVQA contains 725K QA pairs. We further present \textbf{InsightVQA-Bench}, a high-quality evaluation benchmark comprising 30K samples for fine-grained evaluation. To support evaluation, we introduce \textbf{InsightNet}, an emotion-tuned baseline for MLLMs. Results demonstrate that InsightVQA poses significant challenges for grounded emotion understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [247] [Order within Chaos: Capturing Intrinsic Energy Anomalies for AI-Manipulated Image Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02178)
*Yiming Wang,Baiqi Wu,Qingming Li,Jiahao Chen,Tong Zhang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAME框架，利用扩散过程对局部高频方差的抑制特性（统计能量差）进行AI生成图像篡改定位，并引入EditStream流水线实现持续的训练数据合成，在多项基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像篡改定位方法依赖物理噪声特征，无法有效检测生成式AI模型产生的逼真合成图像伪造，需要新的理论基础和方法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 理论证明扩散过程会抑制局部高频方差，产生与光学成像自然熵不同的统计能量差；提出FLAME框架，包含LAD图捕捉异常、参数高效适配器与SAM结合实现像素级定位；同时设计EditStream自动化流水线实现持续指令式训练数据合成。

Result: FLAME在AI生成伪造数据集上显著超越现有方法，达到新最优性能，并能有效泛化到未见过的生成架构。

Conclusion: 基于扩散过程内在统计特性的方法可以有效检测AI生成图像篡改，且所提数据合成流水线有助于弥合取证基准与生成模型发展之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative AI have led to image editing models capable of producing realistic forgeries that evade traditional image forgery localization methods, as these approaches depend on physical noise absent in synthetic data. To address this challenge, we theoretically demonstrate that the diffusion process inherently suppresses local high-frequency variance, creating a statistical energy gap that is distinguishable from the natural entropy of optical imaging. Guided by this insight, we propose FLAME, a unified framework that utilizes a LAD map to capture these intrinsic anomalies, coupled with a parameter-efficient adapter for SAM to achieve precise, pixel-level forgery localization. Furthermore, to bridge the lag between forensic benchmarks and evolving generative models, we introduce EditStream, an automated pipeline for continuous, instruction-based training data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FLAME establishes a new state-of-the-art, significantly outperforming previous methods on AI-generated forgery datasets while effectively generalizing to unseen generative architectures. Our code is available at https://github.com/phoenixnir/FLAME.

</details>


### [248] [Symmetry-Aware 9D Pose Estimation with Sim(3)-Consistent Feature and Spherical Inception Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02219)
*Panfei Cheng,Hongshan Yu,Wenrui Chen,Xiaojun Tang,Jian Liu,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSH-Pose方法，通过语义引导的对称点估计模块（利用大视觉模型LVM）实现无需形状先验的平移/尺寸估计，以及基于球形大核初始卷积的特征融合模块，有效解决了类别级物体姿态估计中的Sim(3)空间学习困难和类内变化问题，在基准测试和真实场景中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例级姿态估计方法难以泛化到未见物体，而类别级方法受限于Sim(3)空间的非线性学习复杂性和类内变化，亟需一种能同时处理这两大挑战的有效方法。

Method: (1) 平移/尺寸估计器：利用大视觉模型（LVM）的强泛化能力，设计语义引导的对称感知模块来推断对称点，无需形状先验即可准确估计平移和尺寸，为旋转估计提供预计算线索；(2) 特征融合模块：提出球形大核初始卷积，融合LVM语义特征与系统计算的几何特征，通过长程依赖建模提取关键姿态特征，同时控制计算成本。

Result: 在多个基准测试和真实场景中达到SOTA性能，并成功构建了能够处理多样化物体的鲁棒机器人拾取系统。

Conclusion: 通过将平移/尺寸估计与旋转估计解耦，并利用LVM的语义能力结合高效的几何特征融合，有效降低了Sim(3)空间的学习难度，为类别级物体姿态估计提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Object pose estimation is a fundamental problem for an agent system to perceive or manipulate objects in images or videos. However, current instance-level methods struggle with generalization to unseen objects. Category-level methods seek to address this, but remain constrained by the complexities of learning in the non-linear Sim(3) space and intra-class variations. To address these challenges, We propose an effective method for category-level object pose estimation with two key innovations: (1) A translation/size estimator, featuring a semantic-guided symmetry-aware module that leverages robust generalization capabilities of a large vision model (LVM) to infer symmetry points, resulting in accurate translation and size without shape priors. This result serves as a precomputed cue for rotation estimation, thereby reducing the difficulty of learning in the non-linear Sim(3) space and laying a robust foundation for tackling the inherently more challenging rotation estimation. (2) A feature fusion module, based on our proposed spherical large-kernel inception convolution, fuses semantic features from the LVM with systematically computed geometric features to extract essential pose features from intra-class variations by modeling long-range dependencies without excessive computational cost. Built on these innovations, we achieve SOTA on benchmarks and real-world scenes, while developing a robust robotic picking system capable of handling diverse objects. Our code will be available at the project page: {\hypersetup{urlcolor=blue}https://panfei-cheng.github.io/SSH-Pose}.

</details>


### [249] [CORE-MTL: Rethinking Gradient Balancing via Causal Orthogonal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02221)
