*Chengfeng Wu,Tao Zou,Yanru Wu,Jingge Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE-MTL，一个基于因果表征的多任务学习框架，通过对共享表征进行语义-残差因子分解，将任务相关结构与虚假上下文分离，从而缓解负迁移并提升分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化导向的多任务学习方法要么平衡任务梯度，要么修改共享架构，但这些方法对共享表征的内容不可知，无法将任务相关结构与虚假上下文解耦，导致负迁移和泛化能力差。

Method: 提出CORE-MTL框架，对共享表征进行结构化的语义-残差因子分解：语义流集中任务相关结构，残差流排除干扰变异。在视觉领域通过物理先验和统计约束实例化该方法。

Result: 理论上，相比优化导向方法，CORE-MTL拥有更紧的分布外泛化界，且无需显式梯度投影或重加权即可减少任务梯度干扰。实验上，在视觉多任务基准的分布内和分布外场景中均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CORE-MTL通过因果驱动的表征分解策略，有效解决了多任务学习中的负迁移和泛化问题，为多任务学习提供了新的表征中心视角。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) aims to construct a joint model for multiple tasks by sharing a common representation across domains. To achieve this goal, existing optimization-centric methods either balance task gradients or modify the shared architecture. However, as these approaches remain agnostic to the content of the shared representation, they fail to disentangle task-relevant structure from spurious context, leading to negative transfer and poor generalization. To overcome this limitation, we propose Causal Orthogonal Representations for Multi-Task Learning (CORE-MTL), a causally motivated representation-centric framework that encourages a structured semantic-residual factorization of the shared representation, concentrating task-relevant structure in the semantic stream while relegating nuisance variation to the residual stream. We instantiate this framework in the visual domain by leveraging physical priors for structured scenes and statistical constraints for attributes. Theoretically, our method enjoys a tighter out-of-distribution generalization bound than optimization-centric methods and reduces task gradient interference without explicit gradient projection or reweighting. Empirically, CORE-MTL consistently outperforms existing methods on visual multi-task benchmarks in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Hope-Rita/CORE-MTL.

</details>


### [250] [Chroma Clues: Leveraging Color Statistics to Detect Synthetic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02224)
*Lea Uhlenbrock,Davide Cozzolino,Christian Riess*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用生成模型在颜色统计上的弱点（LPIPS损失对色度不敏感），提出六种手工颜色变换和学习任务优化颜色变换方法，实现对AI生成图像的检测、可视化评估和多类别归因，平均泛化准确率达93.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI合成图像快速进化，对图像取证构成挑战；现有方法对颜色统计线索利用不足，而生成模型在颜色模仿上存在固有弱点。

Method: 1) 分析LPIPS损失对色度不敏感导致颜色统计差异；2) 设计六种手工颜色变换及一种可学习的任务优化颜色变换；3) 基于变换提取像素级/块级颜色敏感特征；4) 利用变换产生视觉噪声模式进行可视化；5) 增强颜色模式支持多类别归因。

Result: 简单可解释分类器平均泛化准确率93.27%；对六种后处理具有强鲁棒性；变换可产生自然与合成区域的视觉噪声差异；可增强颜色模式用于多类归因。

Conclusion: 颜色统计是生成图像检测的有效线索，所提方法兼具高准确率、可解释性、鲁棒性和多任务适用性。

Abstract: The evolution and dissemination of AI-synthesized images is occurring at an unprecedented rate. Image generators are making rapid progress in their goal of perfectly imitating natural images, which also challenges image forensics.
  In this work, we exploit an underexplored cue in current generative models, namely their weakness to imitate color statistics of natural images. We first show that the LPIPS loss used for training image generators is less sensitive to chrominance than to luminance, which may lead to statistical discrepancies in the colors of synthetic images. Building on this observation, we then introduce six hand-crafted color transformations and a method to learn a task-optimized color transform to statistically expose generated images. These transformations can be used in various ways. First, we define color-sensitive features at pixel-level or patch-level. A simple, interpretable classifier achieves with these features an average generalization accuracy of 93.27% and strong robustness against six types of post-processing. Second, we demonstrate that the transformations exhibit characteristic visual noise patterns in natural and synthetic image areas, which enables an intuitive visual image evaluation. Third, we demonstrate that the transforms can enhance color patterns in generated images for improved multiclass attribution.

</details>


### [251] [Towards Resolving Optimization Conflicts Between Image- and Text-Based Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02242)
*Karina Kvanchiani,Timur Mamedov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦的两阶段训练流程，通过单一视觉编码器同时支持图像和文本行人重识别，避免跨任务干扰，并发现I2I预训练和文本监督能提升两者性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像行人重识别（I2I ReID）和文本行人重识别（T2I ReID）之间存在模态差异和冲突的训练目标，导致共享表征学习次优。两类任务常被独立研究，一个检索任务优化的损失函数会负面影响另一个任务的表征质量。

Method: 提出解耦的两阶段训练流程：第一阶段训练视觉编码器（结合文本监督），第二阶段微调跨模态对齐；采用单一视觉编码器同时支持I2I和T2I检索，避免跨任务干扰；对比了多种域混合策略、学习策略和任务目标。

Result: I2I ReID预训练对T2I数据的泛化能力有正向提升；在视觉编码器训练阶段引入文本监督可同时提升I2I和T2I性能；解耦训练有效避免了跨任务干扰。

Conclusion: 该解耦两阶段训练范式为实现统一ReID系统和跨模态检索提供了有意义的方向，揭示了I2I预训练和文本监督的协同作用。

Abstract: The joint optimization of image-based (I2I) and text-based (T2I) person re-identification (ReID) is hindered by modality discrepancies and conflicting training objectives, leading to suboptimal shared representations. While I2I ReID focuses on identity-level invariance across images of the same person, T2I ReID is driven by instance-specific textual descriptions tied to unique visual traits. This paper explores the fundamental difference between two ReID tasks and their optimization processes for effective training. Since I2I and T2I ReID are often studied separately, the loss functions optimized for one retrieval setting may negatively affect the representation quality required by the other. Motivated by these findings, we propose a decoupled two-stage training pipeline for learning a shared representation across image and text modalities. The pipeline is based on a single vision encoder that supports both I2I and T2I retrieval while avoiding cross-task interference during training. We provide extensive experiments across multiple configurations, varying domain mixing procedures, learning strategies, and task objectives. We observed that I2I ReID pre-training positively impacts the generalization ability to T2I data. Besides, we find that incorporating textual supervision during the vision encoder training stage enhances both I2I and T2I performance. We believe our insights provide a meaningful step toward unified ReID systems and cross-modal retrieval overall.

</details>


### [252] [Ego-METAS: Egocentric online Multimodal Energy-efficient Temporal Action Segmentation benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02246)
*Maria Santos-Villafranca,Jesus Bermudez-cameo,Alejandro Perez-Yus,Giovanni Maria Farinella,Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego-METAS是首个面向在线、多模态、节能的时序动作分割基准，包含100+小时第一人称视频、5种模态，要求模型在硬件能量预算下动态选择传感器，揭示了现有方法在连续未修剪视频中的局限性，并证明了随机动态融合也能有效平衡精度与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需在资源受限设备上“始终在线”地感知环境，但现有工作大多假设无限算力，缺乏对能量感知感知（energy-aware perception）的系统研究。

Method: 构建Ego-METAS基准：汇集EgoExo4D、CMU-MMAC、CaptainCook4D共100+小时未修剪第一人称视频，涵盖RGB、音频、注视、IMU、单色相机5种模态；定义在线时序动作分割任务，要求模型逐时间步动态选择传感器并遵守硬件能量预算；发布统一分割、精标注、预提取特征及多种基线路由策略。

Result: 最优路由高度依赖场景；现有基于修剪片段设计的策略学习方法难以适应连续未修剪环境；即使简单的随机模态动态融合也能在严格能量预算下显著平衡预测精度。

Conclusion: Ego-METAS为开发鲁棒、成本感知的自治策略提供了标准化基础，推动始终在线的节能型具身AI发展。

Abstract: To operate in the physical world, embodied agents must perceive their environment in an "always-on" fashion, selectively accessing the most informative sensors to balance energy constraints and task accuracy. Despite its importance for resource-constrained devices, energy-aware perception remains under-explored, with most prior work assuming unlimited compute. To address this, we introduce Ego-METAS: the first Egocentric online Multimodal Energy-efficient Temporal Action Segmentation benchmark. Ego-METAS provides a unified testbed of more than 100 hours of untrimmed egocentric video from EgoExo4D, CMU-MMAC, and CaptainCook4D, spanning 5 modalities (RGB, audio, gaze, IMU, and monochrome camera). We formulate an online temporal action segmentation task where models must dynamically select which sensors to activate at each timestep while strictly adhering to hardware-representative energy budgets. Alongside the benchmark, we release unified splits, cleaned annotations, pre-extracted features, and a diverse suite of baseline routing policies. Our evaluations show that optimal routing is highly scenario-dependent, and that existing policy-learning methods, designed primarily for trimmed clips, struggle to adapt to continuous, untrimmed environments. However, even simple dynamic fusion of complementary modalities (e.g., via random routing) proves critical for balancing predictive accuracy against strict energy budgets. Ultimately, Ego-METAS provides a standardized foundation to develop robust, cost-aware policies for autonomous, always-on embodied AI.

</details>


### [253] [From Extrinsic to Intrinsic: Geodesic-Guided Representation Learning for 3D Geometric Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02268)
*Yuming Zhao,Junhui Hou,Qijian Zhang,Jia Qin,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一种新的3D表示学习范式，通过恢复内在表面测地度量来学习等距嵌入，引入拓扑强制目标和两阶段训练策略，在测地距离预测及多项下游任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D表示学习过度依赖外在空间结构或高层语义，难以捕捉形状的同一性特征和底层流形拓扑结构。

Method: 提出PRISM范式，包含拓扑强制目标约束隐空间结构，以及两阶段训练策略缓解测地距离分布中的样本不平衡问题。

Result: 在测地距离预测上表现出满意的精度、鲁棒性和高效率，在形状识别、表面参数化、非刚性对应等下游任务中取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过恢复内在测地度量学习等距嵌入，可以有效弥补当前3D表示学习在捕捉形状本质和流形拓扑方面的不足。

Abstract: Geometric analysis fundamentally distinguishes between \textit{extrinsic} and \textit{intrinsic} perspectives. The dominant paradigm in current 3D representation learning relies on either extrinsic spatial structures or high-level semantics, struggling to capture the essence of shape identity and underlying manifold topology. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel 3D representation learning paradigm, namely \textbf{PRISM}, for \textbf{P}re-training, which learns isometric embeddings by \textbf{R}ecovering the \textbf{I}ntrinsic \textbf{S}urface geodesic \textbf{M}etric. PRISM incorporates a topology-enforcing objective that explicitly constrains the structure of latent space, alongside a specialized two-stage training recipe mitigating sample imbalance inherent in the distribution of geodesic distances. Experiments demonstrate that our approach shows satisfactory accuracy, robustness, and high efficiency in geodesic distance prediction and achieves superior performance across diverse downstream tasks, including shape recognition, surface parameterization, and non-rigid correspondence. The code will be publicly available at https://github.com/AidenZhao/PRISM.

</details>


### [254] [Vision-language Models for Driver Monitoring Systems: A Driver Activity Description Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02273)
*David J. Lerch,Sarath Mulugurthi,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在识别细微驾驶员行为方面的不足，创建了Drive&Act数据集的细粒度自然语言描述版本，并基于该数据集微调VLM。微调后的VLM在驾驶员活动理解上显著提升（ACCR得分从66提升至76），且能跨数据集泛化到Driver Monitoring Dataset（DMD），证明了丰富描述的驾驶员动作数据能有效提升VLM对驾驶员行为的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在通用数据集上训练，难以准确识别驾驶员行为的细微差异，无法可靠地生成细粒度的驾驶员活动描述，亟需针对性的细粒度描述数据集来提升模型在驾驶员监控系统中的表现。

Method: 1) 为Drive&Act数据集创建了包含细粒度描述的自然语言版本；2) 利用基于LLM的评分方法评估三种VLM在新基准上的表现；3) 基于标注的Drive&Act数据集构建细粒度描述数据集用于微调VLM；4) 在Driver Monitoring Dataset（DMD）上进行跨数据集评估验证泛化能力。

Result: 微调后的VLM在ACCR得分上达到76，显著优于零样本VLM基线的66分；跨数据集评估表明微调模型能很好地泛化到DMD数据集中的动作。

Conclusion: 使用丰富的驾驶员动作描述对VLM进行适配能显著提升其解释驾驶员行为的能力，同时未来需要更多样化的数据集以支持更广泛的泛化应用。

Abstract: Understanding subtle driver actions is essential for building reliable driver monitoring systems. Existing visionlanguage models (VLMs) are trained on general datasets and struggle to recognize fine distinctions in driver behaviors. This paper addresses this limitation by creating a detailed natural language version of the Drive&Act dataset. We evaluate three VLMs on our new benchmark using LLM-based scoring methods. Their performance on the new benchmark shows that they cannot reliably generate accurate fine-grained driver activity descriptions. Based on the labeled Drive&Act dataset we create a new Drive&Act description dataset containing finegrained descriptions to train VLMs on driver activity understanding. Cross dataset evaluation on the Driver Monitoring Dataset (DMD) shows that the VLM fine-tuned on our new Drive&Act description dataset generalizes well to actions in the DMD dataset. The VLM fine-tuned on our Drive&Act description dataset achieves an ACCR score of 76 outperforming the zero-shot VLM baseline with an ACCR score of 66. These findings demonstrate that adapting VLMs with richly described driver actions can significantly improve their ability to interpret driver behavior while also highlighting the need for more diverse datasets to support broader generalization in future applications. Our Drive&Act description dataset and code will be publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [255] [Cross-modal linkage risk in clinical vision-language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02276)
*Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型在胸部X光片与放射学报告之间的跨模态重链接隐私风险，发现模型临床专业化程度越高，重链接风险越大；通过仅对投影头进行差分隐私优化，可在不显著损害图像表征质量的前提下大幅降低重链接风险（Recall@1降低61.8%，AUROC仅从79.63%降至79.43%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在胸部X光片与放射报告之间学习到的共享嵌入空间会保留实例级的图像-报告对应关系，这在图像数据单独共享或报告受控访问的场景下构成隐私风险——匿名化图像可能仅通过余弦相似度与原始报告重新关联。现有研究缺乏对此风险的形式化评估与缓解方法。

Method: 将重链接形式化为图像到报告的检索任务，利用公开配对数据集作为基准；评估不同临床专业化程度的VLM在MIMIC-CXR（43,793对）和CheXpert Plus（29,296对）上的重链接性能；采用仅对投影头进行差分隐私优化（ε=0.34, δ=6×10⁻⁶）的缓解方法，冻结双编码器。

Result: 最强VLM在N=100时检索准确率为随机水平的15倍，N=10,000时为50倍，全数据库规模下仍显著高于随机；病理匹配的难负样本下信号依然存在。DP微调后Recall@1在N=10,000上降低61.8%，且零样本迁移至CheXpert Plus；图像侧14标签线性分类宏AUROC仅从79.63%微降至79.43%。

Conclusion: 面向对齐层的差分隐私微调能有效降低跨模态重链接风险，且不显著损害模型的临床图像表征能力，为在隐私敏感场景下安全部署VLM提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) trained on paired chest radiographs and radiology reports learn a shared embedding space that can preserve instance-level image-report correspondence. This poses a privacy risk in settings where radiographs and reports are deliberately kept separate after acquisition, such as image-only data sharing or access-controlled reports, because a de-identified image may be re-linked to its original narrative report through cosine similarity alone. We formalized this as image-to-report retrieval and used public paired cohorts, in which the true pairing is known by design, as ground-truth benchmarks to audit the risk rather than as the privacy scenario. Evaluating VLMs of increasing clinical specialization on 406,241 paired examples from 126,804 patients across MIMIC-CXR (43,793 held-out pairs) and external CheXpert Plus (29,296 pairs), we found that re-linkage rose systematically with specialization: the strongest VLM retrieved the correct report at 15 times chance at a candidate pool of N = 100, 50 times chance at N = 10,000, and well above chance at full-database scale. The signal persisted under pathology-matched hard negatives that removed disease-label shortcuts, indicating correspondence beyond broad diagnostic categories. To reduce it without retraining, we froze both encoders and applied differentially private optimization only to the projection heads defining the alignment layer (epsilon = 0.34, delta = 6x10-6). This reduced Recall@1 by 61.8% at N = 10,000 on MIMIC-CXR and transferred to CheXpert Plus without retraining, while image-side utility was largely preserved: macro AUROC for linear-probe classification across 14 labels shifted only from 79.63% to 79.43%. Targeted DP finetuning of the shared alignment layer can substantially reduce cross-modal re-linkage without materially degrading the image representations that make these models clinically useful.

</details>


### [256] [Neural Acquisition & Representation of Subsurface Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02292)
*Arjun Majumdar,Raphael Braun,Hendrik Lensch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过学习物体表面每点像素足迹响应来获取和估计高细节次表面光传输散射特性的方法，使用3D扫描和U-Net CNN，通过立体投影仪-相机系统和相移轮廓术高效采集数据，实现高分辨率重光照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有次表面散射获取方法难以在高度细节水平上准确估计光传输的散射特性，需要一种既能高效采集又能高精度重建的方法。

Method: 使用立体投影仪-相机系统和相移轮廓术(PSP)模式高效采集散射物体数据；以3D扫描技术作为输入，通过U-Net CNN学习每个点的像素足迹响应；重建密集像素足迹以实现任意高分辨率投影模式的重光照。

Result: 预测的像素足迹与真实响应几乎一致，通过与实际拍摄图像进行定性和定量对比得到验证；同一模型在多个视角和多个物体上训练后，能够泛化到未见过的次表面散射材料。

Conclusion: 该方法能够高细节地获取和重建次表面散射特性，支持高分辨率重光照，且学到的表示具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present a method to acquire and estimate the sub-surface scattering properties of light transport at a highly detailed level by learning the pixel footprint response at each point on the object surface. The reconstruction leverages 3D scanning techniques as input to a U-Net CNN. A stereo projector-camera setup using phase-shifted profilometry (PSP) patterns efficiently captures the data for a variety of scattering objects. Reconstructing dense pixel footprints allows for relighting with arbitrary high-resolution projector patterns. The final output is a relit color image. Qualitative and quantitative comparison against illuminated real-world captured images demonstrate that the predicted footprints are almost identical to the actual responses. The same model is trained for multiple views across multiple objects such that the learned representations can be used to generalize to unseen sub-surface scattering materials as well.

</details>


### [257] [Cross-Domain Dead Tree Detection via Knowledge Distillation in Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02303)
*Anis Ur Rahman,Mete Ahishali,Einari Heinaro,Samuli Junttila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过知识蒸馏（KD）将TreeMort-1T-UNet模型从芬兰航空影像（源域）迁移至波兰、德国、爱沙尼亚等目标域，发现特征级KD在低数据场景下表现最优，实现了跨域森林死树检测的鲁棒泛化，关键指标如波兰数据集上Mean Tree IoU达0.106、Instance F1-score达0.63，并展现出优越的表征不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空影像中死树检测对评估森林健康至关重要，但气候变化导致全球树木死亡率上升，而域变异性和标记数据稀缺限制了模型的泛化能力，亟需有效的域适应方法。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏（KD）四种变体（Basic、Self、Feature-level、Ensemble）将预训练的TreeMort-1T-UNet从芬兰源域迁移至波兰、德国、爱沙尼亚目标域，以微调为基线，使用Mean Tree IoU、Instance F1-score等指标评估，并结合余弦相似度、CKA、SSIM、t-SNE、线性探测等表征分析。

Result: 特征级KD在所有KD变体中表现最优，波兰数据集上Mean Tree IoU 0.106、Instance F1-score 0.63、Instance Precision 0.55、Mean Centroid Error 3.039；跨域泛化稳健（芬兰0.15、波兰0.67、德国0.60、爱沙尼亚0.59）；在低数据场景下假阳性更少；表征不变性更优（深层CKA/SSIM更高、t-SNE域混合更好、线性探测AUC达0.95）。

Conclusion: 特征级KD在跨域死树检测中优于微调基线，尤其在精度关键型应用和低数据场景下表现突出；特征对齐等关键组件有助于平衡性能指标；知识蒸馏有望增强遥感领域的迁移学习，为生态监测和可持续森林管理提供可扩展、域鲁棒的工具。

Abstract: Detecting dead trees in aerial imagery is vital for assessing forest health, especially as tree mortality increases globally due to climate change, but domain variability and scarce labeled data often limit model generalization. This study advances the TreeMort-1T-UNet (Tree Mortality 1-Task U-Net) model, initially trained on Finnish aerial imagery (source domain), by applying knowledge distillation (KD) to adapt it to various target domains, including Polish, German, and Estonian datasets representing diverse forest types. We assess four KD variants: Basic, Self, Feature-level, and Ensemble, against a fine-tuning baseline, using Mean Tree IoU, Instance F1-score, Instance Precision, and Mean Centroid Error as key metrics, alongside representational analyses (e.g., cosine similarity, CKA, SSIM, t-SNE, and linear probing) for domain invariance. Feature-level KD outperforms others, yielding a Mean Tree IoU of 0.106, Instance F1-score of 0.63, Instance Precision of 0.55, and Mean Centroid Error of 3.039 on the Polish dataset, with robust precision across other target domains (e.g., 0.15 on Finnish, 0.67 on Polish, 0.60 on German, 0.59 on Estonian). It excels in low-data scenarios with fewer false positives and shows superior representational invariance (e.g., higher deep-layer CKA/SSIM, better domain mixing in t-SNE, and linear probing AUC of 0.95), making it ideal for precision-critical forestry applications. Additional ablation studies confirm that key components like feature alignment enhance its performance balance across metrics. Our findings demonstrate KD's potential to enhance transfer learning in remote sensing, offering a scalable, domain-robust tool for ecological monitoring and sustainable forest management.

</details>


### [258] [Deep Learning for Remote Sensing to Improve Flood Inundation Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02310)
*Yogesh Bhattarai,Vijay Chaudhary,Wai Lim Kim,Sanjib Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）和掩码扩散Transformer（MDT）架构的云去除框架，用于多光谱Sentinel-2B洪水影像，在保持视觉保真度和水文一致性的同时实现云遮挡区域重建，显著提升水体连续性和光谱特征保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学卫星影像在极端降水事件期间常被云层遮挡，传统云去除方法（如时间合成或插值）难以捕捉洪水淹没的动态变化，亟需一种能够生成物理一致、云无关洪水影像的方法以支持灾害风险管理。

Method: 基于Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models（DDPM）与Masked Diffusion Transformer（MDT）架构，利用自注意力机制捕获广域空间上下文，通过掩码令牌建模显式学习重建云遮挡区域，在真实云模式的多光谱Sentinel-2B洪水场景上训练模型。

Result: 模型生成的去云影像在标准图像质量指标和水文特定指标（水体连续性、光谱特征保留、水检测指数）上均表现优异，验证了扩散模型在洪水监测云去除中的鲁棒性和物理一致性。

Conclusion: 扩散生成模型为光学洪水监测中的云去除提供了一种稳健且物理一致的替代方案，能够实现更可靠、连续的观测，有效支撑灾害风险管理与洪水相关决策。

Abstract: Flooding is the most pervasive natural disaster worldwide. Timely and accurate flood inundation mapping are essential for informing disaster risk management. Optical satellite missions provide high-resolution, multispectral observations critical for flood detection and inundation mapping. However, their operational utility is severely constrained by cloud cover during extreme precipitation events. Conventional cloud-removal techniques based on temporal compositing or interpolation often fail to capture inundation dynamics. In this study, we introduce a cloud-removal framework for flood imagery based on Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models, leveraging the Masked Diffusion Transformer architecture. The proposed approach exploits self-attention mechanisms to capture wider spatial context and employs masked token modeling to explicitly learn the reconstruction of cloud-obscured regions. Trained on multispectral Sentinel-2B flood scenes with realistic cloud patterns, the model generates cloud-free image realizations that preserve both visual fidelity and hydrological consistency. Reconstruction performance is evaluated using standard image quality metrics alongside flood-specific hydrological measures, demonstrating improved continuity of water bodies and preservation of spectral signatures critical for water detection indices. The results indicate that diffusion-based generative modeling offers a robust and physically consistent alternative for cloud removal in optical flood monitoring, enabling more reliable, continuous observations to support disaster risk management and flood-related decision making.

</details>


### [259] [Training-Free Composed Video Retrieval via Visual Representation-Guided Video-LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02321)
*Yang Liu,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Siran Dai,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的组合视频检索框架，先通过DINOv3筛选视觉相关候选，再用视频-LLM评估修改指令，最后进行推理精炼，在CVPR 2026挑战赛中取得48.78 Recall@1和51.48 Recall@5的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来大视觉语言模型的发展使得视频检索从简单的文本搜索扩展到更灵活的场景，用户可以通过视觉示例和文本指令共同指定检索目标。CVPR 2026推理感知组合视频检索挑战赛要求系统根据参考视频和修改指令检索目标视频，现有方法需要在这类复杂检索场景中有效结合视觉表征与语言推理。

Method: 提出Visual Representation-Guided Video-LLM Reasoning框架，包含三个阶段：1) 使用冻结的DINOv3模型获取紧凑的视觉相关候选集；2) 应用大视觉语言模型评估每个候选是否满足修改指令；3) 对排名靠前的候选进行基于推理的精炼以改善首位预测。

Result: 在测试集上Recall@1达到48.78，Recall@5达到51.48，且无需训练。

Conclusion: 该无训练框架在组合视频检索任务上取得了有竞争力的结果，未来可通过更强的视频-LLM以及视觉表征与语言推理的深度融合进一步提升检索精度。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models have expanded video retrieval from simple text-based search to more flexible scenarios, where users may specify the desired result through both visual examples and textual instructions. In the CVPR 2026 Reason-Aware Composed Video Retrieval Challenge, the system is required to retrieve a target video according to a reference video and a modification instruction. To address this task, we develop Visual Representation-Guided Video-LLM Reasoning for Training-Free Composed Video Retrieval. Our framework first uses frozen DINOv3 models to obtain a compact set of visually relevant candidates, and then applies large vision-language models to evaluate whether each candidate satisfies the modification instruction. A final reasoning-based refinement is further performed on the top candidates to improve the first-ranked prediction. Without training, our system achieves 48.78 Recall@1 and 51.48 Recall@5 on the test set. Future work may further improve retrieval accuracy through stronger video-LLMs and detailed integration between visual representations and language reasoning.

</details>


### [260] [Hallucination-Aware Diffusion Sampling for Inverse Problems via Robust Prior Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02331)
*Pengfei Jin,Yiqi Tian,Kailong Fan,Bingjie Qi,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出鲁棒先验更新（RPU）模块，通过探测扩散先验更新的局部稳定性并重新锚定位移，减少扩散逆求解器中的测量条件幻觉，在不改变测量更新的情况下提升重建结果的实例保真度。在FFHQ和ImageNet上的实验表明，RPU在PSNR、LPIPS及人工判断中均优于DPS基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的逆问题求解器虽然能生成真实感的重建结果，但"真实感"本身并不保证恢复的细节有测量数据支持。现有方法存在的测量条件幻觉（measurement-conditioned hallucination）会导致视觉上有意义的内容与测量实例不一致或不可信。作者旨在分析这一失败的根源并提出改进方案。

Method: 将基于贝叶斯规则的扩散逆求解器分解为先验更新和测量条件化两步，指出幻觉内容可通过先验侧提议在测量校正之前进入。据此提出RPU（Robust Prior Update）模块：探测扩散先验更新的局部稳定性，将产生的位移重新锚定到当前迭代点，同时保持测量更新不变。将RPU实例化到DPS中。

Result: 在FFHQ上，RPU在框内修复、高斯去模糊和运动去模糊任务中PSNR和LPIPS均优于DPS。人工判断中，FFHQ框内修复任务RPU获得91.9%的盲目非平局多数偏好和91.1%的辅助偏好；ImageNet高斯去模糊实验非平局情况偏好RPU。

Conclusion: 鲁棒化先验更新能够提升扩散逆求解器的实例保真度，尤其是在先验塑造弱约束内容时效果显著。

Abstract: Diffusion-based inverse problem solvers can produce realistic reconstructions, but realism alone does not ensure that the recovered details are supported by the measurement. We study this failure as measurement-conditioned hallucination: visually meaningful content that is either implausible or inconsistent with the measured instance. Our analysis separates Bayes-rule-based diffusion inverse solvers into a prior update and a measurement-conditioning step, showing that hallucinated content can enter through the prior-side proposal before the measurement correction is applied. Motivated by this view, we propose Robust Prior Update (RPU), a solver-level module that probes the local stability of the diffusion prior update, re-anchors the resulting displacement at the current iterate, and leaves the measurement update unchanged. We instantiate RPU in DPS and evaluate it on FFHQ and ImageNet inverse problems using automatic metrics and human faithfulness studies. On FFHQ, RPU improves PSNR and LPIPS over DPS across box inpainting, Gaussian deblurring, and motion deblurring. In human judgments, RPU receives 91.9% of blind non-tie majority preferences and 91.1% of ground-truth-assisted non-tie preferences on FFHQ box inpainting, while the ImageNet Gaussian reader study is tie-heavy but favors RPU among non-tie cases. These results support a targeted claim: robustifying the prior update can improve instance faithfulness in diffusion inverse solvers, especially when the prior shapes weakly constrained content.

</details>


### [261] [Detecting Pen-In-Air States from Video: A Proof-of-Concept Toward Complementary Handwriting Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02342)
*Lauren Sismeiro,Remy Plastre,Binbin Xu,Frederic Puyjarinet,Gerard Dray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于俯视视频的混合管道，通过YOLO笔尖追踪、运动特征提取和机器学习分类，在不依赖数位板近场传感的情况下推断笔触状态（Pen-Up/Pen-Down），在手动标注的手写视频数据集上使用留一视频交叉验证取得了F2分数最高0.805的可靠事件级检测结果，验证了视频作为低成本、非侵入式互补手段用于书写动态分析的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数位板只能捕捉书写面上方短距离内的笔悬空行为，无法记录高抬笔的空中运动，而空中运动信息对评估书写障碍（如书写困难）具有重要价值。因此，研究者探索俯视视频作为补充信息源，在不依赖数位板近场感应的条件下推断笔触状态。

Method: 提出了一种可解释的混合管道：首先使用基于YOLO的检测器进行笔尖追踪，然后提取运动学特征，最后采用机器学习分类器进行笔触状态分类。使用了人工逐帧标注的手写视频小规模数据集，并采用留一视频交叉验证（LOVO）进行评估。

Result: 该方法实现了可靠的事件级Pen-Up段检测，F2分数最高达0.805，该指标在筛查场景下侧重召回率，与评估目标一致。

Conclusion: 视频基笔触检测作为数位板的低成本、非侵入式互补手段具有可行性，为未来大规模研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Dynamic aspects of handwriting are critical for assessing developmental disorders such as dysgraphia and are typically captured using digitizing tablets. However, tablet-based sensing restricts analysis of Pen-Up behavior to a short proximity range above the writing surface, potentially missing high-lift in-air movements. As a proof of concept, we investigate whether top-view video can provide a complementary source of information for inferring pen-contact states without relying on tablet proximity sensing. We propose an interpretable hybrid pipeline combining pen-tip tracking using a YOLO-based detector with kinematic feature extraction and machine learning classification. A pilot dataset of diverse handwriting videos was manually annotated at the frame level and evaluation used a Leave-One-Video-Out (LOVO) protocol. The method achieved reliable event-level detection of Pen-Up segments, with an F_2 score up to 0.805, consistent with the emphasis on recall in a screening-oriented setting. These results support the feasibility of video-based Pen-Up detection as a low-cost and non-intrusive complement to digitizing tablets, and provide a foundation for future large-scale studies.

</details>


### [262] [VEDAL: Variational Error-Driven Asynchronous Learning for 3D Gaussian Splatting Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02346)
*Aoduo Li,Jiancheng Li,Huan Ye,Hongjian Xu,Shiting Wu,Xiujun Zhang,Zimeng Li,Xuhang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEDAL通过变分自由能最小化框架，将3D高斯泼溅中的高斯原语修剪形式化，利用预测误差门控机制和变分不确定性头实现异步修剪，在Mip-NeRF 360等数据集上达到5.2倍压缩且PSNR仅下降0.31 dB，同时保持185 FPS的实时渲染速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现高质量新视角合成和实时渲染，但数百万高斯原语导致巨大内存消耗；现有修剪方法依赖启发式重要性分数或同步批量更新，导致压缩次优和训练不稳定。

Method: 提出VEDAL框架：(1) 预测误差门控机制，基于每个高斯的重建不确定性异步激活修剪；(2) 变分不确定性头，将修剪决策建模为具有可学习先验的潜变量。通过变分自由能目标在信息论视角下自然平衡重建保真度与模型复杂度。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks&Temples和Deep Blending上，VEDAL实现5.2倍压缩，PSNR仅下降0.31 dB；相比PUP 3D-GS在更高压缩比下PSNR提升0.05 dB，相比LightGaussian在可比质量下PSNR提升0.35 dB，渲染速度达185 FPS。

Conclusion: VEDAL是一个原则性的3DGS高斯修剪框架，通过变分自由能最小化有效平衡了压缩率与渲染质量，在显著降低内存消耗的同时保持了实时渲染性能。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves remarkable novel view synthesis quality with real-time rendering, yet suffers from excessive memory consumption due to millions of Gaussian primitives. Existing pruning methods rely on heuristic importance scores or synchronous batch updates, leading to suboptimal compression and training instability. We propose VEDAL, a principled framework that formulates Gaussian pruning as variational free energy minimization. Our approach introduces (1) a prediction-error gating mechanism that asynchronously activates pruning based on per-Gaussian reconstruction uncertainty, and (2) a variational uncertainty head that models pruning decisions as latent variables with learnable priors. The free energy objective naturally balances reconstruction fidelity against model complexity through an information-theoretic lens. Extensive experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks&Temples, and Deep Blending demonstrate that VEDAL achieves 5.2x compression with only 0.31 dB PSNR drop, outperforming PUP 3D-GS by +0.05 dB at a higher compression ratio and LightGaussian by +0.35 dB at comparable quality, while maintaining real-time rendering at 185 FPS.

</details>


### [263] [TROPHIES: Temporal Reconstruction of Places, Humans, and Cameras from Multi-view Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02350)
*Jinpeng Liu,Yukang Xu,Yutong Li,Xingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TROPHIES提出从多视角视频中联合重建动态人体、静态场景和相机位姿的统一框架，通过时空推理、人体感知注意力和全局对齐优化，实现全局一致且物理可信的4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设单视角输入或将人体、场景、相机解耦处理，导致无法恢复一致的几何结构、稳定的运动以及物理对齐的轨迹。因此需要一个新的统一任务：从多视角视频中进行人体-场景-相机的联合重建。

Method: 提出TROPHIES统一框架，包含：1) Human Branch：通过时空推理对人体进行建模；2) Scene Branch：利用人体感知注意力重建静态几何；3) 全局对齐与优化模块：通过尺度一致性、接触先验和跨视图时间一致性将两个分支耦合。

Result: 在EgoHuman和EgoExo4D数据集上，TROPHIES实现了全局对齐、物理可信的4D重建，在全局保真度和人体-场景一致性上持续优于现有范式。

Conclusion: TROPHIES证明了从多视角视频中统一重建人体、场景和相机是可行且有效的，为4D综合感知提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing humans and their surrounding environments in a globally consistent 4D space is essential for comprehensive perception. However, prior works typically assume single-view inputs or decouple humans, scenes, and cameras, making them unable to recover coherent geometry, stable motion, and physically aligned trajectories. These limitations motivate us to introduce a new task: unified human-scene-camera reconstruction from multi-view videos, which aims to jointly estimate dynamic humans, static scenes, and camera poses in one global coordinate frame. We propose TROPHIES--Temporal Reconstruction of Places, Humans, and Cameras from Multi-view Videos-a unified framework tailored for this task. TROPHIES features a Human Branch that models humans through temporal and spatial reasoning, and a Scene Branch that reconstructs static geometry with human-aware attention. A global alignment and optimization module couples both branches by enforcing scale consistency, contact priors, and cross-view temporal coherence. Experiments on EgoHuman and EgoExo4D demonstrate that TROPHIES achieves globally aligned, physically plausible 4D reconstructions and consistently outperforms existing paradigms in both global fidelity and human-scene consistency.

</details>


### [264] [Multi-modal Video Representation Alignment for Robust Self-supervised Driver Distraction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02352)
*David J. Lerch,Livien Majer,Zeyun Zhong,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态全局对齐框架，通过软目标（基于循环一致性得分）和加权机制（基于相似性分布）分别处理有问题的负样本和不可靠的正样本，在Drive&Act数据集上优于现有基线方法，实现鲁棒的驾驶员分心检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对比学习（如InfoNCE）假设所有负样本等量信息、所有正样本可靠，这在多模态数据中因视角变化、遮挡或模态间语义重叠而频繁被违反，导致多模态视频表示学习不鲁棒。

Method: 提出多模态全局对齐框架：(1) 基于循环一致性得分生成软目标，放松硬负样本假设；(2) 基于相似性分布的加权机制缓解噪声/错误正样本的影响；(3) 将传统成对对齐扩展为全局多模态对齐，聚合所有模态对间的对齐信息。

Result: 在Drive&Act数据集上，该方法在RGB、IR、Depth、Skeleton四种模态上一致优于成对对齐和现有全局对齐基线；跨视角消融实验表明对未见相机视角具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展且有效的自监督全局多模态表示学习方案，能够实现可靠的驾驶员分心检测，并推动真实世界多模态视频理解的发展。

Abstract: Robust self-supervised learning of multi-modal video representations is critical for real-world applications such as driver distraction detection, where multiple sensors provide complementary but noisy signals. Conventional contrastive objectives, such as InfoNCE, assume all negatives are equally informative and all positives are reliable. However, this assumption is frequently violated in multi-modal data due to viewpoint changes, occlusions, or semantic overlap across modalities. In this work, we propose a novel framework for multi-modal global alignment that addresses these challenges by jointly modeling faulty negatives and unreliable or faulty positives. We introduce soft targets derived from cycle-consistency scores to relax the hard-negative assumption, and a weighting mechanism based on similarity distributions to mitigate the impact of noisy or faulty positives. Our approach extends traditional pairwise alignment to a principled global multi-modal setting, aggregating alignment information across all modality pairs. We evaluate our method on the Drive&Act dataset, demonstrating that it consistently outperforms both pairwise and existing global alignment baselines across RGB, IR, Depth, and Skeleton modalities. Cross-view ablation studies further show strong generalization to unseen camera perspectives, highlighting the robustness of our representations. Overall, our framework provides a scalable and effective solution for self-supervised global multi-modal representation learning, enabling reliable driver distraction detection and pioneering in real-world multi-modal video understanding. Our code will be published on GitHub.

</details>


### [265] [Do Multimodal Agents Really Benefit from Tool Use? A Systematic Study of Capability Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02357)
*Garvin Guo,Donglei Yu,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Xinpei Zhao,Huaxing Liu,Qinghao Wang,Minpeng Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑工具增强多模态智能体（Thyme和DeepEyesV2）的性能提升是否真正源于工具使用能力。实验表明，工具访问并未带来一致的聚合改进，工具单独解决的问题极少（DeepEyesV2仅7%，Thyme仅4%未被非工具设置解决），且完整的工具使用循环并不稳定优于工具调用格式或执行结果本身。智能体更多学到了工具调用模式而非工具贡献的实际能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将工具增强智能体的性能提升归因于其学会了使用工具，但本文认为仅凭工具调用痕迹无法判断工具是否提供了答案关键信息，因此需要严谨区分"工具可用性"与"工具实际扩展了解题能力"。

Method: 以Thyme和DeepEyesV2两个代表性"图文思考"智能体为研究对象，在现实世界理解、OCR、图表理解和数学推理等任务上，分别将其与无工具版本和同源纯文本推理器进行比较。通过聚合指标、生成成本、工具独解集分析以及机制消融实验（解构工具使用循环的各组件）进行系统评估。

Result: 1) 工具访问带来的聚合提升不一致且微小；2) 工具使用未可靠降低生成token成本；3) DeepEyesV2仅7%、Thyme仅4%的问题为工具独解；4) 完整工具循环并未稳定优于工具调用格式或执行结果单独使用。

Conclusion: 被分析智能体更可靠地学到了工具调用模式，而非工具带来的实际能力增益。评估时应区分工具可用性与工具是否真正扩展了智能体的解题能力。

Abstract: Tool-augmented multimodal agents show strong benchmark gains, often taken as evidence that agents have learned to use tools. We argue that this interpretation can be premature: a tool-call trace alone does not show whether the tool supplied answer-critical information. We study two representative ``thinking with images'' agents, Thyme and DeepEyesV2, across real-world understanding, OCR, chart understanding, and mathematical reasoning. Each agent is compared with its Tool-Free counterpart and with a Pure-Text Reasoner trained from the same source pool without tool-calling trajectories. Tool access yields little consistent aggregate improvement, does not reliably reduce generated-token cost, and leaves only a small tool-only solved set: 93% of DeepEyesV2's tool-solved problems and 96% of Thyme's are also solved by at least one non-tool setting. Mechanism ablations further show that the full tool-use loop does not consistently outperform either the tool-call format or the returned execution result alone. In the settings we study, the analyzed agents appear to learn tool-calling patterns more reliably than tool-contributed capabilities, suggesting that evaluation should distinguish tool availability from whether tools actually expand what agents can solve.

</details>


### [266] [PRIMA: Boosting Animal Mesh Recovery with Biological Priors and Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02366)
*Xiaohang Yu,Ti Wang,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIMA是一个针对严重物种和姿态不平衡下鲁棒3D四足动物网格恢复的框架，通过生物先验（BioCLIP嵌入）、测试时自适应（TTA）策略以及大规模伪3D数据集Quadruped3D的构建，在多个基准上实现了最先进的结果，尤其提升了稀有物种和挑战性姿态的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动物重建方法因3D监督有限和长尾物种分布，常退化为预测平均形状和姿态，对稀有动物和罕见关节姿态泛化能力差。

Method: 1) 引入BioCLIP嵌入作为生物学先验，注入语义和形态学知识以提升形状预测的准确性和泛化性；2) 提出测试时自适应（TTA）策略，利用2D重投影约束和辅助关键点引导优化SMAL预测，并生成高质量伪3D标注；3) 基于TTA框架构建大规模伪3D数据集Quadruped3D，覆盖多样物种和姿态变化。

Result: 在Animal3D、CtrlAni3D、Quadruped2D和Animal Kingdom数据集上达到最先进性能，尤其在稀有物种和挑战性姿态上提升显著。

Conclusion: 生物学先验和自适应驱动的数据扩展对于可扩展、泛化性强的动物网格恢复至关重要。

Abstract: We present PRIMA (*PRI*ors for *M*esh *A*daptation), a framework for robust 3D quadruped mesh recovery under severe species and pose imbalance. Existing animal reconstruction methods often regress toward mean shapes and poses due to limited 3D supervision and long-tailed species distributions, resulting in poor generalization to underrepresented animals and rare articulations. PRIMA addresses this challenge through three key contributions. First, we incorporate BioCLIP embeddings as biological priors to inject semantic and morphological knowledge into the reconstruction process, enabling more accurate and generalizable shape prediction across diverse quadrupeds. Second, we introduce a test-time adaptation (TTA) strategy that refines SMAL predictions using 2D reprojection constraints together with auxiliary keypoint guidance, improving pose and shape estimation while enabling the generation of high-quality pseudo-3D annotations from existing 2D datasets. Third, leveraging this TTA framework, we construct Quadruped3D, a large-scale pseudo-3D dataset that covers diverse species and pose variations to systematically improve model performance. Extensive experiments on Animal3D, CtrlAni3D, Quadruped2D, and Animal Kingdom demonstrate that PRIMA achieves state-of-the-art results, with particularly strong improvements on underrepresented species and challenging poses. Our results highlight the importance of biological priors and adaptation-driven data expansion for scalable and generalizable animal mesh recovery. Code is available at https://github.com/AdaptiveMotorControlLab/PRIMA.

</details>


### [267] [Honey, I Shrunk the Arc de Triomphe!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02379)
*Yuanbo Xiangli,Hanyu Chen,Xueqing Tsang,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MetricScenes数据集，通过从互联网照片集和立体图像中收集带度量尺度的野外场景数据，采用两阶段泊松补全方法提升深度图质量，并在MoGe-2模型上微调，有效缓解了单目几何估计中的"尺度坍缩"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量尺度单目几何估计基础模型存在"尺度坍缩"现象（远处地标和广阔景观被度量级低估），其根源在于训练数据瓶颈：现有度量尺度数据集受硬件限制，要么是车辆采集的LiDAR数据或短距离室内扫描，要么是缺乏物理世界语义复杂性的合成数据。

Method: 1) 构建MetricScenes数据集：从互联网照片集和立体图像中收集多样化的野外场景，利用现成方法估计相机位姿和初始深度图，从地理标签元数据和已知立体相机基线恢复绝对尺度。2) 提出两阶段泊松补全方法提升MetricScenes深度图质量。3) 在MoGe-2模型上微调。

Result: 微调后的MoGe-2显著缓解了尺度坍缩，在无约束、开放域场景中实现了更优的度量精度，同时在标准基准上保持最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过构建包含真实世界度量尺度的大规模野外数据集，能够有效克服数据瓶颈导致的尺度坍缩问题，提升单目几何估计模型在开放场景中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Metric scale monocular geometry estimation has seen significant progress through large-scale data aggregation, yet current foundation models suffer from a persistent ''scale-collapse'' phenomenon: distant landmarks and vast landscapes are metrically underestimated. We hypothesize that this performance gap stems from a training data bottleneck, where existing metric-scale datasets are hardware-constrained to homogenous vehicle-captured LiDAR or short-range indoor scans, or consist of synthetic data that lacks the semantic complexity of the physical world. To bridge this gap, we curate a new metrically-grounded, in-the-wild dataset that we call MetricScenes, gathered from a variety of sources including Internet photo collections and stereo imagery. We estimate camera poses and initial depth maps for each scene using off-the-shelf methods, and recover absolute scale from geo-tagged metadata as well as known stereo camera baselines. We also improve the quality of depth maps derived from MetricScenes via a new two-stage Poisson completion method. Fine-tuning MoGe-2 on our dataset significantly mitigates scale-collapse and achieves superior metric accuracy in unconstrained, open-domain scenes while maintaining state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [268] [Explainable Forensics of Manipulated Segments in Untrimmed Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02402)
*Yue Feng,Jingjing Li,Qijia Lu,Wei Ji,Jingrou Zhang,Fei Shen,Xiao Li,Yizhen Jia,Qiang Chen,Limin Wang,Wentong Li,Jie Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出时序AI生成片段定位与解释任务（TASLE），构建了包含12,472个未修剪长视频的大规模基准数据集，并提出了MSLoc基线方法，用于在长视频中检测、定位和解释AI生成内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频取证方法主要处理短视频片段，无法应对AI生成内容稀疏嵌入在真实长视频中的现实场景，缺乏针对长视频中局部AI生成篡改的检测、定位和可解释分析能力。

Method: 提出了TASLE基准数据集（12,472个未修剪长视频，含多样篡改模式和丰富标注），以及MSLoc基线方法，采用粗到细的取证策略，包含边界敏感提议生成模块用于高效长视频扫描，以及基于MLLM的精修模块用于精确边界定位和可解释推理。

Result: 实验验证了MSLoc基线方法的有效性，表明片段级可解释取证对于长视频AI生成内容分析的重要性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了长视频AI生成内容时序定位与解释的空白，为长视频取证研究提供了标准化基准和方法框架。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of AI-driven video generation has transformed content creation, while simultaneously increasing the risk of misinformation through localized manipulations in long-form videos. Existing video forensic methods predominantly operate on short, independent clips, and thus fail to capture realistic scenarios where AI-generated content is sparsely embedded within otherwise authentic footage. To bridge this gap, we formulate the task of Temporal AI-Generated Segment Localization and Explanation, which targets authenticity detection, temporal localization, and interpretable analysis of manipulated segments in untrimmed long videos. We further introduce TASLE, a large-scale benchmark comprising 12,472 untrimmed videos with diverse manipulation patterns and rich annotation signals, including temporal boundaries, authenticity labels, and segment-level rationales. In addition, we propose MSLoc, a coarse-to-fine forensic baseline that combines a boundary-sensitive proposal generation module for efficient long-video scanning with an MLLM-based refinement module for precise boundary localization and interpretable reasoning. Experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed baseline, highlighting the importance of segment-level explainable forensics for long-form AI-generated video analysis. Our dataset and code are publicly available at https://debby-0527.github.io/TASLE.

</details>


### [269] [Edge Prediction for Roof Wireframe Reconstruction with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02406)
*Gustav Hanning,Ludvig Dillén,Jonathan Astermark,Johanna Lidholm,Viktor Larsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端Transformer编解码器架构，用于从稀疏SfM点云和地面语义分割/深度图中重建3D房屋屋顶线框模型。该方法通过语义优先级动态子采样点云，融合Gestalt和ADE20k特征，利用交叉注意力机制解码为3D线框边，在HoHo 22k数据集上达到0.6476 HSS，获S23DR Challenge 2026第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决S23DR Challenge 2026的挑战目标：从稀疏SfM点云和地面级语义分割及深度图中重建3D房屋屋顶线框模型，克服传统手工方法或已有学习方法在此任务上的性能不足。

Method: 采用端到端Transformer编解码器架构（受DETR启发）。首先对稀疏SfM点云基于语义优先级进行动态子采样，并增强Gestalt和ADE20k类别特征；随后将点投影到冻结自编码器的潜在特征图中获取Gestalt特征编码以增加分割上下文；最后通过可学习查询嵌入经交叉注意力机制直接解码为3D线框边。

Result: 在HoHo 22k数据集上显著优于手工和学习的基线方法，混合结构评分（HSS）达0.6476，在挑战赛私有排行榜上位列第二。

Conclusion: 所提出的Transformer-based方法能有效融合几何与语义信息，实现从稀疏点云到3D屋顶线框的端到端重建，验证了注意力机制在此类任务中的优越性。

Abstract: This paper presents a competitive solution to the S23DR Challenge 2026, which aims to reconstruct 3D house roof wireframe models from sparse SfM point clouds and ground-level semantic segmentations and depth maps. Our proposed method utilizes an end-to-end Transformer encoder-decoder architecture inspired by DETR. To effectively process the geometric and semantic data, the sparse SfM point cloud input is dynamically subsampled based on semantic priority and augmented with Gestalt and ADE20k class features. To further increase segmentation context, we fuse the point features with additional Gestalt feature encodings which are obtained by projecting the points into latent feature maps produced by a frozen autoencoder. Learned query embeddings are then decoded directly into 3D wireframe edges via cross-attention mechanisms. Evaluated on the "HoHo 22k" dataset, our approach significantly outperforms both handcrafted and learned baselines, achieving a Hybrid Structure Score (HSS) of 0.6476 and securing the second-highest position on the challenge's private leaderboard.

</details>


### [270] [GC-MoE: Genomics-Guided Cell-Type-Specific Mixture of Experts for Histology-Based Single-Cell Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02424)
*Kaito Shiku,Ahtisham Fazeel Abbasi,Ryoma Bise,Yuichiro Iwashita,Kazuya Nishimura,Andreas Dengel,Muhammad Nabeel Asim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GC-MoE框架，通过细胞类型特异性混合专家模型和共表达感知预测器，从组织学图像和细胞位置预测单细胞基因表达，在公共数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组织学到空间转录组学方法主要预测包含多个细胞的局部区域（斑点级别）的表达谱，无法捕捉细胞间表达变异性。需要一种方法能从组织学图像中估计单细胞级别的基因表达，以降低昂贵单细胞ST测量的需求。

Method: 提出GC-MoE框架：(1) 路由网络估计细胞类型概率；(2) 细胞类型特异性专家混合模型进行基因表达预测；(3) 细胞类型特异性共表达感知预测器(CAP)编码细胞类型依赖的基因程序；(4) 轻量级细胞间交互注意力(C2CA)模块捕获邻域细胞上下文信息。

Result: 在公共单细胞ST数据集上的实验和消融研究显示，该方法在单细胞级别和适配的斑点级别基线上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: GC-MoE通过显式建模细胞类型特异性基因表达程序和细胞间相互作用，有效解决了从组织学图像估计单细胞基因表达的问题，为降低单细胞空间转录组学测量成本提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Histology-based single-cell spatial transcriptomics (ST) estimation aims to predict gene expression for individual cells from histopathological images and cell locations, reducing the need for costly single-cell ST measurements. Unlike existing histology-to-ST methods that mainly predict spot-level profiles for local regions containing multiple cells, this task requires modeling cell-to-cell expression variability, which is strongly structured by cell type. We propose Genomics-Guided Cell-Type-Specific Mixture-of-Experts (GC-MoE), which estimates cell-type probabilities with a routing network and softly combines cell-type-specific experts for gene expression prediction. To further encode cell-type-dependent gene programs, we introduce the Cell-Type-Specific Co-Expression-Aware Predictor (CAP), together with a lightweight Cell-to-Cell Interaction Attention (C2CA) module for neighboring-cell context. Experiments and ablations on public single-cell ST datasets show consistent improvements over existing single-cell and adapted spot-level baselines.

</details>


### [271] [Geometry-Aware Implicit Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02436)
*Zhengxuan Wei,Xu Guo,Xinghui Li,Xunzhi Xiang,Min Wei,Yiran Zhu,Qiulin Wang,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Xiangwang Hou,Qi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GIM-World提出了一种几何感知的隐式记忆框架，通过轻量级Transformer编码器将可变长度历史压缩为固定大小的记忆令牌，利用可冻结基础模型的几何头在训练时蒸馏3D场景结构，并采用信息引导剪枝规则控制编码成本。推理时丢弃几何教师，保留轻量级记忆模块。在MIND数据集上，该方法在长程几何和视觉一致性上优于显式和隐式记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在处理长程 rollout 时存在显式记忆（帧或在线3D重建）的启发式检索错误、冗余外观存储或重建伪影问题，以及隐式记忆缺乏对跨视角场景几何的显式约束。

Method: 1) 轻量级Transformer编码器将可变长度历史压缩为固定大小的记忆令牌；2) 相机可查询的几何头在训练时从冻结的基础模型中将3D场景结构蒸馏到记忆中；3) 信息引导的剪枝规则控制编码成本随历史增长而保持有界。

Result: 在MIND数据集上的实验表明，GIM-World在保持长程几何和视觉一致性方面优于显式记忆和隐式记忆基线。

Conclusion: 提出了一种几何感知的隐式记忆框架，通过知识蒸馏将3D结构信息编码到紧凑的记忆表示中，在保持推理时轻量化的同时显著提升了长程视频世界模型的一致性和可控性。

Abstract: Video world models aim to simulate controllable visual environments, but long-horizon rollouts depend on what the model remembers after observations leave its native context window. Explicit memories retain frames or online 3D reconstructions, which can suffer from heuristic retrieval errors, redundant appearance storage, or reconstruction artifacts. Implicit memories compress history into a compact state, but existing designs are not explicitly constrained to encode cross-view scene geometry. We propose GIM-World, a geometry-aware implicit memory framework for video world models. A lightweight transformer encoder compresses variable-length history into fixed-size memory tokens, a camera-queryable geometry head distills 3D scene structure from a frozen foundation model into the memory during training, and an information-guided pruning rule keeps encoding cost bounded as history grows. The geometry teacher is discarded at inference, leaving a lightweight memory module. Experiments on MIND show that GIM-World better preserves long-horizon geometric and visual consistency than both explicit- and implicit-memory baselines.

</details>


### [272] [Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Reference Conditioning for Identity-Preserving Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02441)
*Yuheng Chen,Teng Hu,Yuji Wang,Qingdong He,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ST-DRC提出了一种空间-时间解耦参考条件框架，通过潜在上下文特征注入、TASS-RoPE位置编码、外观不变参考增强和三分支参考无分类器引导策略，实现了高身份保真度和文本对齐的轻量级视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPVG方法难以平衡高层语义控制和低层身份保真度。

Method: 1) 潜在上下文特征注入：参考图像经视频VAE编码后与噪声潜变量拼接；2) TASS-RoPE：时间邻近但空间偏移的位置编码方案；3) 外观不变参考增强+人脸引导身份目标；4) 三分支参考无分类器引导策略。

Result: 在身份保持、提示对齐、时间一致性和视频质量方面表现优异，基于LTX-2.3的轻量设计在人脸身份保持视频生成赛道中排名前列。

Conclusion: 空间-时间解耦参考条件策略有效平衡了身份保真度与文本控制，验证了框架设计的有效性。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation (IPVG) aims to synthesize high-fidelity videos that follow text prompts while faithfully preserving a reference identity. Despite recent progress, existing IPVG methods still struggle to balance high-level semantic control and low-level identity fidelity. To bridge this gap, we propose ST-DRC, an effective Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Reference Conditioning framework for identity-preserving text-to-video generation. At the framework level, ST-DRC performs latent in-context feature injection by encoding the reference image with the video VAE and concatenating it with noisy video latents, enabling rich low-level identity details to be accessed without additional adapters. To separate identity-aware reference retrieval from appearance copying, we introduce TASS-RoPE, a Temporal-Adjacent Spatial-Shifted RoPE scheme that places reference tokens near the video sequence in time but shifts them in space, allowing reference information to flow through spatio-temporal attention while suppressing pixel-level copy-paste shortcuts. To further prevent shortcut learning and strengthen the otherwise diluted identity supervision in the diffusion objective, we combine appearance-invariant reference augmentation with face-guided identity objectives, encouraging the model to preserve identity under variations in color, pose, and layout. At inference time, we introduce a three-stream reference classifier-free guidance strategy that independently controls text adherence and reference fidelity. Experiments demonstrate that ST-DRC achieves strong identity preservation, prompt alignment, temporal consistency, and video quality with a lightweight design built on LTX-2.3. Our method ranks among the top submissions in the facial identity-preserving video generation track, validating the effectiveness of spatial-temporal decoupled reference conditioning.

</details>


### [273] [Reason-Then-Retrieve for CoVR-R with Structured Edit Prompts and Dense-Sparse Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02450)
*DongQing Liu,MengShi Qi,HongWei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVR-R框架，用于推理感知的组合视频检索。采用Qwen3.5-27B构建零样本"先推理后检索"流程，在验证集和测试集上分别达到R@1=80.81和89.73。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合视频检索中目标视频并非直接描述，而需从对象身份、动作顺序、最终状态、手部交互和场景转换等细粒度变化中推理得出，现有方法难以处理这种推理性检索任务。

Method: 围绕Qwen3.5-27B构建零样本先推理后检索流程：对每个图库视频生成结构化描述和稠密嵌入（基于生成token隐状态加权重池化）；对每个查询先对参考视频和指令进行编辑推理，生成目标视频描述，其隐状态作为查询嵌入；补充TF-IDF分支进行稠密检索并融合排序。

Result: 验证集：R@1=80.81, R@5=94.86, R@10=97.11, R@50=98.59；测试集：R@1=89.73, R@5=95.79, R@10=96.63, R@50=97.98。

Conclusion: 提出的零样本推理感知检索方法在组合视频检索任务上取得了优异性能，验证了先推理再检索范式的有效性。

Abstract: CoVR-R studies reason-aware composed video retrieval: given a reference video and an edit instruction, the system must retrieve the target video that satisfies the edit. The main difficulty is that the target is not described directly; it must be inferred from fine-grained changes in object identity, action order, final state, hand interaction, and scene transition. We build a zero-shot reason-then-retrieve pipeline around Qwen3.5-27B. For each gallery video, the model generates a retrieval-oriented structured description and a dense embedding by pooling generated-token hidden states with token-dependent weights. For each query, the model first performs edit reasoning over the reference video and instruction, then generates a target-video description whose hidden states serve as the query embedding. We complement dense retrieval with a TF-IDF branch over the generated texts and fuse the two rankings with split-specific weights. On validation, the current best submission reaches 80.81 at R@1, 94.86 at R@5, 97.11 at R@10, and 98.59 at R@50. On the blind test split, it reaches 89.73 at R@1, 95.79 at R@5, 96.63 at R@10, and 97.98 at R@50.

</details>


### [274] [Initialization is Half the Battle: Generating Diverse Images from a Guidance Potential Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02453)
*Xiang Li,Dianbo Liu,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DivIn方法，通过从引导势后验中采样初始噪声而非标准高斯初始化，利用朗之万动力学将初始噪声引导至多样性丰富区域，在不改变生成轨迹的情况下提升扩散模型和流匹配模型的生成多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型尽管保真度高但常遭遇模式坍塌，现有提升多样性的策略主要聚焦于干预生成轨迹，而忽略了标准高斯初始化对引导势景观不可知、导致轨迹坍缩到主导模式这一关键问题。

Method: 从引导势后验中公式化地选择初始噪声，有效重加权先验使其偏向多样性丰富区域；利用朗之万动力学主动导航初始化景观，将初始噪声驱离坍塌区域同时锚定在有效数据流形上。

Result: 在类别到图像和文本到图像场景中均表现出优越性能；与基于轨迹的方法正交，结合使用可显著扩展多样性-质量帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: DivIn是一种推理时多样性增强方法，兼容扩散模型和流匹配模型，且能与现有轨迹方法互补，进一步优化多样性-质量权衡。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable fidelity of generative models, they frequently suffer from mode collapse. Existing strategies for enhancing diversity predominantly focus on intervening during the generation trajectory. We identify a critical oversight that the standard Gaussian initialization often causes trajectories to collapse into dominant modes because it is agnostic to the guidance potential landscape. In this work, we formulate selecting the initial noise from a guidance potential posterior, which effectively re-weights the prior towards diversity-rich regions. To sample from this distribution efficiently, we introduce Diversity-inducing Initialization (DivIn), which leverages Langevin dynamics to actively navigate the initialization landscape, steering initial noise away from collapsing regions while anchoring them to the valid data manifold. Our method serves as an inference-time diversity enhancement compatible with both diffusion and flow matching models. Extensive experiments show that DivIn exhibits a superior performance in both class-to-image and text-to-image scenarios. Furthermore, we highlight that as DivIn is orthogonal to trajectory-based methods, combining them significantly expands the diversity-quality Pareto frontier beyond what either achieves in isolation.

</details>


### [275] [Active Exploring like a Pigeon: Reinforcing Spatial Reasoning via Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02459)
*Wei Deng,Xianlin Zhang,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受鸽子认知地图启发的主动空间推理管线，通过动态认知地图参数化场景布局，并引入空间断言代码（SAC）提供密集奖励信号，在MindCube基准上达到80.5%的准确率，相比最优方法在Rotation子集上提升53.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在空间推理中只能作为被动观察者，且强化学习方法依赖稀疏奖励，难以应对复杂推理任务。

Method: 提出动态认知地图用于持久化场景布局参数（物体位置和朝向），并设计空间断言代码（SAC）以Python表达式编程式描述空间关系，通过与动态认知地图协作提供密集奖励信号，结合监督微调和强化微调进行优化。

Result: 在MindCube基准上总体准确率80.5%，在Rotation子集上超越现有最优方法29.5个准确点（相对提升53.2%）。

Conclusion: 受鸽子认知地图启发的主动空间推理管线能显著提升VLM的空间推理能力，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Enabling Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to perform spatial reasoning remains challenging. Existing approaches treat VLMs as passive observers, which is difficult for real-world applications. Moreover, reinforcement learning methods rely on sparse rewards, limiting their effectiveness for complex reasoning tasks. Inspired by pigeons' building and exploiting cognitive maps for navigation, we propose a novel agentic pipeline for spatial reasoning. First, we introduce a new \emph{dynamic cognitive map} parameterizing scene layout as object positions and orientations, serving as persistent memory for new observations. Second, we propose a novel \emph{Spatial Assertion Codes (SAC)}, Python expressions programmatically describing spatial relationships. By collaborating with the dynamic cognitive map, SAC enables verification of intermediate reasoning steps, providing dense reward signals. We optimize the model via supervised and reinforcement finetuning. Experiments on the MindCube benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art performance with \emph{80.5\%} overall accuracy, outperforming the best current method by \emph{29.5} accuracy points (a relative improvement of \emph{53.2\%}) on the challenging \textsc{Rotation} subset. Our code and data are open-sourced at https://github.com/dw-dengwei/active-spatial-reasoning.git.

</details>


### [276] [MASER: Modality-Adaptive Specialist Routing for Embodied 3D Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02463)
*Hilton Raj,Vishnuram AV*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MASER提出了一种轻量级框架，通过训练五个不同的模态适配器并学习神经路由策略，在推理时根据问题语义选择最佳适配器，在Open3D-VQA基准上以单次适配器调用实现了51.3%的Oracle一致率，优于随机森林基线（43.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM仅针对单一模态进行微调，完全忽略了问题语义可能偏好不同于微调模态的其他模态，导致在3D空间问答任务中无法自适应选择最优模态。

Method: 训练共享VLM骨干的五个模态适配器（RGB、点云、深度图、相机位姿、自然语言）；使用冻结的句子编码器将问题转化为嵌入，通过小型MLP学习神经路由策略，该MLP在Oracle适配器准确率标签上训练；推理时根据问题语义选择最佳适配器。

Result: Open3D-VQA基准上，点云在51.5%的案例中表现最优；MASER路由达到51.3%的Oracle一致率，显著优于随机森林消融实验的43.5%，且每次推理仅需一次适配器调用。

Conclusion: 没有单一模态在所有场景中普遍最优，MASER通过问题驱动的模态自适应路由有效提升了3D VQA性能，验证了多模态适配器结合语义路由策略的可行性。

Abstract: In 3D environments, Embodied Agents answer spatially relevant questions through reasoning from a mixture of modalities including natural language, RGB images, point clouds, depth maps and camera poses. Existing Vision-Language models (VLMs) are fine-tuned over a single modality. This completely ignores the question semantics which may favor a different modality than the finetuned modality. To address this, we propose MASER (Modality-Adaptive SpEcialist Routing), a lightweight framework that trains five different modality adapters of a shared VLM backbone and learns a neural routing policy that selects the best adapter based on the question during inference. We encode each question with a frozen sentence transformer and pass the embedding through a small Multi-layer Perceptron (MLP) trained on oracle adapter-accuracy labels. We evaluate our methodology over the Open3D-VQA benchmark and our evaluations show that no single modality is universally optimal -- point-cloud answers are best in 51.5% of cases. MASER routes with 51.3% oracle agreement, outperforming a Random-Forest ablation (43.5%), with only a single adapter call per question.

</details>


### [277] [Retrieve What's Missing: Coverage-Maximizing Retrieval for Consistent Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02479)
*Minseok Joo,Dogyun Park,Taehoon Lee,Kyujin Lee,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COVRAG提出基于深度的记忆检索框架，通过目标视图覆盖图作为轻量级3D记忆证据，并采用最大化残余覆盖增益的帧选择策略，在保持低延迟的同时提升长程视频生成的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强视频生成方法中，基于相机位姿或视场重叠的证据过于粗糙，无法推理像素级可见性；而显式3D重建虽提供细粒度证据但维护成本高，难以支持长序列生成。需设计兼顾细粒度与高效性的3D记忆证据及帧选择策略。

Method: 1) 利用预训练3D先验构建目标视图覆盖图作为轻量级3D记忆证据；2) 采用最大化残余覆盖增益策略迭代选取能解释当前上下文及已选记忆未覆盖区域的帧；3) 引入滑动窗口深度缓存实现高效几何估计以支持长视频生成。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和DL3DV10K上，COVRAG在提升长程几何一致性的同时保持了较低的延迟，优于各基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于深度的覆盖图记忆证据结合残余覆盖增益最大化帧选择策略，能够在不增加计算开销的前提下有效提升长程自回归视频生成的几何一致性。

Abstract: Maintaining long-term geometric consistency remains challenging for long-horizon autoregressive video generation. Memory-augmented generative models address this by retrieving historical frames, but their effectiveness depends on two key design choices: what 3D-geometric evidence should represent past observations, and how memory frames should be selected from this evidence. Existing methods often rely on camera poses or field-of-view overlap, which are lightweight but too coarse to reason about pixel-wise visibility, or use explicit 3D reconstruction, which provides fine-grained evidence but is costly to maintain over long rollouts. We propose Coverage-Maximizing Retrieval-Augmented Generation (COVRAG), a depth-based memory retrieval framework that uses pretrained 3D priors to construct a target-view coverage map as lightweight 3D memory evidence. For frame selection, COVRAG maximizes residual coverage gain, iteratively retrieving frames that explain target-view regions not covered by the current context or previously selected memories. To improve scalability in long-video generation, we introduce sliding-window depth caching for efficient geometry estimation. Experiments on RealEstate10K and DL3DV10K show that COVRAG improves long-horizon geometric consistency while maintaining low latency compared to baselines.

</details>


### [278] [Places in the Wild: A Large, High-Resolution RAW Photograph Dataset for Ecologically Valid Vision Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02481)
*Michelle R. Greene*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Places in the Wild是一个包含67,574张高分辨率照片的数据集，涵盖810个物理位置、260个场景类别，通过360度全景拍摄获取，支持认知神经科学和计算机视觉中的场景理解研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模图像数据集多为低分辨率、网络来源的JPEG图像，缺乏真实拍摄条件和空间上下文信息，难以支持对视点依赖的识别、自然场景统计特征分析等研究。

Method: 在810个物理位置（涵盖260个基本场景类别）使用45兆像素Canon EOS R5相机安装在全景三脚架上，每个位置拍摄72张水平5度间隔图像及12张不同仰角图像，实现密集的360度视点采样；同时记录14位RAW（CR3）文件和压缩JPEG文件，并附带完整EXIF元数据和图像质量指标。

Result: 构建了一个包含67,574张高分辨率照片、覆盖室内/城市/自然环境的大规模真实场景数据集，保留了传感器级别的亮度、对比度、颜色等图像统计信息。

Conclusion: 该数据集支持视点依赖识别、场景理解系统训练与评估、自然场景统计特征分析以及需要近全视野显示条件的实验等多种研究方向。

Abstract: Large image datasets have accelerated progress in cognitive neuroscience and computer vision. However, most datasets are low-resolution, internet-sourced JPEGs with unknown capture conditions and limited spatial context. Places in the Wild is a dataset of 67,574 high-resolution photographs collected in situ across 810 physical locations spanning 260 basic-level scene categories, including indoor, urban, and natural environments. At each location, a 45-megapixel Canon EOS R5 mounted on a panoramic tripod captured 72 images at 5-degree horizontal intervals plus 12 images at varying elevations, yielding dense 360-degree viewpoint sampling. All images were recorded simultaneously as 14-bit RAW (CR3) files and compressed JPEGs, preserving sensor-level detail for analyses of luminance, contrast, color, and other image statistics. The dataset is accompanied by complete EXIF metadata and a suite of image-quality metrics. Places in the Wild supports research on viewpoint-dependent recognition in humans and models, training and evaluation of scene-understanding systems under realistic conditions, characterization of natural scene statistics, and experiments requiring near-full-field visual displays.

</details>


### [279] [X-Stream: Exploring MLLMs as Multiplexers for Multi-Stream Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02482)
*Peiwen Sun,Xudong Lu,Huadai Liu,Yang Bo,Dongming Wu,Huankang Guan,Minghong Cai,Jinpeng Chen,Xintong Guo,Shuhan Li,Rui Liu,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Stream是首个专用于多流流式视频理解的基准测试，包含4,220个QA对和932个视频，覆盖多窗口、多视角、多设备场景，揭示了当前MLLMs在多流并发理解中仅达约50%准确率的严重局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准均局限于单流范式，无法评估在线跨流推理能力，而实际应用（如体育直播、自动驾驶、多屏协作）天然需要持续的多流交互。

Method: 构建了包含4,220个QA对的X-Stream基准，覆盖11个子任务和三种场景；采用双验证流水线避免对单流过度依赖；提出将MLLMs视为朴素多路复用器，从信号复用理论视角系统评估其性能。

Result: 最先进的MLLMs在并发多流理解中仅达到约50%的得分，且主动推理能力较差。

Conclusion: X-Stream揭示了当前复用方案的权衡，为下一代多流智能体提供了评估协议和实验指导。

Abstract: While video streaming understanding has made significant strides, real-world applications, such as live sports broadcasting, autonomous driving, and multi-screen collaboration, inherently demand continuous, multi-stream interactions. However, existing benchmarks are confined to single-stream paradigms, leaving a critical gap in evaluating online, cross-stream reasoning. To bridge this, we introduce X-Stream, the first benchmark dedicated to multi-stream streaming understanding. Comprising 4,220 rigorously curated QA pairs across 932 videos, X-Stream evaluates 11 subtasks across multi-window, multi-view, and multi-device scenarios. Crucially, our dataset is constructed using a novel dual-verification pipeline that prevents over-reliance on a single stream. Furthermore, we pioneer the conceptualization of multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) as naive multiplexers, systematically evaluating their performance through the lens of Signal Multiplexing Theory. Our extensive online inference experiments reveal a stark reality: state-of-the-art MLLMs struggle significantly with concurrent streams, achieving only about 50% score and exhibiting poor proactive ability. Ultimately, X-Stream exposes the trade-off of current multiplexing schemes, providing both a practical evaluation protocol and empirical guidance for next-generation multi-stream agents.

</details>


### [280] [MORPHOS: Autoregressive 4D Generation with Temporal Structured Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02491)
*Minkyung Kwon,Jinhyeok Choi,Youngjin Shin,Jaeyeong Kim,JongMin Lee,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MORPHOS是一个新颖的自回归框架，通过引入时间结构化潜变量（T-SLAT）统一4D表示，能够从视频中生成跨多种3D表示（网格、3D高斯、辐射场）的动态3D资产，在保持时间一致性的同时处理拓扑变化，并在多项基准测试中取得最优外观表现和具有竞争力的几何效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常局限于单一表示形式、难以建模拓扑变化或在长视频中无法维持时间一致性，亟需一种能够统一多种表示、处理动态拓扑并保持长期时间连贯性的动态3D生成方法。

Method: 提出时间结构化潜变量（T-SLAT）作为统一4D表示，联合编码几何与外观的时间维度信息；基于T-SLAT，采用因果注意力机制自回归生成动态3D资产，使每一帧条件于先前历史；提出时序-结构增强方法以缓解自回归生成中的误差累积问题。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，MORPHOS在外观指标上达到最优性能，在几何指标上取得具有竞争力的结果，展现出跨多种表示的优越泛化能力和长时序生成的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MORPHOS通过统一的4D潜变量表示和自回归框架，有效解决了动态3D生成中表示受限、拓扑变化和时序一致性的核心挑战，为多样化3D表示的动态资产生成提供了通用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: We present MORPHOS, a novel autoregressive framework that generates dynamic 3D assets from videos across diverse representations, including meshes, 3D Gaussians, and radiance fields. Existing methods are typically limited to a single representation, struggle to model topological changes, or fail to maintain temporal consistency over long videos. To address these limitations, we introduce the Temporal Structured Latents (T-SLAT), a unified 4D representation that jointly encodes geometry and appearance along the temporal dimension. Leveraging T-SLAT, MORPHOS autoregressively generates dynamic 3D assets via causal attention, conditioning each frame on its preceding history to ensure temporal consistency while handling evolving topologies. We also propose a temporal-structural augmentation to mitigate error accumulation in autoregressive generation. MORPHOS achieves state-of-the-art performance in appearance and competitive results in geometry across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating superior generalization across various representations and robustness in long-horizon generation.

</details>


### [281] [GloResNet: A lightweight 3D CNN with global topological features for preterm brain injury prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02498)
*Boyu Yuan,Jiamiao Lu,Weichuan Zhang,Benqing Wu,Tuo Wang,Changshan Wang,Changming Sun,Liang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GloResNet，一种基于ResNet-10的轻量级3D CNN框架，用于从T2加权MRI预测早产儿脑损伤。该方法在dHCP数据集上通过全局流形映射、Mixup增强、类别加权和测试时增强等技术，在5折交叉验证中达到75.18%的平均准确率（峰值81.82%），特异性0.81，灵敏度0.76。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产儿脑损伤（BI）的早期预测对于临床干预至关重要，但现有方法面临数据稀缺、计算成本高以及缺乏非侵入性筛查工具等挑战。本文旨在通过轻量级深度学习框架解决这些问题，提供一种高效、非侵入性的BI预测手段。

Method: 基于ResNet-10构建轻量级3D CNN（GloResNet），在MedicalNet上预训练以缓解数据稀缺；采用全局流形映射策略将3D体积重采样至128x128x128并进行逐样本z-score强度归一化，保留全局拓扑结构；训练中整合Mixup数据增强、类别加权损失和测试时增强以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在dHCP数据集上进行5折交叉验证，GloResNet平均准确率75.18%，峰值81.82%，特异性0.81，灵敏度0.76。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的轻量级CNN能够有效预测新生儿脑损伤，为临床提供非侵入性筛查工具。代码已开源在GitHub。

Abstract: This study introduces an automated deep learning framework for predicting brain injury (BI) in preterm infants from T2-weighted MRI (dHCP dataset). We propose GloResNet, a lightweight 3D CNN based on ResNet-10, pretrained on MedicalNet to address data scarcity. A global manifold mapping strategy first resamples each 3D volume to 128x128x128 and then applies subject-wise z-score intensity normalization, thereby preserving global topology while standardizing appearance. Training integrates mixup, class weighting, and test-time augmentation for robustness. In 5-fold cross-validation, GloResNet achieved 75.18% average accuracy (peak 81.82%), with specificity 0.81 and sensitivity 0.76. Results demonstrate that a topology-aware lightweight CNN has the capability to effectively predict neonatal BI, offering a non-invasive screening tool. The source code of this paper can be obtained from the GitHub repository: https://github.com/ICL-SUST/GloResNet-Preterm-Brain

</details>


### [282] [Question-Aware Evidence Ledgers for Video Relational Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02506)
*Yilin Ou,Mengshi Qi,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GPT-5.5的测试时推理管线，通过问题感知的证据账本和保守门控机制，在VRR-QA挑战中实现92.95%的整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRR-QA挑战中的视频视觉关系推理问题往往依赖于隐式空间关系、事件边界、目标身份和对话上下文，而非单一显著帧，现有方法难以有效处理这些复杂推理需求。

Method: 构建一个围绕GPT-5.5视频QA求解器的测试时推理管线，包含一组问题感知证据账本（ledgers），针对计数、空间、端点、视角和对话推理显式化所需目标、计数单位、参考帧及时空范围；使用开放词汇检测、深度线索、成对裁剪、ASR和场景图账本作为外部证据源；通过保守门控机制仅在独立证据唯一支持不同选项时才更改当前答案。

Result: 在挑战测试集上达到92.95%的整体准确率和93.79%的宏平均准确率。

Conclusion: 所提出的证据门控管线能有效融合多源证据进行视频关系推理，显著优于单一视频表示的直接求解方式。

Abstract: The VRR-QA challenge evaluates visual relational reasoning in videos, where answers often depend on implicit spatial relations, event boundaries, target identity, and dialogue context rather than a single salient frame. We present a test-time reasoning pipeline built around a strong GPT-5.5 video QA solver and a set of question-aware evidence ledgers. The initial solver answers each question from a uniform video representation, while routed ledgers are prompted to make the required targets, count units, reference frames, and temporal or spatial scope explicit for counting, spatial, endpoint, viewpoint, and dialogue reasoning. External tools such as open-vocabulary detection, depth cues, pair crops, ASR, and scene-graph ledgers are used only as evidence sources. A conservative gate keeps the current answer unless independent evidence uniquely supports a different option. The final evidence-gated pipeline achieves 92.95% overall accuracy and 93.79% macro accuracy on the challenge test split.

</details>


### [283] [Not All Points Are Equal: Uncertainty-Aware 4D LiDAR Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02510)
*Xiang Xu,Alan Liang,Youquan Liu,Xian Sun,Linfeng Li,Lingdong Kong,Ziwei Liu,Qingshan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: U4D提出了一种利用空间不确定性引导的"由难到易"调度策略，通过熵驱动的扩散模型分阶段生成LiDAR 4D场景，在nuScenes和SemanticKITTI上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成框架对所有空间区域采用统一建模能力，忽略了单帧扫描中不同区域（远距离表面、遮挡边界、小物体）的感知难度差异，导致高不确定区域生成质量差。

Method: 1) 通过预训练分割器的香农熵导出逐点不确定性图；2) 无条件扩散阶段先合成高熵区域（精确几何）；3) 条件补全阶段利用已生成结构作为先验填充剩余区域；4) MoST块动态平衡空间细节与时间连续性以保持跨帧一致性。

Result: 在nuScenes和SemanticKITTI数据集上，场景保真度、时间一致性和下游任务性能均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式利用空间不确定性引导生成过程是提升LiDAR 4D场景质量的有效范式，分阶段"由难到易"策略优于统一建模。

Abstract: Constructing faithful 4D worlds from LiDAR-acquired sequences is crucial for embodied AI, yet current generative frameworks apply uniform modeling capacity across all spatial regions. This ignores that perceptual difficulty varies dramatically within a single scan: distant surfaces, occluded boundaries, and small-scale objects carry far higher uncertainty than well-observed structures. We present U4D, a new framework that explicitly leverages spatial uncertainty to guide LiDAR scene generation in a "hard-to-easy" schedule. U4D derives per-point uncertainty maps via Shannon Entropy from a pretrained segmentor, then applies an unconditional diffusion stage to synthesize high-entropy areas with precise geometry, followed by a conditional completion stage that fills in the remaining regions using these structures as priors. A MoST (Mixture of Spatio-Temporal) block further maintains cross-frame coherence by dynamically balancing spatial detail and temporal continuity. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and SemanticKITTI demonstrate state-of-the-art scene fidelity, temporal consistency, and downstream performance.

</details>


### [284] [ToolFG: Towards Well-Grounded Fine-Grained Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02518)
*Yu Xue,Haoxuan Qu,Zhuoling Li,Yihang Lou,Yan Bai,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ToolFG是首个集成外部工具的MLLM框架，通过MCTS引导的工具使用知识蒸馏和模型-工具协同进化机制，实现了细粒度图像分类中更可靠、有依据的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度图像分类（FGIC）具有广泛应用价值，但现有方法在区分高度相似类别时缺乏可靠且可验证的视觉线索收集能力，需要探索新的范式来解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出ToolFG框架，使MLLM在推理过程中自主灵活地使用外部工具；2) 设计MCTS引导的工具使用知识蒸馏机制，从高级专有MLLM中挖掘相关知识；3) 提出模型-工具协同进化机制，联合优化工具集和模型策略。

Result: 大量实验证明了ToolFG框架的有效性，在细粒度图像分类任务上取得了优异性能。

Conclusion: ToolFG通过工具集成和知识蒸馏实现了更可靠、有依据的细粒度图像分类，模型与工具的协同进化进一步提升了系统性能，为FGIC提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Fine-grained image classification (FGIC) has broad applications and has attracted significant research attention. In this paper, we explore a novel paradigm for solving FGIC by proposing \textbf{ToolFG}, the first tool-integrated MLLM-based framework tailored to FGIC. ToolFG enables MLLMs to autonomously and flexibly use external tools during the reasoning process, actively interact with images, and collect verifiable visual cues for distinguishing highly similar categories in a more \textit{reliable} and \textit{well-grounded} manner. To equip the model with such tool-use ability, we design a novel \textbf{MCTS-guided tool-use knowledge distillation mechanism}, which effectively mines tool-use- and FGIC-relevant knowledge from advanced proprietary MLLMs for model training. Furthermore, we propose a \textbf{model-tool co-evolution mechanism} that jointly refines the toolset and the model's tool-use policy, driving them toward a mutually adapted and FGIC-specialized state. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [285] [Moment-Video: Diagnosing Temporal Fidelity of Video MLLMs on Momentary Visual Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02522)
*Xiaolin Liu,Yilun Zhu,Xiangyu Zhao,Xuehui Wang,Yan Li,Xin Li,Haoyu Cao,Xing Sun,Shaofeng Zhang,Xu Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Moment-Video基准，用于评估视频多模态大模型在瞬时视觉事件理解上的时间保真度。测试发现最佳模型Seed-2.0-Pro仅达39.6%准确率，多数开源模型低于25%，表明当前视频MLLM在捕捉短暂但关键视觉证据方面存在严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频MLLM在通用和长视频理解上进展迅速，但其对由短暂视觉事件（如持续数帧的局部动作或状态转变）决定的答案关键信息的保真能力尚未被充分探索。稀疏帧采样、视觉令牌压缩和粗粒度时间聚合会导致这类证据被跳过或稀释，而语言侧推理无法可靠恢复。

Method: 构建Moment-Video基准，包含1,000个人工验证的视频问答对，覆盖7个领域、25个细分子类别、4种任务类型（时间发生、时间计数、动作描述、时间推理）。每道题均基于局部化、视觉可观察且对采样敏感的瞬时事件，要求模型注意、计数、描述或推理瞬态证据。

Result: 评估33个专有和开源MLLM，最佳模型Seed-2.0-Pro仅39.6%准确率，多数开源模型低于25%。密集帧采样有一定改善但未消除瓶颈，更长视频带来更强的时间定位挑战。

Conclusion: 当前视频MLLM缺乏时间保真的表示能力，无法有效捕捉、保留和利用简短但决定性的视觉证据，瞬时视觉事件理解存在显著差距。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress on general and long-form video understanding, yet their ability to preserve brief answer-critical visual evidence remains underexplored. Many practical questions are determined by momentary visual events: localized actions or state transitions that may last only a few frames. Such evidence can be skipped by sparse frame sampling, suppressed by visual-token compression, or diluted by coarse temporal aggregation, causing failures that language-side reasoning cannot reliably recover. We introduce Moment-Video, a benchmark for diagnosing the temporal fidelity of video MLLMs through momentary visual event understanding. Each question is grounded in a localized, visually observable, and sampling-sensitive event, requiring models to notice, count, describe, or reason about transient evidence rather than rely on persistent objects, global scene context, or language priors. Moment-Video contains 1,000 human-verified video-QA pairs across 7 domains and 25 fine-grained subcategories, covering four task types: Temporal Occurrence, Temporal Counting, Action Description, and Temporal Reasoning. We evaluate 33 proprietary and open-source MLLMs on Moment-Video. The best-performing model, Seed-2.0-Pro, achieves only 39.6% overall accuracy, while most open-source models remain below 25%, revealing a substantial gap in momentary visual event understanding. Diagnostic analyses show that denser frame sampling improves some models but does not eliminate the bottleneck, and longer videos introduce stronger temporal-localization challenges. These findings suggest that current video MLLMs still lack temporally faithful representations for capturing, preserving, and using brief but decisive visual evidence.

</details>


### [286] [Why Not Hyperparameter-Friendly Optimisation? A Monotonic Adaptive Norm Rescaling Approach For Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02526)
*Shuo Zhang,Chenqi Li,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需超参数的自适应单调归一化方法（SAMN），通过Pool Adjacent Violators算法直接对每类权重的范数施加单调性约束，有效提升长尾识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段解耦范式中的自适应范数重缩放方法依赖参数正则化引入超参数，而长尾识别对这些超参数高度敏感，性能波动大。

Method: 首先从类条件分布视角为范数重缩放方法提供理论支持；然后提出SAMN方法，利用Pool Adjacent Violators算法直接对每类权重范数施加单调性约束，避免参数正则化，无需超参数调优。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，SAMN显著提升了长尾识别性能，常达到最先进水平，且能与其他方法无缝集成。

Conclusion: SAMN是一种超参数友好、即插即用的通用策略，有效解决了长尾识别中范数重缩放方法的超参数敏感问题。

Abstract: Long-tailed recognition poses a significant challenge for deep learning. The two-stage decoupling paradigm, which separates representation learning from classifier retraining, offers a promising solution. During the classifier retraining stage, adaptive norm rescaling is a popular technique. It adjusts the per-class weight norms via parameter regularization, which inevitably introduces hyperparameters. However, many studies report that long-tailed recognition is sensitive to these hyperparameters, as their setup significantly impacts performance. In this paper, we first provide a class-conditional distribution perspective to support norm rescaling methods.
